\displaystyle|\omega^{(T_{l}+\lceil t_{l}/2\rceil+T)}|

\displaystyle\leq c\sigma\cdot c_{2}\sqrt{2}\sigma\zeta^{\alpha_{l+1}}\sqrt{\zeta}(1+\bar{\gamma}_{l}+\cdots\bar{\gamma}_{T-1})+\bar{\gamma}_{l}^{T}|\omega^{(T_{l}+\lceil t_{l}/2\rceil)}|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2c\sigma\cdot c_{2}\sqrt{2}\sigma}{1-p}\zeta^{\alpha_{l+1}+\frac{1}{2}-2\alpha_{l+1}}+e^{-T\frac{(1-p)\zeta^{2\alpha_{l+1}}}{2}}c_{2}\sqrt{2}\sigma\zeta^{\alpha_{l+1}}

\displaystyle\leq c_{2}^{2}\zeta^{\frac{1}{2}-2\alpha_{l+1}}\cdot\sqrt{2}\sigma\zeta^{\alpha_{l+1}}.

The third inequality follows that
T\geq\frac{1-4\alpha_{l+1}}{(1-p)\zeta^{2\alpha_{l+1}}}\log(1/\zeta)
. Letting

n\geq c_{2}^{4/\epsilon}\sigma^{2}\left(\sum_{m}d_{m}+\log(2l_{\alpha}/\delta)\right),

(A32)

we have
c_{2}^{2}\zeta^{\frac{1}{2}-2\alpha_{l+1}}\leq 1
and (
A30
) can be obtained.

Step 4:
In this step, we want to show (
29
). For the iteration number, straightforward computations yield that

\displaystyle T_{l_{\alpha}-1}=\sum_{l=0}^{l_{\alpha}-1}t_{l_{\alpha}-1}

(A33)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{4}{1-p}\log\left(\frac{|\omega^{(0)}|}{\sqrt{2\zeta}\sigma}\right)+\sum_{l=1}^{l_{\alpha}-1}\frac{1-4\alpha_{l+1}}{(1-p)\zeta^{2\alpha_{l+1}}}\log(1/\zeta)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{4}{1-p}\log\left(\frac{|\omega^{(0)}|}{\sqrt{2\zeta}\sigma}\right)+(l_{\alpha}-1)\frac{4}{(1-p)\zeta^{2\alpha_{l_{\alpha}}}}\log(1/\zeta)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{4}{1-p}\left[\log\left(\frac{|\omega^{(0)}|}{\sqrt{2\zeta}\sigma}\right)+\frac{\log(1/(4\alpha))}{\log(3)}\zeta^{1/2-2\alpha}\log(1/\zeta)\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{3}\left[\log\left(\frac{|\omega^{(0)}|n}{\sigma^{2}\sum_{m}d_{m}}\right)+\left(\frac{n}{\sum_{m}d_{m}}\right)^{1/2-2\alpha}\log(1/\alpha)\log\left(\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}\sum_{m}d_{m}}\right)\sigma^{4\alpha-1}\right]

for some positive constant
c_{3}
. The second inequality uses the bound of
t_{l}
in (
A28
) and (
A31
). This third inequality follows that fact that
\alpha_{l_{\alpha}}\geq\frac{1}{4}-\alpha
. This implies that, when
t\succsim\log\left(\frac{n\omega^{(0)}}{\sigma^{2}\sum_{m}d_{m}}\right)+\left(\frac{n}{\sum_{m}d_{m}}\right)^{1/2-2\alpha}\log(1/\alpha)\log\left(\frac{n}{\sum_{m}d_{m}}\right)
, with probability
1-\delta
, it holds that

|\omega^{(t)}|\leq|\omega^{(0)}|\cdot\prod_{j=0}^{t-1}\gamma_{p}(\zeta^{(j)})+C_{4}\sigma\left(\sigma^{2}\frac{\sum_{m}d_{m}+\log\left(\left(\frac{n}{\sum_{m}d_{m}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}-\alpha}\log\left(\frac{1}{\alpha}\right)\right)}{n}\right)^{\frac{1}{4}-\alpha}

for one positive constant
C_{4}
. Since
\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}^{(t)}\|_{F}=|\omega^{(t)}|
and
\bm{\mathcal{U}}^{\ast}={\bf 0}
, (
29
) is thus shown.

C5
Proof of Theorem
4

The Step 1 in the proof of Theorem
3
can be also used in this case. It can guarantee the non-expansive property of our algorithm. By Lemma
9b
and Lemma
10b
, we can get that

\displaystyle|\omega^{(t+1)}|

\displaystyle\leq|\omega^{(t+1)}-\mathbb{E}[\omega^{(t+1)}]|+|\mathbb{E}[\omega^{(t+1)}]|

(A34)

\displaystyle\leq c\sigma(\sigma|\omega^{(0)}|+\rho)\sqrt{\frac{\sum_{m}d_{m}+\log(1/\delta)}{n}}+\gamma|\omega^{(t)}|

\displaystyle\ldots

\displaystyle\leq c\sigma(\sigma|\omega^{(0)}|+\rho)\sqrt{\frac{\sum_{m}d_{m}+\log(1/\delta)}{n}}(1+\gamma+\cdots+\gamma^{t})+\gamma^{t+1}|\omega^{(0)}|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{c\sigma(\sigma|\omega^{(0)}|+\rho)}{1-\gamma}\sqrt{\frac{\sum_{m}d_{m}+\log(1/\delta)}{n}}+\gamma^{t+1}|\omega^{(0)}|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2c\sigma(\sigma|\omega^{(0)}|+\rho)}{\rho^{2}}\sqrt{\frac{\sum_{m}d_{m}+\log(1/\delta)}{n}}+\gamma^{t+1}|\omega^{(0)}|.

with probability at least
1-\delta
.
The second inequality is true, because
|\omega^{(t)}|\leq|\omega^{(0)}|
is guaranteed by the Step 1 in the proof of Theorem
3
. The last inequality uses the definition of
\gamma
. Since
\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}^{(t)}\|_{F}=|\omega^{(t)}|
and
\bm{\mathcal{U}}^{\ast}={\bf 0}
, (
30
) is thus shown.

C6
Proof of Theorem
5

Our theoretical analysis starts with the following inequality:

\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}^{(t)}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}^{\ast}\|_{\text{F}}\leq\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}^{(t)}\|_{\text{F}}+\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}^{\ast}\|_{\text{F}}=\omega^{(t)}+\omega^{\ast}.

(A35)

As
\omega^{\ast}=\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}^{\ast}\|_{\text{F}}\leq C_{0}\epsilon
, the main objective is to bound
\omega^{(t)}
. To achieve this, we utilize the following result:

|\omega^{(t)}|\leq|\omega^{(t)}-\mathbb{E}[\omega^{(t)}]|+|\mathbb{E}[\omega^{(t)}]|.

(A36)

The term
|\omega^{(t)}-\mathbb{E}[\omega^{(t)}]|
can be bounded using Lemma
10b
. To bound
|\mathbb{E}[\omega^{(t)}]|
, we introduce Lemma
11b
. The proof of Lemma
11b
introduces new challenges due to the low-rank structure of tensor means. In
Kwon et al. 2021
, one key step in the contraction analysis involves considering a linear combination of
\mathbb{E}[{\bm{\theta}}^{(t)}]
, denoted as
\mathbb{E}[{\bm{\theta}}^{(t)}]\bm{v}
, using a specific
\bm{v}
. Here,
{\bm{\theta}}
refers to the mean parameter in the symmetric two-mixture model.
The transformation
\mathbb{E}[{\bm{\theta}}^{(t)}]\bm{v}
is then expressed as an expectation of the tanh function, and the properties of the tanh function are utilized in the analysis. However, in our setting, an additional term
\mathrm{vec}({\bm{\beta}}_{1}^{(t+1)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}^{(t+1)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3}^{(t+1)})
arises from the low-rank structure, making it impossible to express the mean parameters using a tanh function. Consequently, the technique used in
Kwon et al. 2021
is not directly applicable. To overcome this challenge, we consider an orthogonal transformation in each mode and utilize the properties of multivariate normal distributions to bound the additional term. Additionally, since
{\bm{\beta}}_{m}^{(t+1)}
are newly updated parameters that depend on data, Lemma
11b
needs to be established uniformly over
{\bm{\beta}}_{m}^{(t+1)}
. Once Lemmas
10b
and
11b
are established, we can obtain the desired result using annulus-based localization of epochs. Next, we outline the proof strategies.

The proof of (
29
) utilizes the strategy of annulus-based localization of epochs in
Dwivedi et al. 2020
. In this strategy, we define a sequence of outer radius of each annulus.

\mathcal{R}=\left\{\sqrt{2}\sigma\zeta^{\alpha_{0}},\ldots,\sqrt{2}\sigma\zeta^{\alpha_{l_{\alpha}-1}}\right\},

(A37)

where
\zeta=\sigma^{2}\frac{\sum_{m}d_{m}+\log((2l_{\alpha}+1)/\delta)}{n}
,
\alpha_{l}
is a decreasing positive sequence and
l_{\alpha}
is the integer satisfying that
\alpha_{l_{\alpha}-1}\geq\frac{1}{4}-\alpha
. The specific forms of
\zeta_{l}
and
l_{\alpha}
will be decided later in this proof.
The entire sequence of sample ECM iterations is divided into a sequence of epochs. In each epoch, the ECM iterates are localized in to an annulus. That is, the
l
th epoch is defined to be the set of ECM iterations such that the iterate falls in the
l
th annulus.
Given these annulus, the proof can be summarized into four steps. In Step 1, we show that given
|\omega^{(t)}|\leq\sqrt{2}\sigma\zeta^{\alpha_{l}}
, if the sample size is large enough,
|\zeta^{(t_{0})}|\leq\sqrt{2}\sigma\zeta^{\alpha_{l}}
for any
t_{0}>t
. This can guarantee the updated parameter is still satisfying conditions for the initial parameter in each annulus. In Step 2, we use the rate of statistical error and contraction rate to decide the outer bound of each annulus. In Step 3, we show that the updated parameter will go into the next small annular until
|\omega^{(t)}|=O\left(\frac{d}{n}\right)^{1/4-\alpha}
for any
\alpha\in(0,\frac{1}{4})
. In Step 4, the final result can be obtained by putting previous steps together. In the following proof, we will give details for these four steps.

Step 1:
In this step, we show the non-expansive property of our algorithm. This means given
|\omega^{(t)}|\leq\sqrt{2}\sigma\zeta^{\alpha_{l}}
, if the sample size is large enough,
|\zeta^{(t_{0})}|\leq\sqrt{2}\sigma\zeta^{\alpha_{l}}
for any
t_{0}>t
. Without loss of generality, we assume that
|\omega^{(t)}|\geq\sqrt{2}\sigma\zeta^{\alpha_{l+1}}
. Otherwise, we can consider next small annulus until
|\omega^{(t)}|\geq\sqrt{2}\sigma\zeta^{\alpha_{l^{\prime}}}
is satisfied for some
l^{\prime}
.

By Lemma
11b
and Lemma
10b
, we have

\displaystyle|\omega^{(t+1)}|

\displaystyle\leq|\omega^{(t+1)}-\mathbb{E}[\omega^{(t+1)}]|+|\mathbb{E}[\omega^{(t+1)}]|

(A38)

\displaystyle\leq c\sigma r\sqrt{\zeta}+\left(1-\frac{2(\omega^{(t)})^{2}}{\sigma^{2}}\right)|\omega^{(t)}|

\displaystyle\leq c\sigma\cdot\sqrt{2}\sigma\zeta^{\alpha_{l}}\sqrt{\zeta}+\left(1-4\zeta^{2\alpha_{l+1}}\right)\sqrt{2}\sigma\zeta^{\alpha_{l}}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-4\zeta^{2\alpha_{l+1}}+c\sigma\sqrt{\zeta}\right)\sqrt{2}\sigma\zeta^{\alpha_{l}},

with probability at least
1-\delta
. The second inequality is the direct result of Lemma
11b
and Lemma
10b
. The third one follows that
|\omega^{(t)}|\in(\sqrt{2}\sigma\zeta^{\alpha_{l+1}},\sqrt{2}\sigma\zeta^{\alpha_{l}}]
. Note that
\alpha_{l+1}\geq\frac{1}{4}-\alpha
for all
l\leq l_{\alpha}-1
and
\zeta\leq 1
. As a result, for
n\geq\sigma^{2}(4/(c\sigma))^{1/(2\epsilon)}(\sum_{m}d_{m}+\log((2l_{\alpha}+1)/\delta))
,

-4\zeta^{2\alpha_{l+1}}+c\sigma\sqrt{\zeta}\leq-c\sigma\sqrt{\zeta}\left\{\frac{4}{c\sigma}\zeta^{2\alpha_{l+1}-\frac{1}{2}}-1\right\}\leq-c\sigma\sqrt{\zeta}\left\{\frac{4}{c\sigma}\zeta^{-2\alpha}-1\right\}\leq 0

It is then straightforward to get that

1-4\zeta^{2\alpha_{l+1}}+c\sigma\sqrt{\zeta}\leq 1.

Repeating (
A38
) for multiple times, we can get that, for any
t_{0}\geq t
,

|\omega^{(t)}|\leq\sqrt{2}\sigma\zeta^{\alpha_{l}}.

Step 2:
In this step, we move to decide the outer bound of each annulus, which is to specify
\alpha_{l}
. Given
|\omega^{(t)}|\in[\sqrt{2}\sigma\zeta^{\alpha_{l+1}},\sqrt{2}\sigma\zeta^{\alpha_{l}}]
, we can use Lemma
11b
to get that

\displaystyle|\mathbb{E}[\omega^{(t+1)}]|\leq\left(1-4\zeta^{2\alpha_{l+1}}\right)|\omega^{(t)}|\leq e^{-4\zeta^{2\alpha_{l+1}}}|\omega^{(t)}|.

(A39)

This is true, because
e^{-4\zeta^{2\alpha_{l+1}}}=1-4\zeta^{2\alpha_{l+1}}+\frac{1}{2}\left(4\zeta^{2\alpha_{l+1}}\right)^{2}+o\left(16\zeta^{4\alpha_{l+1}}\right)
. On the other hand, using the upper bound
\omega^{(t)}
and Lemma
10b
, there exist positive constant
c_{1}
such that

|\omega^{(t+1)}-\mathbb{E}[\omega^{(t+1)}]|\leq c\sigma^{2}r\sqrt{\frac{\sum_{m}d_{m}+\log(1/\delta)}{n}}\leq c_{1}\left(\frac{\sum_{m}d_{m}}{n}\right)^{\alpha_{l}+\frac{1}{2}},

(A40)

with probability at least
1-\delta
. Letting
\tilde{\gamma}=e^{-4\zeta^{2\alpha_{l+1}}}
, after
t_{l}
steps, we have

\displaystyle|\omega^{(t+t_{l})}|

\displaystyle\leq|\omega^{(t)}-\mathbb{E}[\omega^{(t)}]|(1+\tilde{\gamma}+\cdots+\tilde{\gamma}^{t_{l}-1})+\tilde{\gamma}^{t_{l}}|\omega^{(t)}|

(A41)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{|\omega^{(t)}-\mathbb{E}[\omega^{(t)}]|}{1-\tilde{\gamma}}+\exp^{-\frac{(1-p)^{2}}{4}t_{l}\zeta^{2\alpha_{l+1}}}\cdot\sqrt{2}\sigma\zeta^{\alpha_{l}}.

The second term decays exponentially in
T
and it will be dominated by the first term when
t_{l}
is sufficiently large. This means

|\omega^{(t+t_{l})}|\preceq\frac{|\omega^{(t)}-\mathbb{E}[\omega^{(t)}]|}{1-\tilde{\gamma}}\approx\left(\frac{\sum_{m}d_{m}}{n}\right)^{-2\alpha_{l+1}}\left(\frac{\sum_{m}d_{m}}{n}\right)^{\alpha_{l}+\frac{1}{2}}.

This is true, because
\tilde{\gamma}=1-4\zeta^{2\alpha_{l+1}}+\frac{1}{2}\left(4\zeta^{2\alpha_{l+1}}\right)^{2}+o\left(16\zeta^{4\alpha_{l+1}}\right)
. Note that the epoch is said to be complete once
|\omega^{(t+t_{l})}|\leq\sqrt{2}\sigma\zeta^{\alpha_{l+1}}
. Ignoring constants, this condition is satisfied when

\left(\frac{\sum_{m}d_{m}}{n}\right)^{-2\alpha_{l+1}}\left(\frac{\sum_{m}d_{m}}{n}\right)^{\alpha_{l}+\frac{1}{2}}=\left(\frac{\sum_{m}d_{m}}{n}\right)^{\alpha_{l+1}}.

This implies that
\alpha_{l+1}=\frac{1}{3}\alpha_{l}+\frac{1}{6}
. Similar as
Balakrishnan et al. 2017
, we require that
|\omega^{(0)}|\leq\sqrt{2}\sigma
. This means
\alpha_{0}=0
and then the outer bound for the first annulus is
\sqrt{2}\sigma\zeta^{0}
. Combining with
\alpha_{l+1}=\frac{1}{3}\alpha_{l}+\frac{1}{6}
, we have

\alpha_{l+1}=\frac{1}{3}\alpha_{l}+\frac{1}{6}=\frac{1}{3}\left(\frac{1}{3}\alpha_{l-1}+\frac{1}{6}\right)+\frac{1}{6}=\cdots=\frac{1}{3^{l+1}}\alpha_{0}+\frac{1}{6}\frac{1-1/3^{l+1}}{1-1/3}=\frac{1}{4}(1-1/3^{l+1}).

This implies that for any
\alpha\in(0,\frac{1}{4})
, we have
\alpha_{l}\geq\frac{1}{4}-\alpha
for
l\geq\frac{\log(1/(4\alpha))}{\log(3)}
. Define
l_{\alpha}=\frac{\log(1/(4\alpha))}{\log(3)}+1
, we have
\alpha_{l_{\alpha}-1}\geq\frac{1}{4}-\alpha
.
This implies that
\alpha_{l+1}=\frac{1}{3}\alpha_{l}+\frac{1}{6}
.

Step 3:
In this step, we want to show that the updated parameter will go into the next small annular until
|\omega^{(t)}|=O\left(\frac{\sum_{m}d_{m}}{n}\right)^{1/4-\alpha}
for any
\alpha\in(0,\frac{1}{4})
. Specifically, given
|\omega^{(t)}|\in[\sqrt{2}\sigma\zeta^{\alpha_{l+1}},\sqrt{2}\sigma\zeta^{\alpha_{l}}]
, we need to show that, with enough steps, it will be smaller than
\sqrt{2}\sigma\zeta^{\alpha_{l+2}}
until
l\leq l_{\alpha}-2
. Before we introduce the details of the proof, we define
t_{l}
be the total iteration number in the
(l+1)
th epoch. Let
T_{l^{\prime}}=\sum_{l=1}^{l^{\prime}}t_{l}
be the total iteration number to reach at the
(l+1)
th epoch.

Given
|\omega^{(t)}|\in[\sqrt{2}\sigma\zeta^{\alpha_{l+1}},\sqrt{2}\sigma\zeta^{\alpha_{l}}]
, we have

1-\frac{2\zeta^{2}}{\sigma^{2}}\leq\underbrace{1-4\zeta^{2\alpha_{l+1}}}_{\bar{\gamma}_{l}},

for all
t\in\{T_{l},\ldots,T_{l+1}-1\}
. Based on this, this proof can be divided into two parts. In the first part, we show that

|\omega^{(T_{l}+\lceil t_{l+1}/2\rceil)}|\leq c_{2}\sqrt{2}\sigma\zeta^{\alpha_{l+1}},

(A42)

where
c_{2}=2c\sigma/(1-p)+1
is a positive constant. In the second part, we can get that

|\omega^{(T_{l+1})}|\leq\sqrt{2}\sigma\zeta^{\alpha_{l+1}}.

(A43)

For (
A42
), it can be obtained by

\displaystyle|\omega^{(T_{l}+T)}|

\displaystyle\leq|\omega^{(T_{l}+T)}-\mathbb{E}[\omega^{(T_{l}+T)}]|+|\mathbb{E}[\omega^{(T_{l}+T)}]|

(A44)

\displaystyle\leq c\sigma\cdot\sqrt{2}\sigma\zeta^{\alpha_{l}}\sqrt{\zeta}+\bar{\gamma}_{l}|\omega^{(T_{l}+T-1)}|

\displaystyle\leq c\sigma\cdot\sqrt{2}\sigma\zeta^{\alpha_{l}}\sqrt{\zeta}(1+\bar{\gamma}_{l}+\cdots\bar{\gamma}_{T-1})+\bar{\gamma}_{l}^{T}|\omega^{(T_{l})}|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{c\sigma\cdot\sqrt{2}\sigma}{4}\zeta^{\alpha_{l}+\frac{1}{2}-2\alpha_{l+1}}+e^{-4T\zeta^{2\alpha_{l+1}}}\sqrt{2}\sigma\zeta^{\alpha_{l}}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2}\sigma\zeta^{\alpha_{l}+\frac{1}{2}-2\alpha_{l+1}}\left(\frac{c\sigma}{4}+1\right)=c_{2}\sqrt{2}\sigma\zeta^{\alpha^{l+1}},

where
c_{2}=\frac{c\sigma}{4}+1
.
The third inequality follows the definition of
\bar{\gamma}_{l}
. The last inequality holds by
\alpha_{l}+\frac{1}{2}-2\alpha_{l+1}=\alpha_{l+1}
and
T\geq\frac{1-4\alpha_{l+1}}{4\zeta^{2\alpha_{l+1}}}\log(1/\zeta)
. Similar as (
A44
), staring at time
T_{l}+\lceil t_{l}/2\rceil
, we get that

\displaystyle|\omega^{(T_{l}+\lceil t_{l}/2\rceil+T)}|

\displaystyle\leq c\sigma\cdot c_{2}\sqrt{2}\sigma\zeta^{\alpha_{l+1}}\sqrt{\zeta}(1+\bar{\gamma}_{l}+\cdots\bar{\gamma}_{T-1})+\bar{\gamma}_{l}^{T}|\omega^{(T_{l}+\lceil t_{l}/2\rceil)}|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2c\sigma\cdot c_{2}\sqrt{2}\sigma}{1-p}\zeta^{\alpha_{l+1}+\frac{1}{2}-2\alpha_{l+1}}+e^{-T\frac{(1-p)\zeta^{2\alpha_{l+1}}}{2}}c_{2}\sqrt{2}\sigma\zeta^{\alpha_{l+1}}

\displaystyle\leq c_{2}^{2}\zeta^{\frac{1}{2}-2\alpha_{l+1}}\cdot\sqrt{2}\sigma\zeta^{\alpha_{l+1}}.

The third inequality follows that
T\geq\frac{1-4\alpha_{l+1}}{4\zeta^{2\alpha_{l+1}}}\log(1/\zeta)
. Letting

n\geq c_{2}^{4/\epsilon}\sigma^{2}\left(\sum_{m}d_{m}+\log(2l_{\alpha}/\delta)\right),

(A45)

we have
c_{2}^{2}\zeta^{\frac{1}{2}-2\alpha_{l+1}}\leq 1
and (
A43
) can be obtained.

Step 4:
Given
\omega^{\ast}\leq C_{0}\epsilon
, we get that

\displaystyle\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}^{\ast}\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\|\omega{\bm{\beta}}_{1}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3}-\omega^{\ast}{\bm{\beta}}_{1}^{\ast}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}^{\ast}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3}^{\ast}\|_{F}

(A46)

\displaystyle\leq\|\omega{\bm{\beta}}_{1}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3}-\omega^{\ast}{\bm{\beta}}_{1}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3}\|_{F}+\|\omega^{\ast}{\bm{\beta}}_{1}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3}-\omega^{\ast}{\bm{\beta}}_{1}^{\ast}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3}\|_{F}

\displaystyle+\|\omega^{\ast}{\bm{\beta}}_{1}^{\ast}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3}-\omega^{\ast}{\bm{\beta}}_{1}^{\ast}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}^{\ast}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3}\|_{F}+\|\omega^{\ast}{\bm{\beta}}_{1}^{\ast}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}^{\ast}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3}-\omega^{\ast}{\bm{\beta}}_{1}^{\ast}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}^{\ast}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3}^{\ast}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq|\omega-\omega^{\ast}|+6\omega^{\ast}\leq\omega+7\omega^{\ast}.

The second inequality is the direct result of
\|{\bm{\beta}}_{m}\|_{2}=\|{\bm{\beta}}_{m}^{\ast}\|_{2}=1
and
\|{\bm{\beta}}_{m}-{\bm{\beta}}_{m}^{\ast}\|_{2}\leq 2
. Based on the upper bound of
\omega^{(t)}
from Step 3, the desired result can be obtained. For the iteration number, it holds that

\displaystyle T_{l_{\alpha}-1}=\sum_{l=1}^{l_{\alpha}-1}t_{l_{\alpha}-1}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{l=1}^{l_{\alpha}-1}\frac{1-4\alpha_{l+1}}{4\zeta^{2\alpha_{l+1}}}\log(1/\zeta)

(A47)

\displaystyle\leq(l_{\alpha}-1)\frac{1}{\zeta^{2\alpha_{l_{\alpha}}}}\log(1/\zeta)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\log(1/(4\alpha))}{\log(3)}\zeta^{1/2-2\alpha}\log(1/\zeta)

\displaystyle\leq c_{3}\left(\frac{n}{\sum_{m}d_{m}}\right)^{1/2-2\alpha}\log(1/\alpha)\log\left(\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}\sum_{m}d_{m}}\right)\sigma^{4\alpha-1}

for some positive constant
c_{3}
. The first inequality uses the bound of
t_{l}
in Step 3. This second inequality follows that fact that
\alpha_{l_{\alpha}}\geq\frac{1}{4}-\alpha
. This implies that, when

t\succsim\left(\frac{n}{\sum_{m}d_{m}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}-2\alpha}\log(\frac{1}{\alpha})\log\left(\frac{n}{\sum_{m}d_{m}}\right),

with probability at least
1-\delta
, it holds that

\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}^{(t)}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}^{\ast}\|_{F}\leq\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}^{(0)}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}^{\ast}\|_{F}\cdot\prod_{j=0}^{t-1}\left(1-\frac{2{\omega^{(j)}}^{2}}{\sigma^{2}}\right)+C_{8}\sigma\left(\sigma^{2}\frac{\sum_{m}d_{m}+\log\left(\frac{\log\left(1/\alpha\right)}{\delta}\right)}{n}\right)^{\frac{1}{4}-\alpha}.

D
Proof of Key Technical Lemmas

D1
Proof of Lemma
1b

In this proof, we establish, under
R=1
, restricted strong concavity with respect to
{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}
,
\omega_{k}
and
{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}
, respectively.

First, we consider restricted strong concavity with respect to
{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}
. According to Taylor expansion, we can expand
Q_{n/T}({\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime\prime},\bar{{\bm{\Theta}}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})
around
{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime}
to obtain

\displaystyle Q_{\frac{n}{T}}({\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime\prime},\bar{{\bm{\Theta}}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})=

\displaystyle Q_{\frac{n}{T}}({\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime},\bar{{\bm{\Theta}}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})+\left\langle\nabla_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}Q_{\frac{n}{T}}({\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime},\bar{{\bm{\Theta}}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}}),{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime\prime}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime}\right\rangle

(A48)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}({\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime\prime}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime})^{\top}\nabla_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}^{2}Q_{\frac{n}{T}}(\bm{z},\bar{{\bm{\Theta}}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})({\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime\prime}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime}),

where
\bm{z}=t{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime}+(1-t){\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime\prime}
with
t\in[0,1]
. It follows from Lemma
S14
that

\displaystyle\nabla_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}^{2}Q_{\frac{n}{T}}(\bm{z},\bar{{\bm{\Theta}}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{T}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{\frac{n}{T}}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\bar{\omega}_{k}^{2}\left\{\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m^{\prime}})^{\top}\left(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\right)\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m^{\prime}})\right\}\bar{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m}.

Correspondingly, (
A48
) can be rewritten as

\displaystyle Q_{\frac{n}{T}}({\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime\prime},\bar{{\bm{\Theta}}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})-Q_{\frac{n}{T}}({\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime},\bar{{\bm{\Theta}}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})-\left\langle\nabla_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}Q_{\frac{n}{T}}({\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime},\bar{{\bm{\Theta}}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}}),{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime\prime}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime}\right\rangle

(A49)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\left(\frac{T}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{\frac{n}{T}}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\right)\bar{\omega}_{k}^{2}T_{k,m}({\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime\prime}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime})^{\top}\bar{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m}({\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime\prime}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime}),

where
T_{k,m}=\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m^{\prime}})^{\top}\left(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\right)\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m^{\prime}})
.
By Hoeffding’s inequality and noting that
\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\in[0,1]
, we can get that

\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\frac{T}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n/T}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})-\mathbb{E}(\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}))\right|\leq t\right)\geq 1-2e^{-2nt^{2}/T}.

(A50)

Let
p_{n}=1/\{\log(nd)\}^{2}
and
t=\sqrt{\log({2}/{p_{n}})/(2n/T)}
, it arrives at

\left|\frac{T}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n/T}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})-\mathbb{E}(\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}))\right|\leq\sqrt{\log({2}/{p_{n}})/(2n/T)},

with probability at least
1-p_{n}
. Recall that
\mathbb{E}(\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast}))=\pi_{k}^{\ast}
and
\pi^{\ast}_{k}
’s bounded below by a constant as assumed in Condition
1
. Under Condition
3
with a sufficiently small
\gamma>0
, there exists some constant
c_{0}^{\prime}>0
such that
\min_{k\in[K]}\mathbb{E}(\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}))\geq c_{0}^{\prime}
for
{\bm{\Theta}}\in\mathcal{B}_{\frac{1}{2}}({\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})

(
Hao et al. 2017
)
.
Next, as
\sqrt{\log({2}/{p_{n}})/(2n/T)}=o(1)
, there exists some constant
c_{0}>0
such that

\mathbb{E}(\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}))-\sqrt{\log({2}/{p_{n}})/(2n)}\geq c_{0},

when
n
is large. By the fact that
|a-b|\geq a-b
, we have

\frac{T}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n/T}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\geq\mathbb{E}(\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}))-\sqrt{\log({2}/{p_{n}})/(2n)}\geq c_{0}.

(A51)

Furthermore, by Condition
1
and
\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}\in\mathcal{S}_{1/2}({{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m}^{\ast})
, it holds that

\sigma_{\min}(\bar{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m})\geq\sigma_{\min}({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast})-\|{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}-\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}\|_{2}\geq\phi_{1}/2.

(A52)

Correspondingly, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m^{\prime}})^{\top}\left(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\right)\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m^{\prime}})

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\left\{\prod_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\sigma_{\min}(\bar{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m^{\prime}})\right\}\left\|\bar{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,1}\circ\cdots\circ\bar{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m-1}\circ\bar{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m+1}\circ\cdots\circ\bar{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,M}\right\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\geq(\phi_{1}/2)^{M-1}.

The first inequality is the direct result of Lemma
S7
and
\left\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\right\|_{\text{F}}^{2}=\left\|\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{U}})\right\|_{2}^{2}
. Specifically, if
\bm{A}
and
\bm{B}
are non-negative definite matrix, we can get
\sigma_{\min}\left(\bm{A}\otimes\bm{B}\right)\geq\sigma_{\min}\left(\bm{A}\right)\sigma_{\min}\left(\bm{B}\right)
from Lemma
S7
. The last inequality can be obtained by Lemma
S9
and (
A52
).
As
\bar{\bm{\Theta}}\in\mathbb{B}_{\frac{1}{2}}({\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})
, we have
\bar{\omega}_{k}\geq\omega_{k}^{\ast}-|\bar{\omega}_{k}-\omega_{k}^{\ast}|\geq\omega_{\min}/2
.
Setting
\gamma_{0}=\frac{c_{0}}{4}\omega_{\min}^{2}(\phi_{1}/2)^{M}
, the following holds with probability at least
1-1/\{\log(nd)\}^{2}
,

\displaystyle Q_{\frac{n}{T}}({\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime\prime},\bar{{\bm{\Theta}}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})-Q_{\frac{n}{T}}({\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime},\bar{{\bm{\Theta}}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})-\left\langle\nabla_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}Q_{\frac{n}{T}}({\bm{\beta}}^{\prime},\bar{{\bm{\Theta}}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}}),{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime\prime}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\frac{\gamma_{0}}{2}\left\|{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime\prime}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime}\right\|_{2}^{2}.

The restricted strong concavity with respect to
\omega_{k}
and
{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}
can be shown using similar arguments.
Letting
\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}=c_{0}(\phi_{1}/2)^{M}
, it holds with probability at least
1-1/\{\log(nd)\}^{2}
that

\displaystyle Q_{n/T}(\omega_{k}^{\prime\prime},\bar{{\bm{\Theta}}}_{-\omega_{k}}|{\bm{\Theta}})-Q_{n/T}(\omega_{k}^{\prime},\bar{{\bm{\Theta}}}_{-\omega_{k}}|{\bm{\Theta}})-\left\langle\nabla_{\omega_{k}}Q_{n/T}(\omega_{k}^{\prime},\bar{{\bm{\Theta}}}_{-\omega_{k}}|{\bm{\Theta}}),\omega_{k}^{\prime\prime}-\omega_{k}^{\prime}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\frac{T}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n/T}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m}\bar{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m})^{\top}\left(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m}\bar{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m}\right)\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m}\bar{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m})\left(\omega_{k}^{\prime\prime}-\omega_{k}^{\prime}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\frac{\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}{2}\left(\omega_{k}^{\prime\prime}-\omega_{k}^{\prime}\right)^{2},

For
{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}
, the Taylor expansion can be expressed as

\displaystyle Q_{\frac{n}{T}}({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime\prime},\bar{{\bm{\Theta}}}_{-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})-Q_{\frac{n}{T}}({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime},\bar{{\bm{\Theta}}}_{-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})-\left\langle\nabla_{{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}Q_{\frac{n}{T}}({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime},\bar{{\bm{\Theta}}}_{-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}}),{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime\prime}-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime}\right\rangle

(A53)

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{T}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{\frac{n}{T}}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\mathrm{vec}(\Delta)^{\top}\left\{\frac{d}{2d_{m}}({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime}+t\Delta)^{-1}\otimes({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime}+t\Delta)^{-1}\right\}\mathrm{vec}(\Delta),

where
\Delta={\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime\prime}-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime}
and
t\in[0,1]
. Note that

\displaystyle\sigma_{\min}\left\{({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime}+t\Delta)^{-1}\otimes({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime}+t\Delta)^{-1}\right\}=\left[\sigma_{\min}\left\{\left({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime}+t\Delta\right)^{-1}\right\}\right]^{2}

(A54)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\{\sigma_{\max}\left({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime}+t\Delta\right)\right\}^{-2}\geq\left(\left\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime}\right\|_{2}+\left\|t\Delta\right\|_{2}\right)^{-2}\geq(6\phi_{2})^{-2},

where the last inequality is due to
\left\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime}\right\|_{2}\leq\left\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\right\|_{2}+\left\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime}-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\right\|_{2}\leq 2\phi_{2}
and
\|\Delta\|_{2}\leq\left\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime}-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\right\|_{2}+\left\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime\prime}-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\right\|_{2}\leq 4\phi_{2}
.

Setting
\gamma_{m}=c_{0}\frac{d}{d_{m}}(6\phi_{2})^{-2}
and plugging (
A51
) and (
A54
) into (
A53
), it follows that

\displaystyle Q_{\frac{n}{T}}({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime\prime},\bar{{\bm{\Theta}}}_{-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})-Q_{\frac{n}{T}}({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime},\bar{{\bm{\Theta}}}_{-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})-\left\langle\nabla_{{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}Q_{\frac{n}{T}}({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime},\bar{{\bm{\Theta}}}_{-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}}),{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime\prime}-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{\gamma_{m}}{2}\left\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime\prime}-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime}\right\|_{\text{F}}^{2},

holds with probability at least
1-1/\{\log(nd)\}^{2}
.

D2
Proof of Lemma
2b

We establish, under
R=1
, gradient stability with respect to
{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}
,
\omega_{k}
and
{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}
, respectively. First, we give the population version of the first-order gradient in (
A13
), which is a direct result from Lemma
S14
.

\displaystyle\nabla_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}Q({\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime},\bar{{\bm{\Theta}}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})

(A55)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\bar{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m}\left\{(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i})_{(m)}-\bar{\omega}_{k}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m^{\prime}})^{\top}\right\}\left(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\right)\bar{\omega}_{k}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m^{\prime}})\right],

\displaystyle\nabla_{\omega_{k}}Q(\omega_{k}^{\prime},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-\omega_{k}}|{\bm{\Theta}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\left\{\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i})-\omega_{k}^{\prime}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m})\right\}^{\top}\left(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\right)\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m})\right],

\displaystyle\nabla_{{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}Q({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\left\{\frac{d}{2d_{m}}({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime})^{-1}-\frac{1}{2}\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}-\bar{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{k}\right)_{(m)}\Big(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\Big)\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}-\bar{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{k}\right)_{(m)}^{\top}\right\}\right].

(I) Gradient stability for
{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}
.

First, we extend
\nabla_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}Q({\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})-\nabla_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}Q({\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})
as

\displaystyle\nabla_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}Q({\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})-\nabla_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}Q({\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})

(A56)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[D_{\tau}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}\left\{(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i})_{(m)}-\bar{\omega}_{k}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m^{\prime}})\right\}\Big(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\Big)\bar{\omega}_{k}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m^{\prime}})\right],

where
D_{\tau}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})=\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})-\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})
.

Let
\check{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}={\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}
,
\check{\omega}_{k}={\omega_{k}}/{\omega_{k}^{\ast}}
and
\check{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m}={{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}/{\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{\text{F}}}
. Since
\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})
is the normalized error, here we focus on
\check{{\bm{\Theta}}}
rather than
{\bm{\Theta}}
. Applying Taylor expansion for
\tau_{ik}(\check{{\bm{\Theta}}})
at
\check{{\bm{\Theta}}}^{\ast}
, we have

\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})-\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})=\tau_{ik}(\check{{\bm{\Theta}}})-\tau_{ik}(\check{{\bm{\Theta}}}^{\ast})=\left(\nabla_{\check{{\bm{\Theta}}}^{\delta}}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\right)^{\top}\|\check{{\bm{\Theta}}}-\check{{\bm{\Theta}}}^{\ast}\|_{2},

(A57)

where
\check{{\bm{\Theta}}}^{\delta}=\check{{\bm{\Theta}}}^{\ast}+\delta\Delta
with
\delta\in[0,1]
and
\Delta=\check{{\bm{\Theta}}}-\check{{\bm{\Theta}}}^{\ast}
.
Plugging (
A57
) into (
A56
), we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert\nabla_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}Q({\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})-\nabla_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}Q({\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})\right\rVert_{2}^{2}\leq\tau_{0}^{2}\|\check{{\bm{\Theta}}}-\check{{\bm{\Theta}}}^{\ast}\|_{2}^{2}

(A58)

where

\tau_{0}^{2}=\mathbb{E}\left\{\left\|\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}-\bar{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{l}\right)_{(m)}\left(\prod^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\right)\bar{\omega}_{k}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m^{\prime}})\right\|_{2}^{2}\|\nabla_{\check{{\bm{\Theta}}}^{\delta}}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\|^{2}_{2}\right\}.

We claim that

\tau_{0}\leq\frac{\gamma}{12\sqrt{K(R+1)(M+1)}}.

(A59)

The detailed proof is given at
D2.1
. By the definition of
\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})
, we know that

\displaystyle\|\check{{\bm{\Theta}}}-\check{{\bm{\Theta}}}^{\ast}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k}\left\{\sum_{m}\sum_{r}\|{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\ast}\|_{2}^{2}+\sum_{r}\frac{|\omega_{k,r}-\omega_{k,r}^{\ast}|^{2}}{|\omega_{k,r}^{\ast}|^{2}}+\sum_{m}\frac{\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}}{\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}}\right\}

(A60)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k}\left\{\sum_{m}\sum_{r}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})^{2}+\sum_{r}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})^{2}+\sum_{m}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle=K(RM+R+M)\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})^{2}.

Putting the above results together, we have

\left\lVert\nabla_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}Q({\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})-\nabla_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}Q({\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})\right\rVert_{2}\leq\tau_{0}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast}).

(II) Gradient stability for
\omega_{k}
.

Similar to (
A58
), we can write

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\omega_{\max}^{2}}\left\lVert\nabla_{\omega_{k}}Q(\omega_{k}^{\prime},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-\omega_{k}}|{\bm{\Theta}})-\nabla_{\omega_{k}}Q(\omega_{k}^{\prime},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-\omega_{k}}|{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})\right\rVert_{2}^{2}\leq(\tilde{\tau}_{0}^{\prime\prime})^{2}\|\check{{\bm{\Theta}}}-\check{{\bm{\Theta}}}^{\ast}\|_{2},

(A61)

where
(\tilde{\tau}_{0}^{\prime\prime})^{2}
is defined as

\frac{1}{\omega_{\max}^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\left\{\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i})-\omega_{k}^{\prime}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m})^{\top}\right\}^{\top}\left(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}\right)\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m})\right|^{2}\|\nabla_{\check{{\bm{\Theta}}}^{\delta}}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\|_{2}\right].

We claim that

\tilde{\tau}_{0}^{\prime\prime}\leq\frac{\gamma}{12\sqrt{K(R+1)(M+1)}}.

(A62)

The detailed proof is given at
D2.1
. By (
A60
), we can get that

\left\lVert\nabla_{\omega_{k}}Q(\omega_{k}^{\prime},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-\omega_{k}}|{\bm{\Theta}})-\nabla_{\omega_{k}}Q(\omega_{k}^{\prime},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-\omega_{k}}|{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})\right\rVert_{2}\leq\tau_{0}^{\prime\prime}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})

where
\tau_{0}^{\prime\prime}=\frac{\gamma\omega_{\max}}{12\sqrt{K(R+1)(M+1)}}
.

(III) Gradient stability for
{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}
.

Similar to (
A58
), we can write

\displaystyle\frac{1}{d^{2}}\left\lVert\nabla_{{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}Q({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})-\nabla_{{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}Q({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})\right\rVert_{\text{F}}^{2}\leq\tilde{\tau}_{1}^{2}\|\check{{\bm{\Theta}}}-\check{{\bm{\Theta}}}^{\ast}\|_{2},

(A63)

where

\tilde{\tau}_{1}^{2}=\mathbb{E}\left\{\left\|\frac{1}{2d_{m}}({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime})^{-1}-\frac{1}{2d}\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}-\bar{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{k}\right)_{(m)}\Big(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\Big)\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}-\bar{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{k}\right)_{(m)}^{\top}\right\|_{\text{F}}\|\nabla_{\check{{\bm{\Theta}}}^{\delta}}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\|_{2}\right\}.

We claim that

\tilde{\tau}_{1}\leq\frac{\gamma}{12\sqrt{K(R+1)(M+1)}}.

(A64)

The detailed proof is given at
D2.1
. By (
A60
), we can get that

\left\lVert\nabla_{{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}Q({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})-\nabla_{{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}Q({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})\right\rVert_{\text{F}}\leq\tau_{1}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})

where
\tau_{1}=\frac{\gamma d}{12\sqrt{K(R+1)(M+1)}}
.

D2.1
Claim of (
A59
), (
A62
) and (
A64
)

In this proof, we use (
A14
) in Lemma
S14
and Condition
3
to bound
\tau_{0}
,
\tilde{\tau}_{0}^{\prime\prime}
and
\tilde{\tau}_{1}
respectively.

Recall that
\check{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}={\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}
,
\check{\omega}_{k}={\omega_{k}}/{\omega_{k}^{\ast}}
. We have
\nabla_{\omega_{l}}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})
and
\nabla_{\check{\omega}_{l}}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})
differ by a factor of
\omega_{l}^{\ast}
, and
\nabla_{{\bm{\Omega}}_{l,m}}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})
and
\nabla_{\check{\bm{\Omega}}_{l,m}}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})
differ by a factor of
\sqrt{d_{m}}
. Given these, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert\nabla_{\check{{\bm{\Theta}}}^{\delta}}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\right\rVert_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{l\neq k}(\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\tau_{il}({\bm{\Theta}}))^{2}\left\|J_{i}(\check{\bm{\theta}}^{\delta}_{l})\right\|_{2}^{2}+\left\{\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})(1-\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}))\right\}^{2}\left\|J_{i}(\check{\bm{\theta}}_{k}^{\delta})\right\|_{2}^{2}

(A65)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{l\neq k}(\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\tau_{il}({\bm{\Theta}}))^{2}g_{1}\{J_{i}({\bm{\theta}}^{\delta}_{l})\}^{2}+\left\{\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})(1-\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}))\right\}^{2}g_{1}\{J_{i}({\bm{\theta}}_{k}^{\delta})\}^{2}.

where
g_{1}\{J_{i}({\bm{\theta}}_{l})\}=\|J_{i,1}({\bm{\theta}}_{l})\|_{2}+w_{\max}^{\ast}\|J_{i,2}({\bm{\theta}}_{l})\|_{2}+\sqrt{d_{\max}}\|J_{i,3}({\bm{\theta}}_{l})\|_{2}
. The last inequality is true, because

\displaystyle\left\|J_{i}(\check{\bm{\theta}}_{l})\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\|J_{i,1}({\bm{\theta}}_{l})\|_{2}^{2}+{w_{l}^{\ast}}^{2}\|J_{i,2}({\bm{\theta}}_{l})\|_{2}^{2}+d_{m}\|J_{i,3}({\bm{\theta}}_{l})\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|J_{i,1}({\bm{\theta}}_{l})\|_{2}^{2}+w_{\max}^{2}\|J_{i,2}({\bm{\theta}}_{l})\|_{2}^{2}+d_{\max}\|J_{i,3}({\bm{\theta}}_{l})\|_{2}^{2}\leq g_{1}\{J_{i}({\bm{\theta}}_{l})\}^{2}.

Let
g_{2}\{J_{i}({\bm{\theta}}^{\prime}_{k})\}=\|J_{i,1}({\bm{\theta}}^{\prime}_{k})\|_{2}+\|J_{i,2}({\bm{\theta}}^{\prime}_{k})\|_{2}/\omega_{\max}+\|J_{i,3}({\bm{\theta}}^{\prime}_{k})\|_{2}/d
, it is straightforward to get that

\displaystyle\left\|\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}-\bar{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{l}\right)_{(m)}\left(\prod^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\right)\bar{\omega}_{k}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m^{\prime}})\right\|_{2}\leq\|J_{i,1}(\bar{\bm{\theta}}_{k})\|_{2}\leq g_{2}\{J_{i}(\bar{\bm{\theta}}_{k})\};

(A66)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\omega_{\max}}\left\|\left\{\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i})-\bar{\omega}_{k}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m})^{\top}\right\}^{\top}\left(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}\right)\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m})\right\|_{2}\leq\frac{\|J_{i,2}(\bar{\bm{\theta}}_{k})\|_{2}}{\omega_{\max}}\leq g_{2}\{J_{i}(\bar{\bm{\theta}}_{k})\};

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{2d_{m}}(\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m})^{-1}-\frac{1}{2d}\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}-\bar{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{k}\right)_{(m)}\Big(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\Big)\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}-\bar{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{k}\right)_{(m)}^{\top}\right\|_{\text{F}}\leq\|J_{i,3}(\bar{\bm{\theta}}_{k})\|_{2}/d\leq g_{2}\{J_{i}(\bar{\bm{\theta}}_{k})\}.

Next, we can use (
A65
), (
A66
) and Condition
3
to bound
\tau_{0}
,
\tilde{\tau}_{0}^{\prime\prime}
and
\tilde{\tau}_{1}
respectively. We define
W_{ikl}
as

W_{ikl}=g_{1}\{J_{i}({\bm{\theta}}_{l})\}\times g_{2}\{J_{i}({\bm{\theta}}^{\prime}_{k})\},

(A67)

where
g_{1}\{J_{i}({\bm{\theta}}_{l})\}=\|J_{i,1}({\bm{\theta}}_{l})\|_{2}+w_{\max}^{\ast}\|J_{i,2}({\bm{\theta}}_{l})\|_{2}+\sqrt{d_{\max}}\|J_{i,3}({\bm{\theta}}_{l})\|_{2}
,
g_{2}\{J_{i}({\bm{\theta}}^{\prime}_{k})\}=\|J_{i,1}({\bm{\theta}}^{\prime}_{k})\|_{2}+\|J_{i,2}({\bm{\theta}}^{\prime}_{k})\|_{2}/\omega_{\max}+\|J_{i,3}({\bm{\theta}}^{\prime}_{k})\|_{2}/d
.

Bound for
\tau_{0}
.
By (
A65
), (
A66
) and Condition
3
, we have

\displaystyle\tau_{0}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{l\neq k}W_{ikl}^{2}(\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\tau_{il}({\bm{\Theta}}))^{2}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[W_{ikl}^{2}\{\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})(1-\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}))\}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{l\neq k}\frac{\gamma^{2}}{24^{2}K^{4}(R+1)^{4}(M+1)^{2}}+\frac{\gamma^{2}(K-1)^{2}}{24^{2}K^{4}(R+1)^{4}(M+1)^{2}}

\displaystyle<\frac{\gamma^{2}}{144K^{2}(R+1)^{4}(M+1)^{2}}.

By the definition of
\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})
, it holds that
\|\check{{\bm{\Theta}}}-\check{{\bm{\Theta}}}^{\ast}\|_{2}^{2}\leq K(RM+R+M)\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})^{2}
.

Bound for
\tilde{\tau}_{0}^{\prime\prime}
.
By (
A65
), (
A66
), Condition
3
and (
A61
), it holds that

\displaystyle(\tilde{\tau}_{0}^{\prime\prime})^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{l\neq k}W_{ikl}^{2}(\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\tau_{il}({\bm{\Theta}}))^{2}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[W_{ikl}^{2}\{\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})(1-\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}))\}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{l\neq k}\frac{\gamma^{2}}{24^{2}K^{4}(R+1)^{4}(M+1)^{2}}+\frac{\gamma^{2}(K-1)^{2}}{24^{2}K^{4}(R+1)^{4}(M+1)^{2}}

\displaystyle<\frac{\gamma^{2}}{144K^{2}(R+1)^{4}(M+1)^{2}}.

Bound for
\tilde{\tau}_{1}
part.
By (
A65
), (
A66
) and Condition
3
, it holds that

\displaystyle\tilde{\tau}_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{l\neq k}W_{ikl}^{2}(\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\tau_{il}({\bm{\Theta}}))^{2}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[W_{ikl}^{2}\{\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})(1-\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}))\}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{l\neq k}\frac{\gamma^{2}}{24^{2}K^{4}(R+1)^{4}(M+1)^{2}}+\frac{\gamma^{2}(K-1)^{2}}{24^{2}K^{4}(R+1)^{4}(M+1)^{2}}

\displaystyle<\frac{\gamma^{2}}{144K^{2}(R+1)^{4}(M+1)^{2}}.

D3
Proof of Lemma
3b

We first introduce some notation. Since
{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}
and
{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k,m}^{\ast}
are symmetric matrices, we have

\left\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\right\|_{\max}\leq\max_{k,m}\left\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\right\|_{2}\leq\phi_{2},\quad\left\|{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\right\|_{\max}\leq\max_{k,m}\left\|{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\right\|_{2}\leq 1/\phi_{1}.

(A68)

To ease notation, we define

\displaystyle h_{{\bm{\Theta}},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}({\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime})=\nabla_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}Q_{n/T}({\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})-\nabla_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}Q({\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}}),

\displaystyle h_{{\bm{\Theta}},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}(\omega_{k}^{\prime})=\nabla_{\omega_{k}}Q_{n/T}(\omega_{k}^{\prime},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-\omega_{k}}|{\bm{\Theta}})-\nabla_{\omega_{k}}Q(\omega_{k}^{\prime},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-\omega_{k}}|{\bm{\Theta}}),

\displaystyle h_{{\bm{\Theta}},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime})=\nabla_{{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}Q_{n/T}({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})-\nabla_{{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}Q({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}}).

Recall
\mathcal{P}_{1}({\bm{\beta}}_{k,m})=\|{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}\|_{1}
and
\mathcal{P}_{2}({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m})=\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}\|_{1,\text{off}}
, we have that

\displaystyle\left\|h_{{\bm{\Theta}},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}({\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime})\right\|_{\mathcal{P}_{1}^{\ast}}\leq\max_{k}\underbrace{\left\|h_{{\bm{\Theta}},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}({\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime})\right\|_{\infty}}_{\text{I}},\quad\left|h_{{\bm{\Theta}},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}(\omega_{k}^{\prime})\right|\leq\max_{k}\underbrace{\left|h_{{\bm{\Theta}},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}(\omega_{k}^{\prime})\right|}_{\text{II}},

(A69)

\displaystyle\left\|h_{{\bm{\Theta}},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime})\right\|_{\mathcal{P}_{2}^{\ast}}\leq\max_{k}\underbrace{\left\|h_{{\bm{\Theta}},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime})\right\|_{\max}}_{\text{III}},

where
\mathcal{P}_{1}^{\ast}
,
\mathcal{P}_{2}^{\ast}
be the dual norms of
\mathcal{P}_{1}
,
\mathcal{P}_{2}
, respectively.

(I) Bounding
h_{{\bm{\Theta}},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}({\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime})
.

Recall (
A13
), and we have

\displaystyle h_{{\bm{\Theta}},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}({\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime})

\displaystyle=\frac{T}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n/T}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}\left\{(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i})_{(m)}-\bar{\omega}_{k}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m^{\prime}})^{\top}\right\}\Big(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\Big)\bar{\omega}_{k}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m^{\prime}})

\displaystyle-\mathbb{E}\left[\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}\left\{(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i})_{(m)}-\bar{\omega}_{k}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m^{\prime}})^{\top}\right\}\Big(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\Big)\bar{\omega}_{k}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m^{\prime}})\right].

By the triangle inequality, term (I) can be bounded by

\small\begin{split}&\left\|\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}\right\|_{2}\underbrace{\left\|\frac{T}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n/T}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\right)_{(m)}-\mathbb{E}\left\{\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\right)_{(m)}\right\}\right\|_{\max}}_{\text{I}_{1}}\left\|\Big(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\Big)\bar{\omega}_{k}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m^{\prime}})\right\|_{2}\\
&+\underbrace{\left\|\frac{T}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n/T}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})-\mathbb{E}(\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}))\right\|_{\infty}}_{\text{I}_{2}}\left\|\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}\bar{\omega}^{2}_{k}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m^{\prime}})^{\top}\Big(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\Big)\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m^{\prime}})\right\|_{\infty}.\end{split}

Consider the set of missing data
\{Z_{i},\,i\in[n]\}
, we have

\begin{split}&\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}|Z_{i}=k^{\prime}\sim\mathcal{N}_{T}(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k^{\prime}}^{\ast},{\underline{{\bm{\Sigma}}}_{k^{\prime}}^{\ast}}),\\
&\mathbb{P}(Z_{i}=k^{\prime})=\pi_{k^{\prime}},\quad\sum_{k^{\prime}=1}^{K}\pi_{k^{\prime}}=1.\end{split}

Correspondingly, the
j
-th coordinate of
\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i})
can be written as

\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i})_{j}=\sum_{k^{\prime}=1}^{K}{\bf I}(Z_{i}=k^{\prime})(\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k^{\prime}}^{\ast})_{j}+V_{j,k^{\prime}}).

(A70)

Here
\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k^{\prime}}^{\ast})_{j}=\mathbb{E}\left\{\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i})_{j}|Z_{i}=k^{\prime}\right\}
and
V_{j,k^{\prime}}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,\text{var}\left(\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i})_{j}|Z_{i}=k^{\prime}\right))
.

Denote by

{\bm{M}}=\frac{T}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n/T}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\right)_{(m)}-\mathbb{E}\left\{\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\right)_{(m)}\right\},

where
{\bm{M}}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{m}\times\frac{d}{d_{m}}}
and let
\mathrm{vec}({\bm{M}})_{j}
be the
j
-th element of
\mathrm{vec}({\bm{M}})
.
Plugging (
A70
) into
\mathrm{vec}({\bm{M}})_{j}
, it can be bounded as below.

\displaystyle|\mathrm{vec}({\bm{M}})_{j}|

\displaystyle=\left|\frac{T}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n/T}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i})_{j}-\mathbb{E}\left\{\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i})_{j}\right\}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k^{\prime}=1}^{K}\underbrace{\left|\frac{T}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n/T}{\bf I}(Z_{i}=k^{\prime})\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k^{\prime}}^{\ast})_{j}-\mathbb{E}\left\{{\bf I}(Z_{i}=k^{\prime})\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k^{\prime}}^{\ast})_{j}\right\}\right|}_{{\bm{M}}_{1}(j)}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k^{\prime}=1}^{K}\underbrace{\left|\frac{T}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n/T}{\bf I}(Z_{i}=k^{\prime})\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})V_{j,k^{\prime}}-\mathbb{E}\left\{{\bf I}(Z_{i}=k^{\prime})\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})V_{j,k^{\prime}}\right\}\right|}_{{\bm{M}}_{2}(j)}.

We claim that, with probability at least
1-p_{n}
,

\lvert{\bm{M}}_{1}(j)\rvert\leq\sqrt{\frac{4}{D_{1}}}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k^{\prime}}^{\ast}\|_{\max}\sqrt{\frac{\log(e/p_{n})T}{n}},

(A71)

and

\lvert{\bm{M}}_{2}(j)\rvert\leq\phi_{1}^{-M/2}\sqrt{\frac{4D_{2}^{2}}{D_{3}}}\sqrt{\frac{\log(2/p_{n})T}{n}}.

(A72)

The detailed proof is given in
D3.1
.

Plugging (
A71
) and (
A72
) into
\mathrm{vec}({\bm{M}})_{j}
, it arrives that

\mathrm{vec}({\bm{M}})_{j}\leq\lvert{\bm{M}}_{1}(j)\rvert+\lvert{\bm{M}}_{2}(j)\rvert\leq\sqrt{\frac{4}{D_{0}}}\sum_{k}(\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}^{\ast}\|_{\max}+\phi_{1}^{-M/2})\sqrt{\frac{\log(e/p_{n})T}{n}},

with probability at least
1-2Kp_{n}
, where
D_{0}=\min\{D_{1},D_{3}/D_{2}^{2}\}
. Jointly for all
j
, we have

\text{I}_{1}\leq\sqrt{\frac{4}{D_{0}}}\sum_{k}(\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}^{\ast}\|_{\max}+\phi_{1}^{-M/2})\sqrt{\frac{\log(e/p_{n})+\log d}{n/T}},

(A73)

with probability at least
1-2Kp_{n}
.

Next, we consider term
\text{I}_{2}
, i.e.,

\text{I}_{2}=\left\lVert\frac{T}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n/T}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})-\mathbb{E}(\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}))\right\rVert_{\infty}.

By noting
\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\in[0,1]
, Hoeffding’s inequality gives,

\mathbb{P}\left(\left\lvert\frac{T}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n/T}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})-\mathbb{E}(\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}))\right\rvert\leq t\right)\geq 1-2e^{-2nt^{2}/T},

which implies, with probability at least
1-p_{n}
,

\left\lvert\frac{T}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n/T}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})-\mathbb{E}(\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}))\right\rvert\leq\sqrt{\frac{1}{2}\log({2}/{p_{n}})T/n}.

(A74)

By noting the bounds of
\text{I}_{1}
and
\text{I}_{2}
in (
A73
) and (
A74
), respectively, there exists some constant
D_{4}>0
such that
\text{I}_{2}\leq D_{4}\text{I}_{1}
. Letting
\varphi_{K0}=\sum_{k}(\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}^{\ast}\|_{\max}+\phi_{1}^{-M/2})
, we have

\displaystyle\text{I}

\displaystyle\precsim\left\|\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}\right\|_{2}\left\lVert\Big(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\Big)\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m^{\prime}})\right\rVert_{2}\varphi_{K0}\sqrt{\frac{\log({e}/{p_{n}})+\log d}{n/T}}.

(A75)

holds with probability at least
1-(2K+1)p_{n}
.
By (
A68
) and Condition
2
, we have
\left\|\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}\right\|_{2}\leq\frac{3}{2}\phi_{2}
and
\left\lVert\Big(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\Big)\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m^{\prime}})\right\rVert_{2}\leq(3\phi_{2}/2)^{M-1}
. Since
p_{n}=1/\{\log(nd)\}^{2}
, we have
{\log(e/p_{n})}/{\log d}=o(1)
and it holds with probability at least
1-{K(2K+1)}/\{\log(nd)\}^{2}
that,

\max_{k}\text{I}\leq c_{1}\omega_{\max}\sqrt{\frac{T\log d}{n}},

(A76)

where
c_{1}
is some positive constant and it depends on the spectral limits
(\phi_{1},\phi_{2})
.

(I) Bounding
h_{{\bm{\Theta}},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}(\omega_{k}^{\prime})
.

Recall (
A13
), and we have

\begin{split}&h_{{\bm{\Theta}},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}(\omega_{k}^{\prime})=\left\{\frac{T}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n/T}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})-\mathbb{E}(\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}))\right\}\omega_{k}^{\prime}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m})^{\top}\left(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}\right)\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}).\end{split}

By (
A74
) and
\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m})^{\top}\left(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}\right)\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m})\leq(3\phi_{2}/2)^{M}
, it holds with probability at least
1-{K}/\{\log(nd)\}^{2}
for term (II) that

\max_{k}\text{II}\leq c^{\prime\prime}_{1}\omega_{\max}\sqrt{\frac{T\log(\log(nd))}{n}},

where
c^{\prime\prime}_{1}
is some positive constant and it depends on the spectral limits
(\phi_{1},\phi_{2})
.

(I) Bounding
h_{{\bm{\Theta}},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime})
.

Recall (
A13
), and the term
h_{{\bm{\Theta}},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime})
can be written as

\begin{split}&\frac{T}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n/T}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\frac{d}{2d_{m}}({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime})^{-1}-\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}-\bar{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{k}\right)_{(m)}\Big(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\Big)\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}-\bar{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{k}\right)_{(m)}^{\top}\\
&-\mathbb{E}(\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}))\frac{d}{2d_{m}}({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime})^{-1}-\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}-\bar{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{k}\right)_{(m)}\Big(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\Big)\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}-\bar{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{k}\right)_{(m)}^{\top}\right].\end{split}

Correspondingly, writing
\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{i,k}=\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}-\bar{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{k}
and term (III) can be decomposed as

\begin{split}&\text{III}\leq\text{III}_{1}+\text{III}_{2},\end{split}

where
\text{III}_{1}=\left\lVert\left[\frac{T}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n/T}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})-\mathbb{E}(\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}))\right]\frac{d}{2d_{m}}({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime})^{-1}\right\rVert_{\max}
and
2\text{III}_{2}
is defined as the max norm of

{\footnotesize\frac{T}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n/T}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\left(\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{i,k}\right)_{(m)}\Big(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\Big)\left(\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{i,k}\right)_{(m)}^{\top}-\mathbb{E}\left\{\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\left(\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{i,k}\right)_{(m)}\Big(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\Big)\left(\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{X}}}_{i,k}\right)_{(m)}^{\top}\right\}}.

By (
A74
) and Condition
2
, we have, with probability at least
1-p_{n}
,

\text{III}_{1}\leq\sqrt{\frac{1}{2(\phi_{1}/2)^{2}}\log({2}/{p_{n}})}\sqrt{\frac{(d/d_{m})^{2}}{n/T}}.

For
\text{III}_{2}
, we claim that

\displaystyle\text{III}_{2}\precsim

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{k^{\prime}}\max_{l}\|(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k^{\prime}}^{\ast})_{(m)}(l.\cdot)\|_{2}+\max_{k,l}\|(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k^{\prime}}^{\ast})_{(m)}(l.\cdot)\|_{2}\varphi_{K0}\right)\sqrt{\frac{Td\log d}{nd_{m}}}

(A77)

\displaystyle+\sum_{k^{\prime}}\max_{l}\|(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k^{\prime}}^{\ast})_{(m)}(l.\cdot)\|_{2}^{2}\sqrt{\frac{T\log d}{n}},

with at least probability
1-(8K+1)p_{n}
. The detailed proof is given in
D3.2
. Define
\varphi_{K}=\max_{k,m,l}\|(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}^{\ast})_{(m)}(l,\cdot)\|_{2}
. With the upper bounds of
\text{III}_{1}
and
\text{III}_{2}
, we have

\begin{split}\max_{k}\text{III}\precsim&\left\{\varphi_{K}(\varphi_{K0}+1)\sqrt{\frac{Td\log d}{nd_{m}}}+\varphi_{K}^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\log(d_{m})+\log(2/p_{n})}{n/T}}\right\}\\
&+\sqrt{\log({2}/{p_{n}})}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{(d/d_{m})^{2}}{n/T}},\end{split}

(A78)

with probability at least
1-K(8K+2)p_{n}
. By Condition
1
, we know that
\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}^{\ast}\|_{\max}=O(1)
. Since
(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}^{\ast})_{(m)}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{m}\times\frac{d}{d_{m}}}
and it is easily seen that
\varphi_{k}=\max_{k,m,l}\sqrt{\sum_{j}(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}^{\ast})_{(m)}(l,j)^{2}}\leq\max\limits_{k}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}^{\ast}\|_{\max}\sqrt{\frac{d}{d_{m}}}
. Therefore, for some constant
c_{2}>0
, it holds that

\max_{k}\text{III}\leq c_{2}\frac{d}{d_{m}}\sqrt{\frac{T\log d}{n}},

with probability at least
1-{K(8K+2)}/\{\log(nd)\}^{2}
.

D3.1
Claim of (
A71
) and (
A72
)

We show (
A71
) first, which is the upper bound of
{\bm{M}}_{1}(j)
. From the fact that
|{\bf I}(Z_{i}=k^{\prime})\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k^{\prime}}^{\ast})_{j}|\leq\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k^{\prime}}^{\ast}\|_{\max}
, it holds that
{\bf I}(Z_{i}=k^{\prime})\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}^{\ast}(j)
is a sub-Gaussian random variable with sub-Gaussian norm bounded above by
\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}^{\ast}\|_{\max}
. That is
\|{\bf I}(Z_{i}=k^{\prime})\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k^{\prime}}^{\ast})_{j}\|_{\psi_{2}}\leq\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k^{\prime}}^{\ast}\|_{\max}
, where
\|\cdot\|_{\psi_{2}}
denotes the sub-Gaussian norm. By Lemma
S2
, we get that

\left\lVert{\bf I}(Z_{i}=k^{\prime})\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k^{\prime}}^{\ast})_{j}-\mathbb{E}\left\{{\bf I}(Z_{i}=k^{\prime})\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k^{\prime}}^{\ast})_{j}\right\}\right\rVert_{\psi_{2}}\leq 2\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k^{\prime}}^{\ast}\|_{\max}.

Standard concentration results give that, for some positive constant
D_{1}
and any
t>0
,

\mathbb{P}(\lvert{\bm{M}}_{1}(j)\rvert\geq t)\leq e\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{D_{1}nt^{2}}{4T\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k^{\prime}}^{\ast}\|_{\max}^{2}}\right),

which implies that, with probability at least
1-p_{n}
,

\lvert{\bm{M}}_{1}(j)\rvert\leq\sqrt{\frac{4}{D_{1}}}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k^{\prime}}^{\ast}\|_{\max}\sqrt{\frac{\log(e/p_{n})T}{n}}.

We then move to the proof of (
A72
) and it is to bound term
{\bm{M}}_{2}(j)
. Similarly,
\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}){\bf I}(Z_{i}=k^{\prime})
is a sub-Gaussian random variable, since that
\|\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}){\bf I}(Z_{i}=k^{\prime})\|_{\psi_{2}}\leq 1
. Moreover,
V_{j,k^{\prime}}
is a Gaussian random variable with sub-Gaussian norm
\|V_{j,k^{\prime}}\|_{\psi_{2}}\leq{1}/{\phi_{1}^{M/2}}
. Then by Lemma
S1
, it holds that
{\bf I}(Z_{i}=k^{\prime})\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})V_{j,k^{\prime}}
is sub-exponential random variable. Moreover, there exists a positive constant
D_{2}
such that

\|{\bf I}(Z_{i}=k^{\prime})\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})V_{j,k^{\prime}}\|_{\psi_{1}}\leq{D_{2}}/{\phi_{1}^{M/2}}.

Applying Lemma
S2
, we can get that

\|{\bf I}(Z_{i}=k^{\prime})\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})V_{j,k^{\prime}}-\mathbb{E}\left\{{\bf I}(Z_{i}=k^{\prime})\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})V_{j,k^{\prime}}\right\}\|_{\psi_{1}}\leq{2D_{2}}/{\phi_{1}^{M/2}}.

Following the concentration inequality of sub-exponential random variables
(
Vershynin 2018
)
, there exists some positive constant
D_{3}
such that the following inequality

\mathbb{P}\left(\lvert{\bm{M}}_{2}(j)\rvert\geq t\right)\leq 2\exp\left(-D_{3}\min\left\{\frac{t^{2}}{4D_{2}^{2}/(\phi_{1}^{M})},\frac{t}{2D_{2}/(\phi_{1}^{M/2})}\right\}\frac{n}{T}\right),

holds for any
t\geq 0
. For a sufficiently small
t
, the above inequality reduces to

\mathbb{P}\left(\lvert{\bm{M}}_{2}(j)\rvert\geq t\right)\leq 2\exp\left(-D_{3}\frac{nt^{2}}{4TD_{2}^{2}/(\phi_{1}^{M})}\right),

which implies that, with probability at least
1-p_{n}

\lvert{\bm{M}}_{2}(j)\rvert\leq\phi_{1}^{-M/2}\sqrt{\frac{4D_{2}^{2}}{D_{3}}}\sqrt{\frac{\log(2/p_{n})T}{n}}.

D3.2
Claim of (
A77
)

For
\text{III}_{2}
, it can be bounded as
\text{III}_{2}<\text{III}_{21}+\text{III}_{22}+\text{III}_{23}+\text{III}_{24}
, that is,

\displaystyle\underbrace{\left\lVert\frac{T}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n/T}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\right)_{(m)}\Big(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\Big)\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\right)_{(m)}^{\top}-\mathbb{E}\left\{\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\right)_{(m)}\Big(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\Big)\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\right)_{(m)}^{\top}\right\}\right\rVert_{\max}}_{\text{III}_{21}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\underbrace{\left\lVert\frac{T}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n/T}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\right)_{(m)}\Big(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\Big)\left(\bar{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{k}\right)_{(m)}^{\top}-\mathbb{E}\left\{\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\right)_{(m)}\Big(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\Big)\left(\bar{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{k}\right)_{(m)}^{\top}\right\}\right\rVert_{\max}}_{\text{III}_{22}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\underbrace{\left\lVert\frac{T}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n/T}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\left(\bar{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{k}\right)_{(m)}\Big(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\Big)\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\right)_{(m)}^{\top}-\mathbb{E}\left\{\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\left(\bar{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{k}\right)_{(m)}\Big(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\Big)\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\right)_{(m)}^{\top}\right\}\right\rVert_{\max}}_{\text{III}_{23}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\underbrace{\left\lVert\frac{T}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n/T}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\left(\bar{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{k}\right)_{(m)}\Big(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\Big)\left(\bar{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{k}\right)_{(m)}^{\top}-\mathbb{E}\left\{\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\left(\bar{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{k}\right)_{(m)}\Big(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\Big)\left(\bar{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{k}\right)_{(m)}^{\top}\right\}\right\rVert_{\max}}_{\text{III}_{24}}.

To bound
\text{III}_{21}
, we introduce the following lemma with its proof delayed to Section
E7
.

Lemma S15
.

Let
\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{1},\ldots,\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{n}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{1}\times d_{2}\times d_{3}}
be i.i.d. from the mixture model in (
2
) and define
{\bm{Y}}_{i}=\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\right)_{(m)}\left\{\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\left(\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\right)^{1/2}\right\}
. Given
{\bm{\Theta}},\bar{{\bm{\Theta}}}\in\mathcal{B}_{\frac{1}{2}}({\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})
, we have that

\displaystyle\left\lVert\frac{T}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n/T}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}){\bm{Y}}_{i}{\bm{Y}}_{i}^{\top}-\mathbb{E}\left\{\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}){\bm{Y}}_{i}{\bm{Y}}_{i}^{\top}\right\}\right\rVert_{\max}

\displaystyle\precsim

\displaystyle\sum_{k^{\prime}}\left(\max_{l}\|(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k^{\prime}}^{\ast})_{(m)}(l.\cdot)\|_{2}^{2}\sqrt{d/d_{m}}+1\right)\times\sqrt{\frac{Td_{m}(2\log(d_{m})+\log({2}/{p_{n}}))}{nd}}

with probability at least
1-\frac{4K}{\{\log(nd)\}^{2}}
.

By Lemma
S15
,
\text{III}_{21}
can be bounded by

\text{III}_{21}\precsim\sum_{k^{\prime}}\left(\max_{l}\|(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k^{\prime}}^{\ast})_{(m)}(l.\cdot)\|_{2}^{2}\sqrt{d/d_{m}}+1\right)\times\sqrt{\frac{Td_{m}(2\log(d_{m})+\log({2}/{p_{n}}))}{nd}}

(A79)

with probability at least
1-4Kp_{n}
. Here
p_{n}=1/\{\log(nd)\}^{2}
.

For
\text{III}_{22}
, it holds with probability at least
1-2Kp_{n}
that

\displaystyle\text{III}_{22}=\left\lVert\left[\sum_{i=1}^{n/T}\frac{\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})}{n/T}\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\right)_{(m)}-\mathbb{E}\left\{\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\right)_{(m)}\right\}\right]\Big(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\Big)\left(\bar{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{k}\right)_{(m)}^{\top}\right\rVert_{\max}

(A80)

\displaystyle\precsim\max_{l}\left\lVert\frac{T}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n/T}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\right)_{(m)}(l,\cdot)-\mathbb{E}\left\{\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\right)_{(m)}(l,\cdot)\right\}\right\rVert_{2}\max_{l}\|(\bar{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{k})_{(m)}(l.\cdot)\|_{2}

\displaystyle\precsim\varphi_{K0}\max_{l}\|(\bar{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{k})_{(m)}(l.\cdot)\|_{2}\sqrt{\frac{d\log(e/p_{n})+d\log d}{nd_{m}/T}},

where
\varphi_{K0}
is as defined in (
A75
) and the second inequality is due to (
A73
) and the fact that
\|\bm{a}\|_{2}\leq\sqrt{d}\max_{j}|\bm{a}(j)|
for any
\bm{a}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
. The first inequality is true, because

\|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{A}\bm{B}\|_{\max}=\max_{j}|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{A}\bm{B}_{\cdot j}|\leq\max_{j}\|\bm{a}\|_{2}\|\bm{A}\bm{B}_{\cdot j}\|_{2}\leq\|\bm{a}\|_{2}\sigma_{\max}(\bm{A})\max_{j}\|\bm{B}_{\cdot j}\|_{2}.

Term
\text{III}_{23}
can be bounded similarly.
For
\text{III}_{24}
, we have

\displaystyle\text{III}_{24}

\displaystyle\leq\left|\frac{T}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n/T}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})-\mathbb{E}(\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}))\right|\left\lVert\left(\bar{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{k}\right)_{(m)}\Big(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\Big)\left(\bar{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{k}\right)_{(m)}^{\top}\right\rVert_{\max}

(A81)

\displaystyle\precsim\max_{l}\|(\bar{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{k})_{(m)}(l.\cdot)\|_{2}^{2}\sqrt{\log({2}/{p_{n}})/n},

with probability at least
1-p_{n}
.
By Condition
2
, it holds that that

\|(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k})_{(m)}(l,\cdot)\|_{2}\leq\|(\bar{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{k}^{\ast})_{(m)}(l,\cdot)\|_{2}+\|(\bar{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{k})_{(m)}(l,\cdot)-(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}^{\ast})_{(m)}(l,\cdot)\|_{2}\precsim\|(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k^{\prime}}^{\ast})_{(m)}(l,\cdot)\|_{2}.

Putting (
A79
), (
A80
) and (
A81
) together, we have

\displaystyle\text{III}_{2}\precsim

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{k^{\prime}}\max_{l}\|(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k^{\prime}}^{\ast})_{(m)}(l.\cdot)\|_{2}+\max_{k,l}\|(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k^{\prime}}^{\ast})_{(m)}(l.\cdot)\|_{2}\varphi_{K0}\right)\sqrt{\frac{Td\log d}{nd_{m}}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k^{\prime}}\max_{l}\|(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k^{\prime}}^{\ast})_{(m)}(l.\cdot)\|_{2}^{2}\sqrt{\frac{T\log d}{n}},

with at least probability
1-(8K+1)p_{n}
.

D4
Proof of Lemma
4b

Given
{\bm{\Theta}}^{(0)}
, we bound
\|{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{(1)}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{2}
,
|\omega_{k}^{(1)}-\omega_{k}^{\ast}|
and
\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{(1)}-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{\text{F}}
in this proof. To this end, we first state a set of key lemmas with their proofs delayed to Sections
E8
-
E10
.

Lemma S16
.

Suppose Conditions
1
-
4
hold for
{\bm{\Theta}}
with
\gamma d_{\max}\leq C_{1}
for some constant
C_{1}>0
. Let
\lambda_{0}=4\epsilon_{0}+\tau_{0}\frac{\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})}{\sqrt{s_{1}}}
,
\bar{\bm{\Theta}}
satisfies Condition
2
and

\tilde{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}=\arg\max_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime}}Q_{n}({\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})-\lambda_{0}\left\|{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}\right\|_{1}.

Define
{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime\prime}=\frac{\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,r,m}}{\|\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,r,m}\|_{2}}
, it holds that, with probability at least
1-(2K^{2}+K+1)/\{\log(nd)\}^{2}
,

\displaystyle\left\|{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime\prime}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\right\|_{2}\leq\frac{16\sqrt{s_{1}}\epsilon_{0}}{C\gamma_{0}}+\frac{4\tau_{0}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})}{C\gamma_{0}},

\displaystyle\text{if }R=1,

\displaystyle\left\|{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime\prime}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\ast}\right\|_{2}\leq\frac{16\sqrt{s_{1}}\epsilon_{R,0}}{C\gamma_{0}^{\prime}}+\frac{4\tau_{0}^{\prime}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})}{C\gamma_{0}^{\prime}},

\displaystyle\text{if }R>1,

and
{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime\prime}
satisfies Condition
2
. Here
\epsilon_{0}=c_{1}\omega_{\max}\sqrt{\log d\cdot T/n}
,
\epsilon_{R,0}=c^{\prime}_{1}\omega_{\max}\sqrt{T\frac{\log d}{n}}
,
\tau_{0},\gamma_{0}
are from Lemmas
2b
-
3b
,
\tau_{0}^{\prime},\gamma_{0}^{\prime}
are from Lemmas
6b
-
7b
and
c_{1},c_{1}^{\prime},C
are positive constants.

Lemma S17
.

Suppose Conditions
1
-
4
hold for
{\bm{\Theta}}
with
\gamma d_{\max}\leq C_{1}
for some constant
C_{1}>0
. Let
\bar{\bm{\Theta}}
satisfies Condition
2
and

\omega_{k,r}^{\prime\prime}=\arg\max_{\omega_{k,r}^{\prime}}\,Q_{n}(\omega_{k,r}^{\prime},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-\omega_{k,r}}|{\bm{\Theta}}).

It holds that, with probability at least
1-(2K^{2}+2K+1)/\{\log(nd)\}^{2}
,

\displaystyle\frac{|\omega_{k}^{\prime\prime}-\omega_{k}^{\ast}|}{|\omega_{k}^{\ast}|}\leq\frac{2\epsilon_{0}^{\prime\prime}}{\omega_{k}^{\ast}\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}+\frac{2\tau_{0}^{\prime\prime}}{\omega_{k}^{\ast}\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})+2(3\phi_{2}/2)^{M-1}\sqrt{M}\sum_{m}\|\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{2},

\displaystyle\text{if }R=1,

\displaystyle\frac{|\omega_{k,r}^{\prime\prime}-\omega_{k,r}^{\ast}|}{|\omega_{k,r}^{\ast}|}\leq\frac{2\epsilon_{0}^{\prime\prime}}{\omega_{k,r}^{\ast}\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}+\frac{2(\tau_{0}^{\prime\prime}+\tau_{0}^{\prime\prime\prime})}{\omega_{k,r}^{\ast}\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})

\displaystyle+\frac{2R(3\phi_{2}/2)^{M-1}M^{1/2}(1+\alpha)}{\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}\sum_{m}\|\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\ast}\|_{2},

\displaystyle\text{if }R>1.

Moreover, if
\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}
are updated from Lemma
S16
,
\omega_{k}^{\prime\prime}
satisfies Condition
2
. Here
\epsilon_{0}^{\prime\prime}=c^{\prime\prime}_{1}\omega_{\max}\sqrt{T\frac{\log\log(nd)}{n}}
,
\tau_{0}^{\prime\prime},\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}
are from Lemmas
2b
-
3b
,
\phi_{2}
is from Condition
1
and
c_{1}^{\prime\prime}
is one positive constant.

Lemma S18
.

Suppose Conditions
1
-
4
hold for
{\bm{\Theta}}
with
\gamma d_{\max}\leq C_{1}
for some constant
C_{1}>0
. Let
\lambda_{m}=4\epsilon_{m}+3\tau_{1}\frac{\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})}{2\sqrt{s_{2}+d_{m}}}
,
\bar{\bm{\Theta}}
satisfies Condition
2
and

\tilde{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m}=\arg\max_{{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime}}\,Q_{n}({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})-\lambda_{m}\left\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}\right\|_{1,\text{off}}.

Define
{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime\prime}=\frac{\sqrt{d_{m}}\tilde{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m}}{\|\tilde{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m}\|_{\text{F}}}
, it holds that, with probability at least
1-(8K^{2}+2K+1)/\{\log(nd)\}^{2}
,

\frac{\left\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime\prime}-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\right\|_{\text{F}}}{\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{\text{F}}}\leq\frac{16\sqrt{s_{2}+d_{m}}\epsilon_{m}}{C^{\prime}\sqrt{d_{m}}\gamma_{m}}+\frac{6\tau_{1}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})}{C^{\prime}\sqrt{d_{m}}\gamma_{m}},

and
{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime\prime}
satisfies Condition
2
. Here
\epsilon_{m}=c_{2}({d}/{d_{m}})\sqrt{\log d\cdot T/n}
,
\tau_{1},\gamma_{m}
are from Lemmas
2b
-
3b
and
c_{2},C^{\prime}
are positive constants.

Next, we proceed our proof. Recall that Algorithm
1
update all parameters sequentially following Figure
2
. Our proof for Lemma
4b
can be summarized into two steps. First, we focus on all parameters in cluster 1. This means that we bound
\|{\bm{\beta}}_{1,m}^{(1)}-{\bm{\beta}}_{1,m}^{\ast}\|_{2}
,
|\omega_{1}^{(1)}-\omega_{1}^{\ast}|
and
\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{1,m}^{(1)}-{\bm{\Omega}}_{1,m}^{\ast}\|_{\text{F}}
for
m=1,\ldots,M
. Second, we repeat the analysis in the first step for other clusters and obtain the desired result.

Step 1:
First, we update
{\bm{\beta}}_{1,m}^{(1)}
for all
m
by (
9
) and begin with
{\bm{\beta}}_{1,1}^{(1)}
. Let
{\bm{\Theta}}={\bm{\Theta}}^{\prime}={\bm{\Theta}}^{(0)}
in Lemma
S16
, we get that

\left\|{\bm{\beta}}_{1,1}^{(1)}-{\bm{\beta}}_{1,1}^{\ast}\right\|_{2}\leq\frac{16\sqrt{s_{1}}\epsilon_{0}}{C\gamma_{0}}+\frac{4\tau_{0}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}}^{(0)},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})}{C\gamma_{0}},

with probability at least
1-(2K^{2}+K+1)/\{\log(nd)\}^{2}
. Moreover, by Lemma
S16
,
\beta_{1,1}^{(1)}
is still in the initial ball of Condition
2
. Given this, let
{\bm{\Theta}}={\bm{\Theta}}^{(0)}
and
{\bm{\Theta}}^{\prime}=({\bm{\beta}}_{1,1}^{(1)},{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{1,1}}^{(0)})
, Lemma
S16
is still applicable for the update of
{\bm{\beta}}_{1,2}
. The same argument could be applied for all
{\bm{\beta}}_{1,m}
for
m=1,\ldots,M
.

Next, we update
\omega_{1}
by (
11
). Let
{\bm{\Theta}}={\bm{\Theta}}^{(0)}
and
{\bm{\Theta}}^{\prime}=({\bm{\beta}}_{1,1}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{\beta}}_{1,M}^{(1)},{\bm{\Theta}}_{-\{{\bm{\beta}}_{1,1},\cdots,{\bm{\beta}}_{1,M}\}}^{(0)})
, Lemma
S16
is applicable and we can get that

\displaystyle\frac{|\omega_{k}^{(1)}-\omega_{k}^{\ast}|}{|\omega_{k}^{\ast}|}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\epsilon_{0}^{\prime\prime}}{\omega_{k}^{\ast}\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}+\frac{2\tau_{0}^{\prime\prime}}{\omega_{k}^{\ast}\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}}^{(0)},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})+2(3\phi_{2}/2)^{M-1}\sqrt{M}\sum_{m}\|{\bm{\beta}}_{1,m}^{\prime}-{\bm{\beta}}_{1,m}^{\ast}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\epsilon_{0}^{\prime\prime}}{\omega_{k}^{\ast}\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}+\frac{2\tau_{0}^{\prime\prime}}{\omega_{k}^{\ast}\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}}^{(0)},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})+\frac{2(3\phi_{2}/2)^{M-1}M^{3/2}}{\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}\left\{\frac{16\sqrt{s_{1}}\epsilon_{0}}{C\gamma_{0}}+\frac{4\tau_{0}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})}{C\gamma_{0}}\right\},

with probability at least
1-(2K^{2}+2K+1)/\{\log(nd)\}^{2}
. Moreover, by Lemma
S16
,
\omega_{1}^{(1)}
is still in the initial ball of Condition
2
.

Finally, we update
{\bm{\Omega}}_{1,m}
for all
m
by (
12
). Similarly, we let
{\bm{\Theta}}={\bm{\Theta}}^{(0)}
and
{\bm{\Theta}}^{\prime}=({\bm{\beta}}_{1,1}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{\beta}}_{1,M}^{(1)},\omega_{1}^{(1)},{\bm{\Theta}}_{-\{{\bm{\beta}}_{1,1},\cdots,{\bm{\beta}}_{1,M},\omega_{1}\}}^{(0)})
, Lemma
S18
is applicable and we can get that

\frac{\left\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{(1)}-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\right\|_{\text{F}}}{\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{\text{F}}}\leq\frac{16\sqrt{s_{2}+d_{m}}\epsilon_{m}}{C^{\prime}\sqrt{d_{m}}\gamma_{m}}+\frac{6\tau_{1}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}}^{(0)},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})}{C^{\prime}\sqrt{d_{m}}\gamma_{m}}.

with probability at least
1-(8K^{2}+2K+1)/\{\log(nd)\}^{2}
.

Step 2:
We repeat the same analysis in Step 1 for other groups. We can conclude that, with probability at least
1-(2K^{2}+K+1)/\{\log(nd)\}^{2}
that

\left\|{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{(1)}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\right\|_{2}\leq\frac{16\sqrt{s_{1}}\epsilon_{0}}{C\gamma_{0}}+\frac{4\tau_{0}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}}^{(0)},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})}{C\gamma_{0}},

with probability at least
1-(2K^{2}+2K+1)/\{\log(nd)\}^{2}
that

\frac{|\omega_{k}^{(1)}-\omega_{k}^{\ast}|}{|\omega_{k}^{\ast}|}\leq\frac{2\epsilon_{0}^{\prime\prime}}{\omega_{k}^{\ast}\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}+\frac{2\tau_{0}^{\prime\prime}}{\omega_{k}^{\ast}\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}}^{(0)},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})+\frac{2(3\phi_{2}/2)^{M-1}M^{3/2}}{\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}\left\{\frac{16\sqrt{s_{1}}\epsilon_{0}}{C\gamma_{0}}+\frac{4\tau_{0}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})}{C\gamma_{0}}\right\}

with probability at least
1-(8K^{2}+2K+1)/\{\log(nd)\}^{2}
that

\frac{\left\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{(1)}-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\right\|_{\text{F}}}{\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{\text{F}}}\leq\frac{16\sqrt{s_{2}+d_{m}}\epsilon_{m}}{C^{\prime}\sqrt{d_{m}}\gamma_{m}}+\frac{6\tau_{1}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}}^{(0)},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})}{C^{\prime}\sqrt{d_{m}}\gamma_{m}}.

Recall that

\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})=\max\limits_{k,r,m}\left\{\|{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\ast}\|_{2},\frac{|\omega_{k,r}-\omega_{k,r}^{\ast}|}{|\omega_{k,r}^{\ast}|},\frac{\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{\text{F}}}{\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{\text{F}}}\right\}.

Thus, with probability at least
1-C_{3}/\{\log(nd)\}^{2}
for some constant
C_{3}>0
, it holds that

\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}}^{(1)},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})\leq\epsilon+\rho\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}}^{(0)},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast}),

(A82)

where

\epsilon=\max\left\{\frac{16\sqrt{s_{1}}\epsilon_{0}}{C\gamma_{0}},\frac{2\epsilon_{0}^{\prime\prime}}{\omega_{k}^{\ast}\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}+\frac{2(3\phi_{2}/2)^{M-1}M^{3/2}}{\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}\frac{16\sqrt{s_{1}}\epsilon_{0}}{C\gamma_{0}},\frac{16\sqrt{s_{2}+d_{m}}\epsilon_{m}}{C^{\prime}\sqrt{d_{m}}\gamma_{m}}\right\}

and

\rho=\max\left\{\frac{4\tau_{0}}{C\gamma_{0}},\frac{2\tau_{0}^{\prime\prime}}{\omega_{k}^{\ast}\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}+\frac{2(3\phi_{2}/2)^{M-1}M^{3/2}}{\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}\frac{4\tau_{0}}{C\gamma_{0}},\frac{6\tau_{1}}{C^{\prime}\sqrt{d_{m}}\gamma_{m}},\frac{6\tau_{1}}{C^{\prime}\phi_{1}\gamma_{m}}\right\}.

Following the discussions in Step 2, there exists a constant
C_{1}>0
such that
\rho\leq\frac{1}{3}
when
\gamma\leq{C_{1}}/{d_{\max}}
. By (
A133
), (
A139
), (
A148
), there exists one positive constant
C_{2}
such that

\epsilon\leq C_{2}\left\{\frac{1}{\omega_{\min}}\sqrt{T\frac{s_{1}\log d}{n}}+\max_{m}\sqrt{\frac{(s_{2}+d_{m})\log d\cdot T}{nd_{m}}}\right\}.

(A83)

Moreover, under Condition
4
, we have
\epsilon\leq\frac{2\alpha}{3}
, which gives that
\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}}^{(1)},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})\leq\alpha
.

D5
Proof of Lemma
5b

In this proof, we show the strong concavity with respect to
{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}
for a general rank.
First, we introduce the first- and second-order derivatives of
Q_{n/T}({\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})
with respect to
{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}
.

First-order:

\displaystyle\nabla_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}}Q_{n/T}({\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime},\bar{{\bm{\Theta}}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})

(A84)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{T}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n/T}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}\left\{\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}-\bar{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{k,-r}\right)_{(m)}-\bar{\omega}_{k}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r^{\prime},m^{\prime}})^{\top}\right\}\Big(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\Big)\bm{a}_{k,r,m},

Second-order:

\nabla_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}}^{2}Q_{n/T}({\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime},\bar{{\bm{\Theta}}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})=-\frac{T}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n/T}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\left\{\bm{a}_{k,r,m}^{\top}\Big(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\Big)\bm{a}_{k,r,m}\right\}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m},

(A85)

where
\bar{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{k,-r}=\sum_{r^{\prime}\neq r}\bar{\omega}_{k}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r^{\prime},1}\circ\cdot\circ\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r^{\prime},M}
and
\bm{a}_{k,r,m}=\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{R}\xi_{k,m,r^{\prime}r}\bar{\omega}_{k,r^{\prime}}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r^{\prime},m^{\prime}})
with
\xi_{k,m,r^{\prime}r}=\left\langle\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r^{\prime},m},\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}\right\rangle
.

Expand
Q_{n/T}({\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime\prime},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})
around
