{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime}
using Taylor expansion, we have

\displaystyle Q_{n/T}({\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime\prime},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})

(A86)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle Q_{n/T}({\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})+\left\langle\nabla_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}}Q_{n/T}({\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}}),{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime\prime}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}({\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime\prime}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime})^{\top}\nabla^{2}Q_{n/T}(\bm{z},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})({\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime\prime}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime})

where
\bm{z}=t{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime}+(1-t){\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime\prime}
with
t\in[0,1]
. By (
A85
), we have

\nabla^{2}Q_{n/T}(\bm{z},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})=-\frac{T}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n/T}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\left\{\bm{a}_{k,r,m}^{\top}\Big(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\Big)\bm{a}_{k,r,m}\right\}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}.

By (
A51
), with probability as least
1-p_{n}
,
\frac{T}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n/T}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\geq c_{0}
.
Noting
\sigma_{\min}(\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m})\geq\phi_{1}/2
from Conditions
1
-
2
, we have

\displaystyle\left\{\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{R}\xi_{k,m,r^{\prime}r}\bar{\omega}_{k,r^{\prime}}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r^{\prime},m^{\prime}})^{\top}\right\}\Big(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\Big)\left\{\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{R}\xi_{k,m,r^{\prime}r}\bar{\omega}_{k,r^{\prime}}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r^{\prime},m^{\prime}})\right\}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle(\phi_{1}/2)^{M-1}\sum_{r_{1}=1}^{R}\sum_{r_{2}=1}^{R}\xi_{k,m,r_{1}r}\xi_{k,m,r_{2}r}\bar{\omega}_{k,r_{1}}\bar{\omega}_{k,r_{2}}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r_{1},m^{\prime}})^{\top}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r_{2},m^{\prime}})

To ease notation, we discuss
\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r_{1},m^{\prime}})^{\top}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m^{\prime}}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r_{2},m})
when
M=3
and
m=1
while general cases follow similarly.

When
r_{1}=r_{2}
, we have
\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r_{1},m^{\prime}})^{\top}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r_{2},m^{\prime}})=1
. Otherwise, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r_{1},m^{\prime}})^{\top}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r_{2},m^{\prime}})=\sum_{l_{3}=1}^{d_{3}}\left\{\left(\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r_{1},2}\right)^{\top}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r_{2},2}\right\}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r_{1},3}(l_{3})\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r_{2},3}(l_{3})

(A87)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\langle\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r_{1},2},\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r_{2},2}\right\rangle\left\langle\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r_{1},3},\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r_{2},3}\right\rangle=\prod_{m^{\prime}\neq 1}\left\langle\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r_{1},m^{\prime}},\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r_{2},m^{\prime}}\right\rangle.

By Condition
2
, we have

\displaystyle\left\langle\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r_{1},m},\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r_{2},m}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\langle{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r_{1},m}^{\ast},{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r_{2},m}^{\ast}\right\rangle+\left\langle\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r_{1},m}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r_{1},m}^{\ast},{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r_{2},m}^{\ast}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\left\langle\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r_{1},m},\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r_{2},m}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r_{2},m}^{\ast}\right\rangle+\left\langle\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r_{1},m}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r_{1},m}^{\ast},\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r_{2},m}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r_{2},m}^{\ast}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\xi+\|\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r_{1},m}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r_{1},m}^{\ast}\|_{2}+\|\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r_{2},m}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r_{2},m}^{\ast}\|_{2}+\|\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r_{1},m}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r_{1},m}^{\ast}\|_{2}\|\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r_{2},m}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r_{2},m}^{\ast}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\xi+2c_{\alpha}+c_{\alpha}^{2}.

Then it arrives at

\displaystyle\left\{\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{R}\xi_{k,m,r^{\prime}r}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r^{\prime},m^{\prime}})^{\top}\right\}\Big(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\Big)\left\{\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{R}\xi_{k,m,r^{\prime}r}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r^{\prime},m^{\prime}})\right\}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle(\phi_{1}/2)^{M-1}\left\{\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{R}(\xi_{k,r^{\prime}r^{\prime},m})^{2}\bar{\omega}_{k,r^{\prime}}^{2}-\sum_{r_{1}\neq r_{2}}\xi_{k,r_{1}r,m}\xi_{k,r_{2}r,m}\bar{\omega}_{k,r_{1}}\bar{\omega}_{k,r_{2}}\prod_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\left\langle\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r_{1},m},\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r_{2},m}\right\rangle\right\}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle(\phi_{1}/2)^{M-1}\left\{(1-c_{\alpha})^{2}\omega_{\min}^{2}-(1+c_{\alpha})^{2}\omega_{\max}^{2}(R-1)R(\xi+2c_{\alpha}+c_{\alpha}^{2})^{M+1}\right\}.

With
\gamma_{0}^{\prime}=c_{0}(\phi_{1}/2)^{M-1}\left\{(1-c_{\alpha})^{2}\omega_{\min}^{2}-(1+c_{\alpha})^{2}\omega_{\max}^{2}(R-1)R(\xi+2c_{\alpha}+c_{\alpha}^{2})^{M+1}\right\}
, it arrives at that

\displaystyle Q_{\frac{n}{T}}({\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime\prime},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})-Q_{\frac{n}{T}}({\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})

\displaystyle-\left\langle\nabla_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}}Q_{\frac{n}{T}}({\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}}),{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime\prime}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime}\right\rangle\leq-\frac{\gamma_{0}^{\prime}}{2}\left\|{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime\prime}\right\|^{2},

with probability at least
1-1/\{\log(nd)\}^{2}
.

D6
Proof of Lemma
6b

In this proof, we establish the gradient stability for
{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}
.
Recalling (
A84
) and we have

\displaystyle\nabla_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}}Q({\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}\left\{\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}-\bar{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{k,-r}\right)_{(m)}-\bar{\omega}_{k}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r^{\prime},m^{\prime}})^{\top}\right\}\Big(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\Big)\bm{a}_{k,r,m}\right],

where
\bm{a}_{k,r,m}=\sum_{r^{\prime}}\bar{\omega}_{k,r^{\prime}}\xi_{k,m,r^{\prime}r}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r^{\prime},m^{\prime}})
.

First, we expand
\nabla_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}}Q({\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})-\nabla_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}}Q({\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})
as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[D_{\tau}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast}){\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}\left\{\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}-\bar{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{k,-r}\right)_{(m)}-\bar{\omega}_{k}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r^{\prime},m^{\prime}})^{\top}\right\}\Big(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\Big)\bm{a}_{k,r,m}\right],

where
D_{\tau}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})=\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})-\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})
.
By the definition of
\tau_{0}
, we can obtain that

\displaystyle\left\lVert\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}\left\{\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}-\bar{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{k,-r}\right)_{(m)}-\bar{\omega}_{k}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r^{\prime},m^{\prime}})^{\top}\right\}\Big(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\Big)\bm{a}_{k,r,m}(\nabla_{\check{{\bm{\Theta}}}^{\delta}}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}))^{\top}\right]\right\rVert_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\{1+(R-1)(\xi+2c_{\alpha}+c_{\alpha}^{2})\}^{2}\tau_{0},

where the inequality holds due to
\left|\xi_{k,m,r^{\prime}r}\right|\leq\xi+2c_{\alpha}+c_{\alpha}^{2}
.
Correspondingly, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert\nabla_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}}Q({\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})-\nabla_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}}Q({\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})\right\rVert_{2}^{2}

(A88)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\{1+(R-1)(\xi+c_{\alpha}+c_{\alpha}^{2})\}^{2}\tau_{0}^{2}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})\leq\tau_{0}^{\prime 2}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast}),

where
\tau_{0}^{\prime}=\{1+(R-1)(\xi+2c_{\alpha}+c_{\alpha}^{2})\}\tau_{0}
.

D7
Proof of Lemma
7b

Similar as in Lemma
3b
, define

h_{{\bm{\Theta}},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}({\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime})=\nabla_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}}Q_{n/T}({\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})-\nabla_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}}Q({\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}}).

Based on the definition of dual norm
\mathcal{P}_{1}^{\ast}
, we have that

\left\|\nabla_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}}Q_{n/T}({\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})-\nabla_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}}Q({\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})\right\|_{\mathcal{P}_{1}^{\ast}}\leq\max_{k}\left\|h_{{\bm{\Theta}}}({\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime})\right\|_{\infty},

(A89)

Recalling (
A84
) and we have

\displaystyle h_{{\bm{\Theta}},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}({\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{T}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n/T}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}\left\{\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}-\bar{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{k,-r}\right)_{(m)}-\bar{\omega}_{k}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r^{\prime},m^{\prime}})^{\top}\right\}\Big(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\Big)\bm{a}_{k,r,m}

\displaystyle-\mathbb{E}\left[\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}\left\{\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}-\bar{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{k,-r}\right)_{(m)}-\bar{\omega}_{k}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r^{\prime},m^{\prime}})^{\top}\right\}\Big(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\Big)\bm{a}_{k,r,m}\right].

By the triangle inequality,
h_{{\bm{\Theta}},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}({\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime})
can be bounded as

\begin{split}&h_{{\bm{\Theta}},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}({\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime})\leq\left\|\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}\right\|_{\max}\text{I}\left\|\Big(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\Big)\bm{a}_{k,r,m}\right\|_{\infty}\\
&{+\text{II}\left\|\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}\left\{\left(\bar{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{k,-r}\right)_{(m)}+\bar{\omega}_{k}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r^{\prime},m^{\prime}})^{\top}\right\}\Big(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\Big)\bm{a}_{k,r,m}\right\|_{\infty}},\end{split}

where
\text{I}=\left\|\frac{T}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n/T}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\right)_{(m)}-\mathbb{E}\left\{\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\right)_{(m)}\right\}\right\|_{\max}
and
\text{II}=\|\frac{T}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n/T}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})-\mathbb{E}(\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}))\|_{\infty}
.
By (
A73
), we have

\text{I}\leq\sqrt{{4}/{D_{0}}}\varphi_{K0}\sqrt{\frac{\log(e/p_{n})+\log d}{n/T}},

with probability at least
1-2Kp_{n}
. Applying the result in (
A74
) to
II
, we have

\left\lvert\frac{T}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n/T}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})-\mathbb{E}(\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}))\right\rvert\leq\sqrt{\frac{1}{2}\log({2}/{p_{n}})T/n},

with probability at least
1-p_{n}
.

Note that the bound for
I
is
O_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log(d)T}{n}}\right)
while the bound from
II
is
O_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log(2/p_{n})T}{n}}\right)
, thus

\displaystyle h_{{\bm{\Theta}},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}}({\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime})

\displaystyle\precsim\text{I}\times\left\|\Big(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\Big)\bm{a}_{k,r,m}\right\|_{\infty}

(A90)

\displaystyle\precsim\text{I}\times\left\lVert\Big(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\Big)\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{R}\xi_{k,m,r^{\prime}r}\bar{\omega}_{k,r^{\prime}}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r^{\prime},m^{\prime}})\right\rVert_{\infty}

with probability at least
1-(2K+1)p_{n}
.
By (
A68
) and
\|\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}\|_{2}\leq 3\phi_{2}/2
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert\Big(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\Big)\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{R}\xi_{k,m,r^{\prime}r}^{\ast}\bar{\omega}_{k,r^{\prime}}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r^{\prime},m^{\prime}})\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{R}\left|\xi_{k,m,r^{\prime}r}\right|\bar{\omega}_{k,r}(3\phi_{2}/2)^{M-1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1+c_{\alpha})\omega_{\max}(3\phi_{2}/2)^{M-1}\left\{1+(R-1)(\xi+2c_{\alpha}+c_{\alpha}^{2})\right\}.

Therefore, there exist some constant
c^{\prime}_{1}>0
such that

\max_{k}\text{I}\leq c^{\prime}_{1}\omega_{\max}\sqrt{\frac{T\log d}{n}},

with probability at least
1-K(2K+1)/\{\log(nd)\}^{2}
.

D8
Proof of Lemma
8b

In this proof, given
{\bm{\Theta}}^{(0)}
, we in turn bound
\|{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{(1)}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\ast}\|_{2}
,
|\omega_{k,r}^{(1)}-\omega_{k,r}^{\ast}|
and
\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{(1)}-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{\text{F}}
using results from Lemmas
S16
-
S18
. Recall that Algorithm
1
update all parameters sequentially following Figure
2
. Similar as in Lemma
4b
, this proof can be summarized into two steps. First, we focus on all parameters in subgroup 1. This means that we bound
\|{\bm{\beta}}_{1,r,m}^{(1)}-{\bm{\beta}}_{1,r,m}^{\ast}\|_{2}
,
|\omega_{1,r}^{(1)}-\omega_{1,r}^{\ast}|
and
\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{1,m}^{(1)}-{\bm{\Omega}}_{1,m}^{\ast}\|_{\text{F}}

for
m=1,\ldots,M
. Second, we repeat the analysis in the first step and obtain the desired result.

Step 1:
First, we focus on the analysis of
r=1
. We update
{\bm{\beta}}_{1,1,m}^{(1)}
for all
m
by (
9
) and begin with
{\bm{\beta}}_{1,1,1}^{(1)}
. Let
{\bm{\Theta}}={\bm{\Theta}}^{\prime}={\bm{\Theta}}^{(0)}
in Lemma
S16
, we get that

\left\|{\bm{\beta}}_{1,1,1}^{(1)}-{\bm{\beta}}_{1,1,1}^{\ast}\right\|_{2}\leq\frac{16\sqrt{s_{1}}\epsilon_{R,0}}{C\gamma_{0}^{\prime}}+\frac{4\tau_{0}^{\prime}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}}^{(0)},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})}{C\gamma_{0}^{\prime}},

with probability at least
1-(2K^{2}+K+1)/\{\log(nd)\}^{2}
. Moreover, by Lemma
S16
,
\beta_{1,1,1}^{(1)}
is still in the initial ball of Condition
2
. Given this, let
{\bm{\Theta}}={\bm{\Theta}}^{(0)}
and
{\bm{\Theta}}^{\prime}=({\bm{\beta}}_{1,1,1}^{(1)},{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{1,1,1}}^{(0)})
, Lemma
S16
is still applicable for the update of
{\bm{\beta}}_{1,1,2}
. The same argument could be applied for all
{\bm{\beta}}_{1,1,m}
for
m=1,\ldots,M
.

Next, we update
\omega_{1,1}
by (
11
). Let
{\bm{\Theta}}={\bm{\Theta}}^{(0)}
and
{\bm{\Theta}}^{\prime}=({\bm{\beta}}_{1,1,1}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{\beta}}_{1,1,M}^{(1)},{\bm{\Theta}}_{-\{{\bm{\beta}}_{1,1,1},\cdots,{\bm{\beta}}_{1,1,M}\}}^{(0)})
, Lemma
S16
is applicable and we can get that

\displaystyle\frac{|\omega_{1,1}^{(1)}-\omega_{1,1}^{\ast}|}{|\omega_{1,1}^{\ast}|}\leq\frac{2\epsilon_{0}^{\prime\prime}}{\omega_{1,1}^{\ast}\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}+\frac{2(\tau_{0}^{\prime\prime}+\tau_{0}^{\prime\prime\prime})}{\omega_{k,r}^{\ast}\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}\alpha+\frac{2R(3\phi_{2}/2)^{M-1}M^{3/2}(1+\alpha)}{\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}\sum_{m}\|{\bm{\beta}}_{1,1,m}^{(1)}-{\bm{\beta}}_{1,1,m}^{\ast}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2\epsilon_{0}^{\prime\prime}}{\omega_{k,r}^{\ast}\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}+\frac{2(\tau_{0}^{\prime\prime}+\tau_{0}^{\prime\prime\prime})}{\omega_{k,r}^{\ast}\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}\alpha+\frac{2R(3\phi_{2}/2)^{M-1}M^{3/2}(1+\alpha)}{\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}\left\{\frac{16\sqrt{s_{1}}\epsilon_{R,0}}{C\gamma_{0}^{\prime}}+\frac{4\tau_{0}^{\prime}\alpha}{C\gamma_{0}^{\prime}}\right\},

with probability at least
1-(2K^{2}+2K+1)/\{\log(nd)\}^{2}
. Moreover, by Lemma
S16
,
\omega_{1,1}^{(1)}
is still in the initial ball of Condition
2
. These analysis can be directly applied to
r=2,\ldots,R
and the same conclusion can be obtained for
\|{\bm{\beta}}_{1,r,m}^{(1)}-{\bm{\beta}}_{1,r,m}^{\ast}\|_{2}
and
|\omega_{1,r}^{(1)}-\omega_{1,r}^{\ast}|
.

Finally, we update
{\bm{\Omega}}_{1,m}
for all
m
by (
12
). Similarly, we let
{\bm{\Theta}}={\bm{\Theta}}^{(0)}
and
{\bm{\Theta}}^{\prime}=({\bm{\beta}}_{1,1}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{\beta}}_{1,M}^{(1)},\omega_{1}^{(1)},{\bm{\Theta}}_{-\{{\bm{\beta}}_{1,1},\cdots,{\bm{\beta}}_{1,M},\omega_{1}\}}^{(0)})
, Lemma
S18
is applicable and we can get that

\frac{\left\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{(1)}-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\right\|_{\text{F}}}{\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{\text{F}}}\leq\frac{16\sqrt{s_{2}+d_{m}}\epsilon_{m}}{C^{\prime}\sqrt{d_{m}}\gamma_{m}}+\frac{6\tau_{1}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}}^{(0)},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})}{C^{\prime}\sqrt{d_{m}}\gamma_{m}}.

with probability at least
1-(8K^{2}+2K+1)/\{\log(nd)\}^{2}
.

Step 2:
We repeat the same analysis in Step 1 for other groups. We can conclude that, with probability at least
1-C^{\prime}_{3}/\{\log(nd)\}^{2}
for some constant
C^{\prime}_{3}>0
, it holds that

\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}}^{(1)},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})\leq\epsilon^{\prime}+\rho_{R}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}}^{(0)},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast}),

(A91)

where

\epsilon^{\prime}=\max\left\{\frac{16\sqrt{s_{1}}\epsilon_{R,0}}{C\gamma_{0}^{\prime}},\frac{2\epsilon_{0}^{\prime\prime}}{\omega_{k}^{\ast}\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}+\frac{2R(3\phi_{2}/2)^{M-1}M^{3/2}}{\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}\frac{16\sqrt{s_{1}}\epsilon_{R,0}}{C\gamma_{0}^{\prime}},\frac{16\sqrt{s_{2}+d_{m}}\epsilon_{m}}{C^{\prime}\sqrt{d_{m}}\gamma_{m}}\right\}

and

\rho_{R}=\max\left\{\frac{4\tau_{0}^{\prime}}{C\gamma_{0}^{\prime}},\frac{2(\tau_{0}^{\prime\prime}+\tau_{0}^{\prime\prime\prime})}{\omega_{k}^{\ast}\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}+\frac{2R(3\phi_{2}/2)^{M-1}M^{3/2}}{\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}\frac{4\tau_{0}^{\prime}}{C\gamma_{0}^{\prime}},\frac{6\tau_{1}}{C^{\prime}\sqrt{d_{m}}\gamma_{m}},\frac{6\tau_{1}}{C^{\prime}\phi_{1}\gamma_{m}}\right\}.

From the discussion in Step 2 of Lemma
4b
-
8b
, when
\gamma\leq{C_{1}}/{d_{\max}}
, we have
\rho_{R}\leq\frac{1}{2}
. From the definition of
\rho_{R}
, it is easy to know that
\rho_{R}\geq\rho
. By (
A134
), (
A141
), (
A148
), there exists some constant
C_{2}^{\prime}>0
such that

\epsilon^{\prime}\leq C_{2}^{\prime}\left\{\frac{1}{\omega_{\min}}\sqrt{T\frac{s_{1}\log d}{n}}+\max_{m}\sqrt{\frac{(s_{2}+d_{m})\log d\cdot T}{nd_{m}}}\right\}.

(A92)

D9
Proof of Lemma
9b

In this proof, we mainly focus on the bound of
|\mathbb{E}[\omega^{(t+1)}]
. Since
\nabla_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}Q({\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast}|{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})=\mathbb{E}[\tau({\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}^{\ast})-(1-\tau({\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast}))(\bm{\mathcal{X}}+\bm{\mathcal{U}}^{\ast})]=0
, we can get that

\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{2\tau({\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})-1\right\}\bm{\mathcal{X}}\right]=\bm{\mathcal{U}}^{\ast}={\bf 0}.\\

(A93)

Let
{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast}=\left({{\bm{\beta}}_{1}^{(t)}}^{\top},{{\bm{\beta}}_{2}^{(t)}}^{\top},{{\bm{\beta}}_{3}^{(t)}}^{\top},0\right)^{\top}
. With (
28
) and (
A93
), we get that

\displaystyle|\mathbb{E}[\omega^{(t+1)}]|

(A94)

\displaystyle=\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{2\tau({\bm{\Theta}}^{(t)})-1\right\}\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{\top}-(2\tau({\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})-1)\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{\top}\right]\mathrm{vec}({\bm{\beta}}_{1}^{(t+1)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}^{(t+1)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3}^{(t+1)})\right|_{2}

\displaystyle=2\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{\tau({\bm{\Theta}}^{(t)})-\tau({\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})\right\}\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{\top}\right]\mathrm{vec}({\bm{\beta}}_{1}^{(t+1)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}^{(t+1)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3}^{(t+1)})\right|_{2}

\displaystyle=2\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{\top}\mathrm{vec}({\bm{\beta}}_{1}^{(t+1)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}^{(t+1)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3}^{(t+1)})\int_{0}^{1}\nabla\tau({\bm{\Theta}}_{u})^{\top}({\bm{\Theta}}-{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})\mathrm{d}u\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq 2\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{1}\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{\top}\mathrm{vec}({\bm{\beta}}_{1}^{(t+1)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}^{(t+1)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3}^{(t+1)})\nabla_{\omega}\tau({\bm{\Theta}}_{u})\mathrm{d}u\right]\right||\omega-\omega^{\ast}|

\displaystyle=2\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{1}\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{\top}\mathrm{vec}({\bm{\beta}}_{1}^{(t+1)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}^{(t+1)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3}^{(t+1)})\nabla_{\omega}\tau({\bm{\Theta}}_{u})\mathrm{d}u\right]\right||\omega|.

The last inequality follows the fact that
|ab|\leq|a||b|
and
{\bm{\beta}}_{m}-{\bm{\beta}}_{m}^{\ast}={\bf 0}
. The third equality is the direct result of Taylor series expansion. Applying Taylor’s theorem for
\tau({\bm{\Theta}}^{(t)})
at
{\bm{\beta}}^{\ast}
, we have

\tau({\bm{\Theta}}^{(t)})-\tau({\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})=\nabla\tau({\bm{\Theta}}_{u})\Delta,

(A95)

where
\nabla\tau({\bm{\Theta}})=(\nabla_{{\bm{\beta}}_{1}}\tau({\bm{\Theta}})^{\top},\nabla_{{\bm{\beta}}_{2}}\tau({\bm{\Theta}})^{\top},\nabla_{{\bm{\beta}}_{3}}\tau({\bm{\Theta}})^{\top},\nabla_{\omega}\tau({\bm{\Theta}})^{\top})\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{1}+d_{2}+d_{3}+1}
,
\Delta={\bm{\Theta}}-{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast}
and
{\bm{\Theta}}_{u}={\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast}+u\Delta
. Here
\tau({\bm{\Theta}})=\frac{\pi\exp\left(-\frac{\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}-\omega{\bm{\beta}}_{1}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3}\|_{F}^{2}}{2\delta^{2}}\right)}{\pi\exp\left(-\frac{\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}-\omega{\bm{\beta}}_{1}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3}\|_{F}^{2}}{2\delta^{2}}\right)+(1-\pi)\exp\left(-\frac{\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}+\omega{\bm{\beta}}_{1}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3}\|_{F}^{2}}{2\delta^{2}}\right)}
and
\nabla_{\omega}\tau({\bm{\Theta}})
is expressed as

\displaystyle\nabla_{\omega}\tau({\bm{\Theta}})=\frac{2\pi(1-\pi)\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{\top}\mathrm{vec}({\bm{\beta}}_{1}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3})}{\sigma^{2}\left\{\pi\exp\left(-\frac{\omega\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{\top}\mathrm{vec}({\bm{\beta}}_{1}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3})}{\delta^{2}}\right)+(1-\pi)\exp\left(\frac{\omega\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{\top}\mathrm{vec}({\bm{\beta}}_{1}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3})}{\delta^{2}}\right)\right\}^{2}}.

(A96)

The next lemma is useful in the final step of this proof, and its proof is shown in Section
E11
.

Lemma S19
.

Let
\Gamma_{u}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})=\mathbb{E}[\int_{0}^{1}\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{\top}\mathrm{vec}(\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{1}\circ\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{2}\circ\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{3})\nabla_{\omega}\tau({\bm{\Theta}}_{u})\mathrm{d}u]
,
\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})\sim\mathcal{N}({\bf 0},\sigma^{2}{\bf I})
and
{\bm{\Theta}}_{u}=({\bf 0}_{d_{1}}^{\top},{\bf 0}_{d_{2}}^{\top},{\bf 0}_{d_{3}}^{\top},u\omega)^{\top}
. Given any
\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{m}
satisfying
\|\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{m}\|_{2}=1
, if
\pi=\frac{1}{2}
, it holds that

\left|\Gamma_{u}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})\right|\leq\frac{\gamma_{p}(\omega)}{2}

(A97)

where
\gamma_{p}(\omega)=p+\frac{1-p}{1+\omega^{2}/(2\sigma^{2})}
and
p=\frac{1}{2}(1+\mathbb{P}_{Z\sim N(0,1)}(|Z|\leq 1))\leq 1
. If
\pi=\frac{1-\rho}{2}\neq\frac{1}{2}
with
\rho\in(0,1)
, it holds that

\left|\Gamma_{u}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})\right|\leq\frac{\gamma}{2}.

(A98)

where
\gamma=1-\rho^{2}/2
.

By Lemma
S19
, if
\pi=\frac{1}{2}
, we get that,

|\mathbb{E}[\omega^{(t+1)}]|\leq 2\cdot\frac{\gamma_{p}(\omega^{(t)})}{2}|\omega^{(t)}|=\gamma_{p}(\omega^{(t)})|\omega^{(t)}|,

(A99)

where
\gamma_{p}(\omega)=p+\frac{1-p}{1+\omega^{2}/(2\sigma^{2})}
. If
\pi=\frac{1-\rho}{2}\neq\frac{1}{2}
with
\rho\in(0,1)
, we get that,

|\mathbb{E}[\omega^{(t+1)}]|\leq 2\cdot\frac{\gamma}{2}|\omega^{(t)}|=(1-\rho^{2}/2)|\omega^{(t)}|.

(A100)

D10
Proof of Lemma
10b

Recall that

\omega^{(t+1)}=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{2\tau_{i}({\bm{\Theta}}^{(t)})-1\right\}\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i})^{\top}\mathrm{vec}({\bm{\beta}}_{1}^{(t+1)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}^{(t+1)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3}^{(t+1)}),

and

\mathbb{E}[\omega^{(t+1)}]=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{2\tau({\bm{\Theta}}^{(t)})-1\right\}\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{\top}\right]\mathrm{vec}({\bm{\beta}}_{1}^{(t+1)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}^{(t+1)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3}^{(t+1)}).

Let
\bm{b}=\mathrm{vec}({\bm{\beta}}_{1}^{(t+1)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}^{(t+1)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3}^{(t+1)})
, we have that

\displaystyle|\omega^{(t+1)}-\mathbb{E}[\omega^{(t+1)}]|\leq

\displaystyle\underbrace{\left|\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{\tau_{i}({\bm{\Theta}}^{(t)})-\pi\right\}\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i})^{\top}\bm{b}-\mathbb{E}\left[2\left\{\tau({\bm{\Theta}}^{(t)})-\pi\right\}\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{\top}\right]\bm{b}\right|}_{I_{1}}

(A101)

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i})^{\top}\bm{b}-\mathbb{E}\left[\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{\top}\bm{b}\right]\right|\cdot|2\pi-1|}_{I_{2}}.

Noting that
\|{\bm{\beta}}_{m}^{(t+1)}\|_{2}=1
, we can get that
\|\mathrm{vec}({\bm{\beta}}_{1}^{(t+1)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}^{(t+1)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3}^{(t+1)})\|_{2}=1
. Additionally, we know that
\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i})\sim\mathcal{N}({\bf 0},\sigma^{2}{\bf I})
. Combining them together, it arrives that
\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i})^{\top}\mathrm{vec}({\bm{\beta}}_{1}^{(t+1)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}^{(t+1)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3}^{(t+1)})\sim\mathcal{N}(0,\sigma^{2})
. Then the standard concentration bounds yield that

\mathbb{P}\left(|I_{2}|\leq|2\pi-1|\sigma\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)}{n}}\right)\geq 1-\delta.

(A102)

Next, we need to bound
|I_{1}|
. Let
\{\epsilon_{i}\}_{i=1}^{n}
denote an i.i.d. sequence of Rademacher variables which are independent of
\{\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\}_{i=1}^{n}
, for any
\lambda>0
, we have

\mathbb{E}[e^{\lambda I_{1}}]\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(\frac{\lambda}{n}\sup_{|\omega^{(t)}|\leq r}\sum_{i=1}^{n}2\epsilon_{i}(\tau_{i}({\bm{\Theta}}^{(t)})-\pi)\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i})^{\top}\mathrm{vec}({\bm{\beta}}_{1}^{(t+1)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}^{(t+1)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3}^{(t+1)})\right)\right]

using a standard symmetrization result for empirical processes
(
Koltchinskii 2011
;
Ledoux and Talagrand 1991
)
. Note that

\displaystyle\tau({\bm{\Theta}})

\displaystyle=\frac{\pi\exp\left(-\frac{\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}-\omega{\bm{\beta}}_{1}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3})^{\top}\|_{F}^{2}}{2\sigma^{2}}\right)}{\pi\exp\left(-\frac{\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}-\omega{\bm{\beta}}_{1}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3}\|_{F}^{2}}{2\sigma^{2}}\right)+(1-\pi)\exp\left(-\frac{\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}+\omega{\bm{\beta}}_{1}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3}\|_{F}^{2}}{2\sigma^{2}}\right)}

\displaystyle=\frac{\pi}{\pi+(1-\pi)\exp\left(-\frac{2\langle\bm{\mathcal{X}},\omega{\bm{\beta}}_{1}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3}\rangle}{\sigma^{2}}\right)}.

Let
\psi(x)=\frac{\pi}{\pi+(1-\pi)\exp(x)}-\pi
, it is easy to verify
\psi(x)
is Lipschitz and
\psi(0)=0
. Now Lemma
S8
is applicable, we get that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(\frac{\lambda}{n}\sup_{|\omega^{(t)}|\leq r}\sum_{i=1}^{n}2\epsilon_{i}(\tau_{i}({\bm{\Theta}}^{(t)})-\pi)\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i})^{\top}\mathrm{vec}({\bm{\beta}}_{1}^{(t+1)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}^{(t+1)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3}^{(t+1)})\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(\left|\frac{4\lambda}{n}\sup_{|\omega^{(t)}|\leq r}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\epsilon_{i}\langle\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i},\omega^{(t)}{\bm{\beta}}_{1}^{(t)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}^{(t)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3}^{(t)}\rangle\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i})^{\top}\mathrm{vec}({\bm{\beta}}_{1}^{(t+1)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}^{(t+1)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3}^{(t+1)})\right|\right)\right].

Using the fact that
\|\mathrm{vec}({\bm{\beta}}_{1}^{(t)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}^{(t)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3}^{(t)})\|_{2}=1
,
\|\mathrm{vec}({\bm{\beta}}_{1}^{(t+1)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}^{(t+1)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3}^{(t+1)})\|_{2}=1
and the standard bound
\bm{u}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{v}\leq\|\bm{u}\|_{2}\|\bm{B}\|_{op}\|\bm{v}\|_{2}
, we obtain that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(\left|\frac{4\lambda}{n}\sup_{|\omega|\leq r}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\epsilon_{i}\langle\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i},\omega^{(t)}{\bm{\beta}}_{1}^{(t)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}^{(t)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3}^{(t)}\rangle\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i})^{\top}\mathrm{vec}({\bm{\beta}}_{1}^{(t+1)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}^{(t+1)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3}^{(t+1)})\right|\right)\right]

(A103)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(4\lambda r\left\|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\epsilon_{i}\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i})\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i})^{\top}\right\|_{op}\right)\right].

Let
\mathbb{S}^{d_{m}}=\{u_{m}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{m}}|\|u_{m}\|_{2}=1\}
denote the unit sphere in
d_{m}
-dimensions. Since
\bm{\mathcal{X}}
has low rank structure, we have that

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\epsilon_{i}\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i})\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i})^{\top}\right\|_{op}

\displaystyle=\sup_{u_{1},u_{2},u_{3}}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\epsilon_{i}\langle\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i})^{\top}\mathrm{vec}(u_{1}\circ u_{2}\circ u_{3})\rangle^{2}\right|

\displaystyle=\sup_{u_{1},u_{2},u_{3}}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\epsilon_{i}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\times_{1}u_{1}\times_{2}u_{2}\times_{3}u_{3})^{2}\right|.

Using a standard discretization argument, we reduce our problem to a maximum over a finite cover. In particular, we denote
\{u_{m}^{1},\ldots,u_{m}^{N_{m}}\}
a
1/8
-cover for the unit sphere
\mathbb{S}^{d_{m}}
. It is well known that we can find a set with
N_{m}\leq 17^{d_{m}}

(
Wainwright 2019
)
. Let
u^{\ast}=\{u_{1}^{\ast},u_{2}^{\ast},u_{3}^{\ast}\}
and
a_{u}=\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\epsilon_{i}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\times_{1}u_{1}\times_{2}u_{2}\times_{3}u_{3})^{2}\right|
, there exist
u_{j}=\{u_{1}^{j_{1}},u_{2}^{j_{2}},u_{3}^{j_{3}}\}
such that
\|u_{m}^{j_{m}}-u_{m}^{\ast}\|\leq\frac{1}{8}
. This implies that

a_{u^{\ast}}\leq a_{u_{j}}+|a_{u}-a_{u^{\ast}}|\leq\max_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3}}a_{u_{j}}+\left(\frac{1}{8}\cdot 3+\frac{1}{8^{2}}\cdot 3+\frac{1}{8^{3}}\cdot 1\right)a_{u^{\ast}}.

Since
\frac{1}{8}\cdot 3+\frac{1}{8^{2}}\cdot 3+\frac{1}{8^{3}}\cdot 1<\frac{1}{2}
, we get that

\sup_{u_{1},u_{2},u_{3}}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\epsilon_{i}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\times_{1}u_{1}\times_{2}u_{2}\times_{3}u_{3})^{2}\right|\leq\max_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3}}2\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\epsilon_{i}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\times_{1}u_{1}^{j_{1}}\times_{2}u_{2}^{j_{2}}\times_{3}u_{3}^{j_{3}})^{2}\right|.

(A104)

Putting (
A104
) into (
A103
), it arrives that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(4\lambda r\left\|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\epsilon_{i}\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i})\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i})^{\top}\right\|_{op}\right)\right]

(A105)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(8\lambda r\max_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3}}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\epsilon_{i}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\times_{1}u_{1}^{j_{1}}\times_{2}u_{2}^{j_{2}}\times_{3}u_{3}^{j_{3}})^{2}\right|\right)\right].

Since
\|u_{m}\|_{2}=1
and
\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i})\sim\mathcal{N}(0,\sigma^{2}I_{d})
, we have that
\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\times_{1}u_{1}^{j_{1}}\times_{2}u_{2}^{j_{2}}\times_{3}u_{3}^{j_{3}}\overset{i.i.d.}{\sim}\mathcal{N}(0,\sigma^{2})
. Following the fact that square of a sub-Gaussian random variable with parameter
\sigma
is a sub-exponential random variable with parameter
(4\sigma^{2},4\sigma^{2})
, we obtain the following inequality
(
Vershynin 2018
)
:

\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(t(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\times_{1}u_{1}^{j_{1}}\times_{2}u_{2}^{j_{2}}\times_{3}u_{3}^{j_{3}})^{2}-t\mathbb{E}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\times_{1}u_{1}^{j_{1}}\times_{2}u_{2}^{j_{2}}\times_{3}u_{3}^{j_{3}})^{2}\right)\right]\leq e^{16t^{2}\sigma^{4}}

(A106)

for all
|t|\leq\frac{1}{4\sigma^{2}}
. Noting that the random variable
\epsilon_{i}
is independent of
\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}
, we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(t\epsilon_{i}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\times_{1}u_{1}^{j_{1}}\times_{2}u_{2}^{j_{2}}\times_{3}u_{3}^{j_{3}})^{2}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(t(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\times_{1}u_{1}^{j_{1}}\times_{2}u_{2}^{j_{2}}\times_{3}u_{3}^{j_{3}})^{2}\right)\right]+\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(-t(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\times_{1}u_{1}^{j_{1}}\times_{2}u_{2}^{j_{2}}\times_{3}u_{3}^{j_{3}})^{2}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle e^{16t^{2}\sigma^{4}}\cdot\frac{1}{2}(e^{t\sigma^{2}}+e^{-t\sigma^{2}})\leq e^{17t^{2}\sigma^{2}},

for all
|t|\leq\frac{1}{4\sigma^{2}}
. The first inequality is the direct result of (
A106
) with
\mathbb{E}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\times_{1}u_{1}^{j_{1}}\times_{2}u_{2}^{j_{2}}\times_{3}u_{3}^{j_{3}})^{2}=\sigma^{2}
. The second inequality follows the fact that
e^{x}+e^{-x}\leq 2e^{x^{2}}
for all
x\in\mathbb{R}
.
Putting together these pieces, we get that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[e^{\lambda I_{1}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(8\lambda r\max_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3}}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\epsilon_{i}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\times_{1}u_{1}^{j_{1}}\times_{2}u_{2}^{j_{2}}\times_{3}u_{3}^{j_{3}})^{2}\right|\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(8\lambda r\max_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\epsilon_{i}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\times_{1}u_{1}^{j_{1}}\times_{2}u_{2}^{j_{2}}\times_{3}u_{3}^{j_{3}})^{2}\right)\right]

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(-8\lambda r\max_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\epsilon_{i}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\times_{1}u_{1}^{j_{1}}\times_{2}u_{2}^{j_{2}}\times_{3}u_{3}^{j_{3}})^{2}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2\left(\sum_{m=1}^{3}N_{m}\right)\cdot\prod_{j_{1}=1}^{N_{1}}\prod_{j_{2}=1}^{N_{2}}\prod_{j_{3}=1}^{N_{3}}\exp\left(17\frac{8^{2}\lambda^{2}r^{2}}{n^{2}}\sigma^{4}\right)

for any
|\lambda|\leq\frac{n}{32r\sigma^{2}}
. Let
c_{1}=17\cdot 8^{2}
, invoking the inequality
2N_{m}\leq 34^{d_{m}}\leq e^{4d_{m}}
, we have

\mathbb{E}\left[e^{\lambda I_{1}}\right]\leq\exp\left(c_{1}\lambda^{2}r^{2}\sigma^{4}/n+4\sum_{m}d_{m}\right),

for sufficiently small
\lambda
. Using the standard approach for applying Chernoff bound, we have that, for some positive constants
c_{2}
and
c_{3}
, as long as
n\geq c_{3}\left(\sum_{m}d_{m}+\log(1/\delta)\right)
,

{\bf I}_{1}\leq c_{2}r\sigma^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\sum_{m}d_{m}+\log(1/\delta)}{n}}

(A107)

with probability at least
1-\delta
.

Putting (
A102
) and (
A107
) together, we get that, there exists positive constant
c
and
c^{\prime}
such that for any positive radius
r
, any threshold
\delta\in(0,1)
, and any smaple size
n\geq c^{\prime}\left(\sum_{m}d_{m}+\log(1/\delta)\right)
,

\mathbb{P}\left[\sup_{|\omega^{(t)}|\leq r}|\omega^{(t+1)}-\mathbb{E}[\omega^{t+1}])|\leq c\sigma(\sigma r+\rho)\sqrt{\frac{\sum_{m}d_{m}+\log(1/\delta)}{n}}\right]\geq 1-\delta,

where
\rho=|1-2\pi|
.

D11
Proof of Lemma
11b

In this proof, we need to bound
|\mathbb{E}[\omega^{(t+1)}]|
. By the definition of
\mathbb{E}[\omega^{(t+1)}]
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\omega^{(t+1)}]=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{2\tau({\bm{\Theta}}^{(t)})-1\right\}\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{\top}\right]\mathrm{vec}({\bm{\beta}}_{1}^{(t+1)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}^{(t+1)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3}^{(t+1)})

(A108)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{\frac{2\exp\left(-\frac{\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}^{(t)}\|_{F}^{2}}{2\sigma^{2}}\right)}{\exp\left(-\frac{\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}^{(t)}\|_{F}^{2}}{2\sigma^{2}}\right)+\exp\left(-\frac{\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}+\bm{\mathcal{U}}^{(t)}\|_{F}^{2}}{2\sigma^{2}}\right)}-1\right\}\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{\top}\right]\mathrm{vec}({\bm{\beta}}_{1}^{(t+1)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}^{(t+1)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3}^{(t+1)})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{\frac{2}{1+\exp\left(-2\omega^{(t)}\frac{\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{\top}\mathrm{vec}({\bm{\beta}}_{1}^{(t)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}^{(t)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3}^{(t)})}{\sigma^{2}}\right)}-1\right\}\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{\top}\right]\mathrm{vec}({\bm{\beta}}_{1}^{(t+1)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}^{(t+1)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3}^{(t+1)})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1-\exp\left(-2\omega^{(t)}\frac{\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{\top}\mathrm{vec}({\bm{\beta}}_{1}^{(t)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}^{(t)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3}^{(t)})}{\sigma^{2}}\right)}{1+\exp\left(-2\omega\frac{\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{\top}\mathrm{vec}({\bm{\beta}}_{1}^{(t)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}^{(t)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3}^{(t)})}{\sigma^{2}}\right)}\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{\top}\mathrm{vec}({\bm{\beta}}_{1}^{(t+1)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}^{(t+1)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3}^{(t+1)})\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1-\exp\left(2\omega^{(t)}\frac{\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{\top}\mathrm{vec}({\bm{\beta}}_{1}^{(t)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}^{(t)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3}^{(t)})}{\sigma^{2}}\right)}{1+\exp\left(2\omega^{(t)}\frac{\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{\top}\mathrm{vec}({\bm{\beta}}_{1}^{(t)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}^{(t)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3}^{(t)})}{\sigma^{2}}\right)}\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{\top}\mathrm{vec}({\bm{\beta}}_{1}^{(t+1)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}^{(t+1)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3}^{(t+1)})\right].

The last equality uses the symmetry property of
\bm{\mathcal{X}}
. Since
\|{\bm{\beta}}_{m}^{(t)}\|_{2}=1
, let
\bm{R}_{m}
be orthonormal matrices such that
\bm{R}_{m}{\bm{\beta}}_{m}^{(t)}=\|{\bm{\beta}}_{m}^{(t)}\|_{2}e_{1}(d_{m})=e_{1}(d_{m})
. Here
e_{1}(d_{m})\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{m}}
is a vector with the first element as
1
and other elements as 0. By Lemma
S13
, we have
\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{\top}\mathrm{vec}({\bm{\beta}}_{1}^{(t)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}^{(t)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3}^{(t)})=\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}
, where
\bm{\mathcal{V}}=\bm{\mathcal{X}}\times_{1}\bm{R}_{1}\times_{2}\bm{R}_{2}\times_{3}\bm{R}_{3}
. Also, we can obtain that

\displaystyle\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{\top}\mathrm{vec}({\bm{\beta}}_{1}^{(t+1)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}^{(t+1)}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3}^{(t+1)})

\displaystyle=\bm{\mathcal{X}}\times_{1}{\bm{\beta}}_{1}^{(t+1)}\times_{2}{\bm{\beta}}_{2}^{(t+1)}\times_{3}{\bm{\beta}}_{3}^{(t+1)}

\displaystyle=\bm{\mathcal{X}}\times_{1}\bm{R}_{1}\bm{R}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{1}^{(t+1)}\times_{2}\bm{R}_{2}\bm{R}_{2}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{2}^{(t+1)}\times_{3}\bm{R}_{3}\bm{R}_{3}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{3}^{(t+1)}

\displaystyle=\bm{\mathcal{V}}\times_{1}\bm{R}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{1}^{(t+1)}\times_{2}\bm{R}_{2}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{2}^{(t+1)}\times_{3}\bm{R}_{3}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{3}^{(t+1)}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{V}})^{\top}\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{A}}),

where
\bm{\mathcal{A}}=\bm{R}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{1}^{(t+1)}\circ\bm{R}_{2}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{2}^{(t+1)}\circ\bm{R}_{3}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{3}^{(t+1)}
. Then
\mathbb{E}[\omega^{(t+1)}]
can be written as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\omega^{(t+1)}]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1-\exp\left(2\omega^{(t)}\frac{\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}}{\sigma^{2}}\right)}{1+\exp\left(2\omega^{(t)}\frac{\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}}{\sigma^{2}}\right)}\sum_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3}}\bm{\mathcal{A}}_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3}}\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3}}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3}}\bm{\mathcal{A}}_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3}}\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1-\exp\left(2\omega^{(t)}\frac{\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}}{\sigma^{2}}\right)}{1+\exp\left(2\omega^{(t)}\frac{\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}}{\sigma^{2}}\right)}\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3}}\right]

Since
\bm{\mathcal{V}}=\bm{\mathcal{X}}\times_{1}\bm{R}_{1}\times_{2}\bm{R}_{2}\times_{3}\bm{R}_{3}
, it follows a mixture distribution

\bm{\mathcal{V}}\sim\frac{1}{2}\mathcal{N}_{T}(\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{U}}},\underline{{\bm{\Sigma}}}^{\ast})+\frac{1}{2}\mathcal{N}_{T}(-\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{U}}},\underline{{\bm{\Sigma}}}^{\ast}),

where
\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}=\bm{\mathcal{U}}^{\ast}\times_{1}\bm{R}_{1}\times_{2}\bm{R}_{2}\times_{3}\bm{R}_{3}=\omega^{\ast}\bm{R}_{1}{\bm{\beta}}_{1}^{\ast}\circ\bm{R}_{2}{\bm{\beta}}_{2}^{\ast}\circ\bm{R}_{3}{\bm{\beta}}_{3}^{\ast}
and
\underline{{\bm{\Sigma}}}^{\ast}=\{{\bf I}_{d_{1}},{\bf I}_{d_{2}},\sigma^{2}{\bf I}_{d_{3}}\}
. This implies that if
(j_{1},j_{2},j_{3})\neq(1,1,1)
,

\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1-\exp\left(2\omega^{(t)}\frac{\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}}{\sigma^{2}}\right)}{1+\exp\left(2\omega^{(t)}\frac{\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}}{\sigma^{2}}\right)}\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3}}\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1-\exp\left(2\omega^{(t)}\frac{\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}}{\sigma^{2}}\right)}{1+\exp\left(2\omega^{(t)}\frac{\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}}{\sigma^{2}}\right)}\right]\mathbb{E}(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3}})=0.

Now
\mathbb{E}[\omega^{(t+1)}]
can be expressed as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\omega^{(t+1)}]=\bm{\mathcal{A}}_{1,1,1}\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1-\exp\left(2\omega^{(t)}\frac{\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}}{\sigma^{2}}\right)}{1+\exp\left(2\omega^{(t)}\frac{\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}}{\sigma^{2}}\right)}\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}\right]=\bm{\mathcal{A}}_{1,1,1}\mathbb{E}\left[\text{tanh}\left(\omega^{(t)}\frac{\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}}{\sigma^{2}}\right)\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}\right].

Let
\Gamma(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1})=\text{tanh}\left(\omega^{(t)}\frac{\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}}{\sigma^{2}}\right)\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}
. The function
\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}\rightarrow\Gamma(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1})
is symmetric, that is,

\Gamma(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1})=\Gamma_{u}(-\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1})
. Since the distribution of
\bm{\mathcal{V}}
is symmetric around
{\bf 0}
, we conclude that

\mathbb{E}[\Gamma(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1})]=\mathbb{E}[\Gamma(\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{V}}}_{1,1,1})]
, where
\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{V}}}\sim\mathcal{N}_{T}(\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{U}}},\underline{{\bm{\Sigma}}}^{\ast})
. By the Taylor expansion of
x\text{tanh}(x)
, we have

x^{2}-\frac{x^{4}}{3}\leq x\text{tanh}(x)\leq x^{2}-\frac{x^{4}}{3}+\frac{2x^{6}}{15}.

(A109)

Then it is straightforward to get that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\text{tanh}\left(\omega^{(t)}\frac{\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{V}}}_{1,1,1}}{\sigma^{2}}\right)\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{V}}}_{1,1,1}\right]=\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\omega^{(t)}}\mathbb{E}\left[\text{tanh}\left(\omega^{(t)}\frac{\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{V}}}_{1,1,1}}{\sigma^{2}}\right)\omega^{(t)}\frac{\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{V}}}_{1,1,1}}{\sigma^{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\omega^{(t)}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\omega^{(t)}\frac{\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{V}}}_{1,1,1}}{\sigma^{2}}\right)^{2}-\frac{1}{3}\left(\omega^{(t)}\frac{\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{V}}}_{1,1,1}}{\sigma^{2}}\right)^{4}+\frac{2}{15}\left(\omega^{(t)}\frac{\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{V}}}_{1,1,1}}{\sigma^{2}}\right)^{6}\right].

Since
\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{V}}}\sim\mathcal{N}_{T}(\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{U}}},\underline{{\bm{\Sigma}}}^{\ast})
, we know that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{V}}}_{1,1,1}^{2}]=\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{1,1,1}^{2}+\sigma^{2},

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{V}}}_{1,1,1}^{4}]=\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{1,1,1}^{4}+6\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{1,1,1}^{2}\sigma^{2}+3\sigma^{4},

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{V}}}_{1,1,1}^{6}]=\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{1,1,1}^{6}+15\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{1,1,1}^{4}\sigma^{2}+45\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{1,1,1}^{2}\sigma^{4}+15\sigma^{6}.

By the definition of
\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}
,
|\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{1,1,1}|\leq\omega^{\ast}=o(1)
. Then we can get that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\text{tanh}\left(\omega^{(t)}\frac{\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{V}}}_{1,1,1}}{\sigma^{2}}\right)\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{V}}}_{1,1,1}\right]\leq

\displaystyle\sigma^{2}\omega^{(t)}\frac{\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{1,1,1}^{2}+\sigma^{2}}{\sigma^{4}}-\frac{\sigma^{2}(\omega^{(t)})^{3}}{3}\frac{\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{1,1,1}^{4}+6\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{1,1,1}^{2}\sigma^{2}+3\sigma^{4}}{\sigma^{8}}

\displaystyle+\frac{2\sigma^{2}(\omega^{(t)})^{5}}{15}\frac{\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{1,1,1}^{6}+15\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{1,1,1}^{4}\sigma^{2}+45\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{1,1,1}^{2}\sigma^{4}+15\sigma^{6}}{\sigma^{12}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\omega^{(t)}\left(1-\frac{(\omega^{(t)})^{2}}{\sigma^{2}}+o\left((\omega^{(t)})^{2}\right)\right)\leq\omega^{(t)}\left(1-\frac{2(\omega^{(t)})^{2}}{\sigma^{2}}\right).

Since
\|\bm{R}_{m}{\bm{\beta}}_{m}^{(t+1)}\|_{2}\leq 1
,
\bm{\mathcal{A}}_{1,1,1}=(\bm{R}_{1}{\bm{\beta}}_{1}^{(t+1)})_{1}(\bm{R}_{2}{\bm{\beta}}_{2}^{(t+1)})_{1}(\bm{R}_{3}{\bm{\beta}}_{3}^{(t+1)})_{1}\leq 1
. Then it is straightforward to get that

|\mathbb{E}[\omega^{(t+1)}]|\leq\left(1-\frac{2(\omega^{(t)})^{2}}{\sigma^{2}}\right)\omega^{(t)}.

E
Proof of Supporting Lemmas

E1
Proof of Lemma
S9

Without loss of generality, we assume
\omega=1
. If
\omega\neq 1
, we can always reparametrize by setting
\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}=\bm{\mathcal{U}}/\omega
.
To ease notation, we let
M=3
and
m=1
. The proof holds for a general
m
and
M
with straightforward extensions.
Following the definition of mode-
1
matricization, we have

\displaystyle\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{(1)}

\displaystyle=\left[\begin{array}[]{ccccc}\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{1,1,1}&\cdots&\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{1,d_{2},1}&\cdots&\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{1,d_{2},d_{3}}\\
\vdots&\vdots&\vdots&\vdots&\vdots\\
\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{d_{1},1,1}&\cdots&\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{d_{1},d_{2},1}&\cdots&\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{d_{1},d_{2},d_{3}}\end{array}\right]

Note that

\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3}}={\bm{\beta}}_{1}(j_{1}){\bm{\beta}}_{2}(j_{2}){\bm{\beta}}_{3}(j_{3}),

where
{\bm{\beta}}_{m}(j_{m})
is the
j_{m}
-th element of
{\bm{\beta}}_{m}
. Then
\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{(1)}
can be expressed as

\displaystyle\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{(1)}

\displaystyle=\left[\begin{array}[]{ccccccc}{\bm{\beta}}_{1}(1){\bm{\beta}}_{2}(1){\bm{\beta}}_{3}(1)&\cdots&{\bm{\beta}}_{1}(1){\bm{\beta}}_{2}(d_{2}){\bm{\beta}}_{3}(1)&\cdots&{\bm{\beta}}_{1}(1){\bm{\beta}}_{2}(d_{2}){\bm{\beta}}_{3}(d_{3})\\
\vdots&\vdots&\vdots&\vdots&\vdots\\
{\bm{\beta}}_{1}(d_{1}){\bm{\beta}}_{2}(1){\bm{\beta}}_{3}(1)&\cdots&{\bm{\beta}}_{1}(d_{1}){\bm{\beta}}_{2}(d_{2}){\bm{\beta}}_{3}(1)&\cdots&{\bm{\beta}}_{1}(d_{1}){\bm{\beta}}_{2}(d_{2}){\bm{\beta}}_{3}(d_{3})\end{array}\right]

\displaystyle=\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{\bm{\beta}}_{1}(1)\\
\vdots\\
{\bm{\beta}}_{1}(d_{1})\end{array}\right)\left({\bm{\beta}}_{2}(1){\bm{\beta}}_{3}(1),\cdots,{\bm{\beta}}_{2}(d_{2}){\bm{\beta}}_{3}(1),\cdots,{\bm{\beta}}_{2}(d_{2}){\bm{\beta}}_{3}(d_{3})\right)={\bm{\beta}}_{1}\mathrm{vec}\left({\bm{\beta}}_{2}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3}\right)^{\top},

where the last equality follows the definition of vectorization of a tensor.

Next, since
{\bm{\beta}}_{m}
are unit norm vectors, we have
\|{\bm{\beta}}_{m}\|_{2}^{2}=1
. It is easy to see that

\displaystyle\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}\|_{F}^{2}=

\displaystyle\omega^{2}\left\|{\bm{\beta}}_{1}\circ\cdots\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{M}\right\|_{\text{F}}^{2}=\omega^{2}\sum_{j_{1},\ldots,j_{m}}({\bm{\beta}}_{1}(j_{1}))^{2}\cdots({\bm{\beta}}_{M}(j_{M}))^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\omega^{2}\sum_{j_{1}}({\bm{\beta}}_{1}(j_{1}))^{2}\sum_{j_{2},\ldots,j_{M}}({\bm{\beta}}_{2}(j_{2}))^{2}\cdots({\bm{\beta}}_{M}(j_{M}))^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\omega^{2}\sum_{j_{1}}({\bm{\beta}}_{1}(j_{1}))^{2}\cdots\sum_{j_{M}}({\bm{\beta}}_{M}(j_{M}))^{2}=\omega^{2}.

E2
Proof of Lemma
S10

Write
{\bm{Y}}=\begin{bmatrix}{\bm{Y}}_{1,1}&\cdots&{\bm{Y}}_{1,d_{2}}\\
\vdots&\vdots&\vdots\\
{\bm{Y}}_{d_{1},1}&\cdots&{\bm{Y}}_{d_{1},d_{2}}\end{bmatrix}
and
{\bm{D}}=\begin{bmatrix}{\bm{D}}_{1,1}&\cdots&{\bm{D}}_{1,d_{2}}\\
\vdots&\vdots&\vdots\\
{\bm{D}}_{d_{2},1}&\cdots&{\bm{D}}_{d_{2},d_{2}}\end{bmatrix}
. Then
{\bm{Y}}{\bm{D}}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{1}\times d_{2}}
can be written as

\displaystyle{\bm{Y}}{\bm{D}}

\displaystyle=\left[\begin{array}[]{ccc}\sum_{i=1}^{d_{2}}{\bm{Y}}_{1,i}{\bm{D}}_{i,1}&\cdots&\sum_{i=1}^{d_{2}}{\bm{Y}}_{1,i}{\bm{D}}_{i,d_{2}}\\
\vdots&\vdots&\vdots\\
\sum_{i=1}^{d_{2}}{\bm{Y}}_{d_{1},i}{\bm{D}}_{i,1}&\cdots&\sum_{i=1}^{d_{2}}{\bm{Y}}_{d_{1},i}{\bm{D}}_{i,d_{2}}\end{array}\right].

For the matrix
\mathbb{E}({\bm{Y}}{\bm{D}}{\bm{Y}}^{\top})\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{1}\times d_{1}}
, denote each element as
\mathbb{E}({\bm{Y}}{\bm{D}}{\bm{Y}}^{\top})_{l,k}
. If
l=k
, we have

\mathbb{E}({\bm{Y}}{\bm{D}}{\bm{Y}}^{\top})_{l,l}=\sum_{j=1}^{d_{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{d_{2}}\mathbb{E}({\bm{Y}}_{l,i}{\bm{D}}_{i,j}{\bm{Y}}_{l,j})=\sum_{i=1}^{d_{2}}{\bm{D}}_{i,i}\mathbb{E}({\bm{Y}}_{l,i}^{2})=\text{tr}({\bm{D}}).

If
l\neq k
, we have

\mathbb{E}({\bm{Y}}{\bm{D}}{\bm{Y}}^{\top})_{l,k}=\sum_{j=1}^{d_{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{d_{2}}\mathbb{E}({\bm{Y}}_{l,i}{\bm{D}}_{i,j}{\bm{Y}}_{k,j})=\sum_{j=1}^{d_{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{d_{2}}{\bm{D}}_{i,j}\mathbb{E}({\bm{Y}}_{l,i}{\bm{Y}}_{k,j})=0.

Putting all
(l,k)
pairs, it arrives at that

\mathbb{E}({\bm{Y}}{\bm{D}}{\bm{Y}}^{\top})=\text{tr}({\bm{D}}){\bf I}_{d_{1}}.

E3
Proof of Lemma
S11

Consider the first inequality. Let
{\bm{\beta}}_{m}(l_{m})
be the
l
th element of
{\bm{\beta}}_{m}
, we have

\displaystyle{\bm{\beta}}_{m}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{m}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\sum_{l_{m}}{\bm{\beta}}_{m}(l_{m}){\bm{\beta}}_{m}^{\prime}(l_{m})=\sum_{l_{m}}{\bm{\beta}}_{m}(l_{m})\{{\bm{\beta}}_{m}(l_{m})-{\bm{\beta}}_{m}(l_{m})+{\bm{\beta}}_{m}^{\prime}(l_{m})\}

\displaystyle=\|{\bm{\beta}}_{m}\|_{2}^{2}-{\bm{\beta}}_{m}^{\top}({\bm{\beta}}_{m}-{\bm{\beta}}_{m}^{\prime})

\displaystyle\geq\|{\bm{\beta}}_{m}\|_{2}^{2}-\|{\bm{\beta}}_{m}\|_{2}\|{\bm{\beta}}_{m}-{\bm{\beta}}_{m}^{\prime}\|_{2}=1-\|{\bm{\beta}}_{m}-{\bm{\beta}}_{m}^{\prime}\|_{2},

where the first inequality follows the fact that
{\bm{\beta}}_{m}^{\top}({\bm{\beta}}_{m}-{\bm{\beta}}_{m}^{\prime})\leq\|{\bm{\beta}}_{m}\|_{2}\|{\bm{\beta}}_{m}-{\bm{\beta}}_{m}^{\prime}\|_{2}
and the last equality is obtained by
\|{\bm{\beta}}_{m}\|_{2}=1
.
Based on this, it holds that

\displaystyle\mathrm{vec}({\bm{\beta}}_{1}\circ\cdots\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{M})^{\top}\mathrm{vec}({\bm{\beta}}_{1}^{\prime}\circ\cdots\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{M}^{\prime})=\sum_{l_{1},\ldots,l_{M}}\{{\bm{\beta}}_{1}(l_{1}){\bm{\beta}}_{1}^{\prime}(l_{1})\}\cdots\{{\bm{\beta}}_{M}(l_{M}){\bm{\beta}}_{M}^{\prime}(l_{M})\}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\prod_{m}(1-\|{\bm{\beta}}_{m}-{\bm{\beta}}_{m}^{\prime}\|_{2}).

Consider the second inequality. Based on
\|{\bm{\beta}}_{m}\|_{2}=\|{\bm{\beta}}_{m}^{\prime}\|_{2}=1
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\prod_{m}^{\circ}{\bm{\beta}}_{m}-\prod_{m}^{\circ}{\bm{\beta}}_{m}^{\prime}\right\|_{\text{F}}=\left\|\prod_{m}^{\circ}{\bm{\beta}}_{m}-{\bm{\beta}}_{1}^{\prime}\prod_{m\neq 1}^{\circ}{\bm{\beta}}_{m}+{\bm{\beta}}_{1}^{\prime}\prod_{m\neq 1}^{\circ}{\bm{\beta}}_{m}-\prod_{m}^{\circ}{\bm{\beta}}_{m}^{\prime}\right\|_{\text{F}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|{\bm{\beta}}_{1}-{\bm{\beta}}_{1}^{\prime}\|_{2}\|\prod_{m\neq 1}^{\circ}{\bm{\beta}}_{m}\|_{\text{F}}+\|{\bm{\beta}}_{1}\|_{2}\left\|\prod_{m\neq 1}^{\circ}{\bm{\beta}}_{m}-\prod_{m\neq 1}^{\circ}{\bm{\beta}}_{m}^{\prime}\right\|_{\text{F}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|{\bm{\beta}}_{1}-{\bm{\beta}}_{1}^{\prime}\|_{2}+\left\|\prod_{m\neq 1}^{\circ}{\bm{\beta}}_{m}-\prod_{m\neq 1}^{\circ}{\bm{\beta}}_{m}^{\prime}\right\|_{\text{F}}\leq\cdots\leq\sum_{m}\left\|{\bm{\beta}}_{m}-{\bm{\beta}}_{m}^{\prime}\right\|_{2}.

The first inequality follows the fact that
\|\bm{A}+\bm{B}\|_{\text{F}}\leq\|\bm{A}\|_{\text{F}}+\|\bm{B}\|_{\text{F}}
. The second equality is the direct result of Lemma
S9
.

Consider the third inequality. By Cauchy Schwarz inequality, then we can get that

\displaystyle\|\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m}{\bm{\beta}}_{m})-\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m}{\bm{\beta}}_{m}^{\prime})\|_{2}^{2}=\|\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m}{\bm{\beta}}_{m}-\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m}{\bm{\beta}}_{m}^{\prime}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\leq M\sum_{m}\|{\bm{\beta}}_{m}^{\prime}-{\bm{\beta}}_{m}\|_{2}^{2}.

E4
Proof of Lemma
S12

Given matrices
{\bm{\Omega}}_{m}
and
{\bm{\Omega}}_{m}^{\prime}
, we have

\displaystyle\Big\|\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m}{\bm{\Omega}}_{m}-\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m}{\bm{\Omega}}_{m}^{\prime}\Big\|_{\text{F}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big\|\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m>1}{\bm{\Omega}}_{m}\otimes({\bm{\Omega}}_{1}-{\bm{\Omega}}_{1}^{\prime})+\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m>2}{\bm{\Omega}}_{m}\otimes({\bm{\Omega}}_{2}-{\bm{\Omega}}_{2}^{\prime})\otimes{\bm{\Omega}}_{1}^{\prime}+\cdots+({\bm{\Omega}}_{M}-{\bm{\Omega}}_{M}^{\prime})\otimes\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m<M}{\bm{\Omega}}_{m}^{\prime}\Big\|_{\text{F}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\Big\|\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m>1}{\bm{\Omega}}_{m}\otimes({\bm{\Omega}}_{1}-{\bm{\Omega}}_{1}^{\prime})\Big\|_{\text{F}}+\cdots+\Big\|({\bm{\Omega}}_{M}-{\bm{\Omega}}_{M}^{\prime})\otimes\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m<M}{\bm{\Omega}}_{m}^{\prime}\Big\|_{\text{F}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{m}\sqrt{\frac{d}{d_{m}}}\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{m}-{\bm{\Omega}}_{m}^{\prime}\|_{\text{F}},

where the last inequality is the direct result of
\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{m}\|_{\text{F}}=\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{m}^{\prime}\|_{\text{F}}=\sqrt{d_{m}}
.

E5
Proof of Lemma
S13

Let
\bm{\mathcal{U}}=e_{1}(d_{1})\circ e_{2}(d_{2})\circ e_{3}(d_{3})
, this can be obtained by

\displaystyle 4\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{\top}\mathrm{vec}({\bm{\beta}}_{1}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3})=-\|{\bm{\beta}}_{1}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3}-\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|_{F}^{2}+\|{\bm{\beta}}_{1}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3}+\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|_{F}^{2}

(A110)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\|\{(\bm{R}_{1}{\bm{\beta}}_{1})\circ(\bm{R}_{2}{\bm{\beta}}_{2})\circ(\bm{R}_{3}{\bm{\beta}}_{3})-\bm{\mathcal{V}}\}\times_{1}\bm{R}_{1}^{\top}\times_{2}\bm{R}_{2}^{\top}\times_{3}\bm{R}_{3}^{\top}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|\{(\bm{R}_{1}{\bm{\beta}}_{1})\circ(\bm{R}_{2}{\bm{\beta}}_{2})\circ(\bm{R}_{3}{\bm{\beta}}_{3})+\bm{\mathcal{V}}\}\times_{1}\bm{R}_{1}^{\top}\times_{2}\bm{R}_{2}^{\top}\times_{3}\bm{R}_{3}^{\top}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\|\bm{R}_{1}^{\top}\{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{(1)}-\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{(1)}\}(\bm{R}_{3}^{\top}\otimes\bm{R}_{2}^{\top})\|_{F}^{2}+\|\bm{R}_{1}^{\top}\{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{(1)}+\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{(1)}\}(\bm{R}_{3}^{\top}\otimes\bm{R}_{2}^{\top})\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\text{tr}\{(\bm{R}_{1}^{\top}\{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{(1)}-\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{(1)}\}(\bm{R}_{3}^{\top}\otimes\bm{R}_{2}^{\top}))(\bm{R}_{1}^{\top}\{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{(1)}-\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{(1)}\}(\bm{R}_{3}^{\top}\otimes\bm{R}_{2}^{\top}))^{\top}\}

\displaystyle+\text{tr}\{(\bm{R}_{1}^{\top}\{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{(1)}+\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{(1)}\}(\bm{R}_{3}^{\top}\otimes\bm{R}_{2}^{\top}))(\bm{R}_{1}^{\top}\{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{(1)}+\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{(1)}\}(\bm{R}_{3}^{\top}\otimes\bm{R}_{2}^{\top}))^{\top}\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 4\text{tr}\{\bm{R}_{1}^{\top}\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{(1)}(\bm{R}_{3}^{\top}\otimes\bm{R}_{2}^{\top})(\bm{R}_{3}^{\top}\otimes\bm{R}_{2}^{\top})^{\top}\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{(1)}^{\top}\bm{R}_{1}\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 4\text{tr}\{\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{(1)}^{\top}\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{(1)}\}=4\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}.

The second equality uses that
\bm{R}_{m}{\bm{\beta}}_{m}=e_{1}(d_{m})
and
(\bm{A}\times_{m}\bm{B})_{(m)}=\bm{B}\bm{A}_{(m)}
. The fifth equality is the direct result of
\bm{R}_{m}^{\top}\bm{R}_{m}=I
and
\text{tr}(\bm{A}\bm{B})=\text{tr}(\bm{B}\bm{A})
. The last equality is true, because
\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{1,1,1}=1
and
\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{l,j,k}=0
for others.

E6
Proof of Lemma
S14

Partial derivatives of
Q_{n}
:
Recall that

Q_{n}({\bm{\Theta}}|{\bm{\Theta}}^{(t)})=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}^{(t)})\left[\log(\pi_{k})+\log\{f_{k}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}|{\bm{\theta}}_{k})\}\right]

where
f_{k}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}|{\bm{\theta}}_{k})=(2\pi)^{-d/2}\left\{\prod_{m=1}^{M}|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}|^{d/(2d_{m})}\right\}\exp\left(-\left\|(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k})\times\underline{{\bm{\Omega}}_{k}}^{1/2}\right\|_{F}^{2}/2\right)
.

For the partial derivative of
Q_{n}({\bm{\Theta}}|{\bm{\Theta}}^{(t)})
around
{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}
, it is equivalent to taking partial derivatives of

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}^{(t)})\log\{f_{k}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}|{\bm{\theta}}_{k})\}

(A111)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}^{(t)})\left\{-\frac{d}{2}\log(2\pi)+\sum_{m}\frac{d}{2d_{m}}\log(|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}|)-\left\|(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k})\times\underline{{\bm{\Omega}}_{k}}^{1/2}\right\|_{F}^{2}/2\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}^{(t)})\Big\{-\frac{d}{2}\log(2\pi)+\sum_{m}\frac{d}{2d_{m}}\log(|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}|)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\text{tr}\left\{\left({\bf V}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i},m}^{\top}-{\bf V}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k},m}^{\top}\right){\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}({\bf V}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i},m}-{\bf V}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k},m})\right\}\Big\},

where
{\bf V}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i},m}=\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\right)_{(m)}\left(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{1/2}\right)^{\top}
and
{\bf V}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k},m}=\left(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}\right)_{(m)}\left(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{1/2}\right)^{\top}
. The last equality is due to (
A158
). By (
A159
) and (
A160
), we can obtain the first partial derivative of
Q_{n}({\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime},\bar{{\bm{\Theta}}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})
about
{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}
as

\displaystyle\nabla_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}Q_{n}({\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime},\bar{{\bm{\Theta}}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\bar{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m}\left\{(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i})_{(m)}-\bar{\omega}_{k}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m^{\prime}})\right\}\left(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\right)\bar{\omega}_{k}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m^{\prime}}).

The second partial derivative of
Q_{n}({\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime},\bar{{\bm{\Theta}}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})
around
{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}
is

\displaystyle\nabla_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}^{2}Q_{n}({\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime},\bar{{\bm{\Theta}}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\bar{\omega}_{k}^{2}\left\{\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m^{\prime}})^{\top}\left(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\right)\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m^{\prime}})\right\}\bar{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m}.

Similarly, for the partial derivative of
Q_{n}({\bm{\Theta}}|{\bm{\Theta}}^{(t)})
around
\omega_{k}
, it is equivalent to taking the partial derivative of

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}^{(t)})\log\{f_{k}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}|{\bm{\theta}}_{k})\}

(A112)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}^{(t)})\left\{\frac{d}{2}\log(2\pi)+\sum_{m}\frac{d}{2d_{m}}\log(|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}|)\right\}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}^{(t)})\{\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i})-\omega_{k}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m}{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m})\}^{\top}\left(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m}{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m}\right)\left\{\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i})-\omega_{k}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m}{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m})\right\}.

Then the first and second partial derivatives of
Q_{n}(\omega_{k}^{\prime},\bar{{\bm{\Theta}}}_{-\omega_{k}}|{\bm{\Theta}})
around
\omega_{k}
are

\displaystyle\nabla_{\omega_{k}}Q_{n}(\omega_{k}^{\prime},\bar{{\bm{\Theta}}}_{-\omega_{k}}|{\bm{\Theta}})=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\{\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i})-\omega_{k}^{\prime}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m}\bar{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m})\}^{\top}\left(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m}\bar{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m}\right)\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m}\bar{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m});

\displaystyle\nabla_{\omega_{k}}^{2}Q_{n}(\omega_{k}^{\prime},\bar{{\bm{\Theta}}}_{-\omega_{k}}|{\bm{\Theta}})=-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m}\bar{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m})^{\top}\left(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m}\bar{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m}\right)\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m}\bar{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}).

For the partial derivative of
Q_{n}({\bm{\Theta}}|{\bm{\Theta}}^{(t)})
around
{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}
, it is equivalent to take the partial derivative of

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}^{(t)})\log\{f_{k}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}|{\bm{\theta}}_{k})\}

(A113)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}^{(t)})\left\{-\frac{d}{2}\log(2\pi)+\sum_{m}\frac{d}{2d_{m}}\log(|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}|)-\left\|(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k})\times\underline{{\bm{\Omega}}_{k}}^{1/2}\right\|_{F}^{2}/2\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}^{(t)})\Big\{-\frac{d}{2}\log(2\pi)+\sum_{m}\frac{d}{2d_{m}}\log(|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}|)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\text{tr}\left\{\left({\bf V}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i},m}^{\top}-{\bf V}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k},m}^{\top}\right){\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}({\bf V}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i},m}-{\bf V}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k},m})\right\}\Big\}.

Then the first and second partial derivatives of
Q_{n}({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime},\bar{{\bm{\Theta}}}_{-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})
around
{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}
are

\displaystyle\nabla_{{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}Q_{n}({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime},\bar{{\bm{\Theta}}}_{-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\left\{\frac{d}{2d_{m}}({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime})^{-1}-\frac{1}{2}\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}-\bar{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{k}\right)_{(m)}\left(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\right)\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}-\bar{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{k}\right)_{(m)}^{\top}\right\}

\displaystyle\nabla_{{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}^{2}Q_{n}({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime},\bar{{\bm{\Theta}}}_{-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})=-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\left\{\frac{d}{2d_{m}}({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime})^{-1}\otimes({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime})^{-1}\right\}.

Derivatives of
\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})
:
Recall that

\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})=\frac{\pi_{k}f_{k}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}|{\bm{\theta}}_{k})}{\sum_{k}\pi_{k}f_{k}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}|{\bm{\theta}}_{k})},

where
f_{k}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}|{\bm{\theta}}_{k})=(2\pi)^{-d/2}\left\{\prod_{m=1}^{M}|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}|^{d/(2d_{m})}\right\}\exp\left(-\left\|(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k})\times\underline{{\bm{\Omega}}_{k}}^{1/2}\right\|_{F}^{2}/2\right)
.
We consider two separate cases. One is the derivative for parameters in
{\bm{\theta}}_{k}
and the another is the derivative for parameters in
{\bm{\theta}}_{l}
, where
l\neq k
.

First, let
\bm{\vartheta}
be any parameters in
{\bm{\theta}}_{k}
, the first derivative of
\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})
around
\bm{\vartheta}
is

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})}{\partial\bm{\vartheta}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\pi_{k}\frac{\partial f_{k}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}|{\bm{\theta}}_{k})}{\partial\bm{\vartheta}}\{\sum_{k}\pi_{k}f_{k}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}|{\bm{\theta}}_{k})\}-\pi_{k}f_{k}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}|{\bm{\theta}}_{k})\pi_{k}\frac{\partial f_{k}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}|{\bm{\theta}}_{k})}{\partial\bm{\vartheta}}}{\{\sum_{k}\pi_{k}f_{k}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}|{\bm{\theta}}_{k})\}^{2}}

(A114)

\displaystyle=\pi_{k}\frac{\partial f_{k}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}|{\bm{\theta}}_{k})}{\partial\bm{\vartheta}}\frac{1-\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})}{\sum_{k}\pi_{k}f_{k}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}|{\bm{\theta}}_{k})}.

The parameters
\bm{\vartheta}
here could be
{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}
,
\omega_{k}
or
{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}
for any
m\in[M]
. By using (
A111
), (
A112
) and (
A113
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{\partial f_{k}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}|{\bm{\theta}}_{k})}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}=f_{k}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}|{\bm{\theta}}_{k}){\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}\right)_{(m)}\left(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\right)\omega_{k}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m^{\prime}})

(A115)

\displaystyle\frac{\partial f_{k}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}|{\bm{\theta}}_{k})}{\partial\omega_{k}}=f_{k}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}|{\bm{\theta}}_{k})\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k})^{\top}\left(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m}{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}\right)\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m^{\prime}})

\displaystyle\frac{\partial f_{k}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}|{\bm{\theta}}_{k})}{\partial{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}=f_{k}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}|{\bm{\theta}}_{k})\left\{\frac{d}{2d_{m}}{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{-1}-\frac{1}{2}\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}\right)_{(m)}\left(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\right)\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}\right)_{(m)}^{\top}\right\}.

Plugging (
A115
) into (
A114
), we can obtain that

\ \begin{aligned} &\frac{\partial\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}=\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})(1-\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})){\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}\right)_{(m)}\left(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\right)\omega_{k}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m^{\prime}})\\
&\frac{\partial\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})}{\partial\omega_{k}}=\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})(1-\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}))\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k})^{\top}\left(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m}{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}\right)\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m^{\prime}})\\
&\frac{\partial\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})}{\partial{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}=\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})(1-\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}))\left\{\frac{d}{2d_{m}}{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{-1}-\frac{1}{2}\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}\right)_{(m)}\left(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\right)\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}\right)_{(m)}^{\top}\right\}.\end{aligned}

By the definition of
{\bm{\theta}}_{k}
, we can get that
\nabla_{{{\bm{\theta}}_{k}}}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})=\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})(1-\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}))J_{i}({\bm{\theta}}_{k})
with
J_{i}({\bm{\theta}}_{k})=(J_{i,1}({\bm{\theta}}_{k}),J_{i,2}({\bm{\theta}}_{k}),J_{i,3}({\bm{\theta}}_{k}))
.

Second, let
\bm{\vartheta}
be any parameters in
{\bm{\theta}}_{l}
with
l\neq k
, the first derivative of
\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})
around
\bm{\vartheta}
is

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})}{\partial\bm{\vartheta}}

\displaystyle=\frac{-\pi_{k}f_{k}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}|{\bm{\theta}}_{k})\pi_{l}\frac{\partial f_{l}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}|{\bm{\theta}}_{l})}{\partial\bm{\vartheta}}}{\{\sum_{k}\pi_{k}f_{k}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}|{\bm{\theta}}_{k})\}^{2}}=\pi_{l}\frac{\partial f_{l}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}|{\bm{\theta}}_{l})}{\partial\bm{\vartheta}}\frac{-\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})}{\sum_{k}\pi_{k}f_{k}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}|{\bm{\theta}}_{k})}

(A116)

The parameters
\bm{\vartheta}
here could be
{\bm{\beta}}_{l,m}
,
\omega_{l}
or
{\bm{\Omega}}_{l,m}
for any
m\in[M]
. Similarly, plugging (
A115
) into (
A116
), we can get that
\nabla_{{{\bm{\theta}}_{l}}}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})=-\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\tau_{il}({\bm{\Theta}})J_{i}({\bm{\theta}}_{l})
with
J_{i}({\bm{\theta}}_{l})=(J_{i,1}({\bm{\theta}}_{l}),J_{i,2}({\bm{\theta}}_{l}),J_{i,3}({\bm{\theta}}_{l}))
for
l\neq k
.

E7
Proof of Lemma
S15

Recall that if
Z_{i}=k^{\prime}
,
\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\sim\mathcal{N}_{T}\left(\bm{\mathcal{U}}^{\ast}_{k^{\prime}},{\underline{{\bm{\Sigma}}}^{\ast}_{k^{\prime}}}\right)
. It could be seen that
(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i})_{(m)}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{m}\times(\frac{d}{d_{m}})}
and

(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i})_{(m)}|Z_{i}=k^{\prime}\sim\mathcal{N}_{T}\left((\bm{\mathcal{U}}^{\ast}_{k^{\prime}})_{(m)},\left\{{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\ast}_{k^{\prime},m},\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\left({\bm{\Sigma}}_{k^{\prime},m^{\prime}}^{\ast}\right)\right\}\right).

Since
{\bm{Y}}_{i}=\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\right)_{(m)}\left\{\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\left(\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\right)^{1/2}\right\}
, it is straightforward to get that

{\bm{Y}}_{i}|Z_{i}=k^{\prime}\sim\mathcal{N}_{T}\left(\tilde{{\bm{U}}}_{k^{\prime}},\{\tilde{{\bm{\Sigma}}}_{k^{\prime},1},\tilde{{\bm{\Sigma}}}_{k^{\prime},2}\}\right)

with
\tilde{{\bm{U}}}_{k^{\prime}}=\left(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k^{\prime}}^{\ast}\right)_{(m)}\left\{\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\left(\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\right)^{1/2}\right\}
,
\tilde{{\bm{\Sigma}}}_{k^{\prime},1}={\bm{\Sigma}}_{k^{\prime},m}^{\ast}
and

\tilde{{\bm{\Sigma}}}_{k^{\prime},2}=\left\{\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\left(\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k^{\prime},m^{\prime}}\right)^{1/2}\right\}\left\{\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\left({\bm{\Sigma}}_{k^{\prime},m^{\prime}}^{\ast}\right)\right\}\left\{\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\left(\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k^{\prime},m^{\prime}}\right)^{1/2}\right\}.

Let
{\bm{Y}}_{i}(l,j)
be
(l,j)
-th element of
{\bm{Y}}_{i}
. It can then be expressed as

{\bm{Y}}_{i}(l,j)=\sum_{k^{\prime}=1}^{K}{\bf I}(Z_{i}=k^{\prime})\{\tilde{{\bm{U}}}_{k^{\prime}}(l,j)+\tilde{V}_{l,j,k^{\prime}}\},

where
\tilde{{\bm{U}}}_{k^{\prime}}(l,j)=\mathbb{E}\left\{{\bm{Y}}_{i}(l,j)|Z_{i}=k^{\prime}\right\}
and
\tilde{V}_{l,j,k^{\prime}}\sim\mathcal{N}\left(0,\text{var}({\bm{Y}}_{i}(l,j)|Z_{i}=k^{\prime})\right)
.
Denote
{\bm{Y}}_{i}(l,\cdot)\in\mathbb{R}^{d/d_{m}}
as the
l
-th row of
{\bm{Y}}_{i}
, and then we may write
{\bm{Y}}_{i}{\bm{Y}}_{i}^{\top}
as

\left(\begin{matrix}{\bm{Y}}_{i}(1,\cdot)^{\top}{\bm{Y}}_{i}(1,\cdot)&\cdots&{\bm{Y}}_{i}(1,\cdot)^{\top}{\bm{Y}}_{i}(d_{m},\cdot)\\
\vdots&\ddots&\vdots\\
{\bm{Y}}_{i}(d_{m},\cdot)^{\top}{\bm{Y}}_{i}(1,\cdot)&\cdots&{\bm{Y}}_{i}(d_{m},\cdot)^{\top}{\bm{Y}}_{i}(d_{m},\cdot)\end{matrix}\right),

where

\displaystyle{\displaystyle\bm{Y}}_{i}(l,\cdot)^{\top}{\bm{Y}}_{i}(l^{\prime},\cdot)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{d/d_{m}}\sum_{k^{\prime}=1}^{K}\sum_{k^{\prime\prime}=1}^{K}{\bf I}(Z_{i}=k^{\prime}){\bf I}(Z_{i}=k^{\prime\prime})\left\{\tilde{{\bm{U}}}_{k^{\prime}}(l,j)+\tilde{V}_{l,j,k^{\prime}}\right\}\left\{\tilde{{\bm{U}}}^{\ast}_{k^{\prime\prime}}(l^{\prime},j)+\tilde{V}_{l^{\prime},j,k^{\prime\prime}}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{d/d_{m}}\sum_{k^{\prime}=1}^{K}{\bf I}(Z_{i}=k^{\prime})\left\{\tilde{{\bm{U}}}_{k^{\prime}}(l,j)+\tilde{V}_{l,j,k^{\prime}}\right\}\left\{\tilde{{\bm{U}}}_{k^{\prime}}(l^{\prime},j)+\tilde{V}_{l^{\prime},j,k^{\prime}}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{d/d_{m}}\sum_{k^{\prime}=1}^{K}{\bf I}(Z_{i}=k^{\prime})\left\{\tilde{{\bm{U}}}_{k^{\prime}}(l,j)\tilde{{\bm{U}}}_{k^{\prime}}(l^{\prime},j)+\tilde{{\bm{U}}}_{k^{\prime}}(l,j)\tilde{V}(l^{\prime},j)+\tilde{V}_{l,j,k^{\prime}}\tilde{{\bm{U}}}_{k^{\prime}}(l^{\prime},j)+\tilde{V}_{l,j,k^{\prime}}\tilde{V}_{l^{\prime},j,k^{\prime}}\right\}.

To ease notation, denote

\tilde{{\bm{M}}}(l,l^{\prime})=\frac{T}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n/T}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}){\bm{Y}}_{i}(l,\cdot)^{\top}{\bm{Y}}_{i}(l^{\prime},\cdot)-\mathbb{E}\left\{\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}){\bm{Y}}_{i}(l,\cdot)^{\top}{\bm{Y}}_{i}(l^{\prime},\cdot)\right\}.

Plugging in the expressions of
{\bm{Y}}_{i}(l,l^{\prime})
,
\tilde{{\bm{M}}}(l,l^{\prime})
can be expressed as

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n/T}\sum_{k^{\prime}=1}^{K}\underbrace{\left[\frac{T}{n}{\bf I}(Z_{i}=k^{\prime})\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\tilde{{\bm{U}}}_{k^{\prime}}(l,\cdot)^{\top}\tilde{{\bm{U}}}_{k^{\prime}}(l^{\prime},\cdot)-\mathbb{E}\left\{{\bf I}(Z_{i}=k^{\prime})\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\tilde{{\bm{U}}}_{k^{\prime}}(l,\cdot)^{\top}\tilde{{\bm{U}}}_{k^{\prime}}(l^{\prime},\cdot)\right\}\right]}_{\tilde{{\bm{M}}}_{1}(l,l^{\prime})}

\displaystyle+\frac{d}{d_{m}}\sum_{i=1}^{n/T}\sum_{k^{\prime}=1}^{K}\underbrace{\frac{Td_{m}}{nd}\sum_{i,j}\left[{\bf I}(Z_{i}=k^{\prime})\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\tilde{{\bm{U}}}_{k^{\prime}}(l,j)\tilde{V}_{l^{\prime},j,k^{\prime}}-\mathbb{E}\left\{{\bf I}(Z_{i}=k^{\prime})\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\tilde{{\bm{U}}}_{k^{\prime}}(l,j)\tilde{V}_{l^{\prime},j,k^{\prime}}\right\}\right]}_{\tilde{{\bm{M}}}_{2}(l,l^{\prime})}

\displaystyle+\frac{d}{d_{m}}\sum_{i=1}^{n/T}\sum_{k^{\prime}=1}^{K}\underbrace{\frac{Td_{m}}{nd}\sum_{i,j}\left[{\bf I}(Z_{i}=k^{\prime})\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\tilde{V}_{l,j,k^{\prime}}\tilde{{\bm{U}}}_{k^{\prime}}(l^{\prime},j)-\mathbb{E}\left\{{\bf I}(Z_{i}=k^{\prime})\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\tilde{V}_{l,j,k^{\prime}}\tilde{{\bm{U}}}_{k^{\prime}}(l^{\prime},j)\right\}\right]}_{\tilde{{\bm{M}}}_{3}(l,l^{\prime})}

\displaystyle+\frac{d}{d_{m}}\sum_{i=1}^{n/T}\sum_{k^{\prime}=1}^{K}\underbrace{\frac{Td_{m}}{nd}\sum_{i,j}\left[{\bf I}(Z_{i}=k^{\prime})\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\tilde{V}_{l,j,k^{\prime}}\tilde{V}_{l^{\prime},j,k^{\prime}}-\mathbb{E}\left\{{\bf I}(Z_{i}=k^{\prime})\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\tilde{V}_{l,j,k^{\prime}}\tilde{V}_{l^{\prime},j,k^{\prime}}\right\}\right]}_{\tilde{{\bm{M}}}_{4}(l,l^{\prime})}

Next, we will bound terms
\tilde{{\bm{M}}}_{1}(l,l^{\prime})
,
\tilde{{\bm{M}}}_{2}(l,l^{\prime})
,
\tilde{{\bm{M}}}_{3}(l,l^{\prime})
and
\tilde{{\bm{M}}}_{4}(l,l^{\prime})
separately. We begin with
\tilde{{\bm{M}}}_{1}(l,l^{\prime})
. Since
|{\bf I}(Z_{i}=k^{\prime})\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\tilde{{\bm{U}}}_{k^{\prime}}(l,\cdot)^{\top}\tilde{{\bm{U}}}_{k^{\prime}}(l^{\prime},\cdot)|\leq\max_{l,l^{\prime}}\left|\tilde{{\bm{U}}}_{k^{\prime}}(l,\cdot)^{\top}\tilde{{\bm{U}}}_{k^{\prime}}(l^{\prime},\cdot)\right|
, it is seen that
{\bf I}(Z_{i}=k^{\prime})\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}(l,\cdot)^{\top}\tilde{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}(l^{\prime},\cdot)
is a sub-Gaussian random variable with

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\bf I}(Z_{i}=k^{\prime})\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\tilde{{\bm{U}}}_{k^{\prime}}(l,\cdot)^{\top}\tilde{{\bm{U}}}_{k^{\prime}}(l^{\prime},\cdot)-\mathbb{E}\left\{{\bf I}(Z_{i}=k^{\prime})\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\tilde{{\bm{U}}}_{k^{\prime}}(l,\cdot)^{\top}\tilde{{\bm{U}}}_{k^{\prime}}(l^{\prime},\cdot)\right\}\right\rVert_{\psi_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\max_{l,l^{\prime}}\left|\tilde{{\bm{U}}}_{k^{\prime}}(l,\cdot)^{\top}\tilde{{\bm{U}}}_{k^{\prime}}(l^{\prime},\cdot)\right|.

By the concentration inequality in Lemma
S3
, we have for any
t>0
,

\mathbb{P}\left(|\tilde{{\bm{M}}}_{1}(l,l^{\prime})|\leq t\right)\geq 1-e\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{Cnt^{2}}{4T\max_{l,l^{\prime}}\left|\tilde{{\bm{U}}}_{k^{\prime}}(l,\cdot)^{\top}\tilde{{\bm{U}}}_{k^{\prime}}(l^{\prime},\cdot)\right|^{2}}\right).

Since
\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{b}\leq\|\bm{a}\|_{2}\|\bm{b}\|_{2}
and
\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k^{\prime},m}\|_{2}\leq 3\phi_{2}/2
, we have

\displaystyle\max_{l,l^{\prime}}\left|\tilde{{\bm{U}}}_{k^{\prime}}(l,\cdot)^{\top}\tilde{{\bm{U}}}_{k^{\prime}}(l^{\prime},\cdot)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\max_{l,l^{\prime}}\left|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k^{\prime}}^{\ast}(l,\cdot)^{\top}\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k^{\prime}}^{\ast}(l^{\prime},\cdot)\right|(3\phi_{2}/2)^{M-1}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{l}\|(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k^{\prime}}^{\ast})_{(m)}(l.\cdot)\|_{2}^{2}(3\phi_{2}/2)^{M-1}.

Therefore, for any pair
(l,l^{\prime})
, it holds that

\displaystyle|{\bm{M}}_{1}(l,l^{\prime})|\leq\sqrt{{4}/{C}}\max_{l}\|(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k^{\prime}}^{\ast})_{(m)}(l.\cdot)\|_{2}^{2}(3\phi_{2}/2)^{M-1}\sqrt{\frac{\log(d_{m})+\log(e/p_{n})}{n/T}}

(A117)

with probability at least
1-p_{n}
.
Note that both
{\bf I}(Z_{i}=k^{\prime})\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\tilde{{\bm{U}}}_{k^{\prime}}(l,j)\tilde{V}_{l^{\prime},j,k^{\prime}}
and
{\bf I}(Z_{i}=k^{\prime})\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\tilde{V}_{l,j,k^{\prime}}\tilde{{\bm{U}}}_{k^{\prime}}(l^{\prime},j)
are sub-exponential random variables with

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\bf I}(Z_{i}=k^{\prime})\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\tilde{{\bm{U}}}_{k^{\prime}}(l,\cdot)^{\top}\tilde{V}_{k^{\prime}}(l^{\prime},\cdot)-\mathbb{E}\left\{{\bf I}(Z_{i}=k^{\prime})\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\tilde{{\bm{U}}}_{k^{\prime}}(l,\cdot)^{\top}\tilde{V}_{k^{\prime}}(l^{\prime},\cdot)\right\}\right\rVert_{\psi_{1}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\max_{l}\|(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k^{\prime}}^{\ast})_{(m)}(l.\cdot)\|_{2}(3\phi_{2}/2)^{(M-1)/2}\cdot\frac{(3\phi_{2}/2)^{(M-1)/2}}{\phi_{1}^{M/2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\max_{l}\|(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k^{\prime}}^{\ast})_{(m)}(l.\cdot)\|_{2}{2(3\phi_{2}/2)^{M-1}}/{\phi_{1}^{M/2}}.

Similar to the argument used in (
A72
), there exist one positive constant
D_{5}
such that

\max\{|\tilde{{\bm{M}}}_{2}(l,l^{\prime})|,|\tilde{{\bm{M}}}_{3}(l,l^{\prime})|\}\leq\sqrt{{4}/{D_{5}}}\max_{l}\|(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k^{\prime}}^{\ast})_{(m)}(l.\cdot)\|_{2}\frac{(3\phi_{2}/2)^{M-1}}{\phi_{1}^{M/2}}\sqrt{\frac{Td_{m}\log(2/p_{n})}{nd}},

with probability at least
1-p_{n}
. Taking the union bound for all pairs
(l,l^{\prime})
gives

\displaystyle

\displaystyle\max\{|\tilde{{\bm{M}}}_{2}(l,l^{\prime})|,|\tilde{{\bm{M}}}_{3}(l,l^{\prime})|\}

(A118)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{{4}/{D_{5}}}\max_{l}\|(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k^{\prime}}^{\ast})_{(m)}t(l.\cdot)\|_{2}\frac{(3\phi_{2}/2)^{M-1}}{\phi_{1}^{M/2}}\sqrt{\frac{Td_{m}\left\{\log(d_{m})+\log(2/p_{n})\right\}}{nd}},

with probability at least
1-p_{n}
.
Finally, we discuss
\tilde{{\bm{M}}}_{4}(l,l^{\prime})
. By the fact that both
I(Z_{i}=k^{\prime})\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\tilde{V}_{l,j,k^{\prime}}
and
\tilde{V}_{l^{\prime},j,k^{\prime}}
are sub-Gaussian random variables, we have
I(Z_{i}=k^{\prime})\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\tilde{V}_{l,j,k^{\prime}}\tilde{V}_{l^{\prime},j,k^{\prime}}
is sub-exponential with parameter
{(3\phi_{2}/2)^{(M-1)}}/{\phi_{1}^{M}}
.
By Lemma
S4
, there exists some positive constant
D_{6}
such that the following inequality

\mathbb{P}\left(|\tilde{{\bm{M}}}_{4}(l,l^{\prime})|\geq t\right)\leq 2\exp\left(-\frac{D_{6}nt^{2}}{4Td_{m}(3\phi_{2}/2)^{2(M-1)}/\phi_{1}^{2M}}\right),

holds for a sufficiently small
t>0
.
When
n
is sufficiently large, it holds for any pair
(l,l^{\prime})
that

|\tilde{{\bm{M}}}_{4}(l,l^{\prime})|\leq\sqrt{{4}/{D_{5}}}\frac{(3\phi_{2}/2)^{M-1}}{\phi_{1}^{M}}\sqrt{\frac{Td_{m}\left(2\log(d_{m})+\log(2/p_{n})\right)}{nd}},

(A119)

with probability at least
1-p_{n}
.

Combining (
A117
), (
A118
) and (
A119
) together, we get that
