\displaystyle\left\lVert\frac{T}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n/T}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}){\bm{Y}}_{i}{\bm{Y}}_{i}^{\top}-\mathbb{E}\left\{\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}){\bm{Y}}_{i}{\bm{Y}}_{i}^{\top}\right\}\right\rVert_{\max}

\displaystyle\precsim

\displaystyle\sum_{k^{\prime}}\left(\max_{l}\|(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k^{\prime}}^{\ast})_{(m)}(l.\cdot)\|_{2}^{2}\sqrt{d/d_{m}}+1\right)\times\sqrt{\frac{Td_{m}(2\log(d_{m})+\log({2}/{p_{n}}))}{nd}}

with probability at least
1-4Kp_{n}
. Let
p_{n}=1/\{\log(nd)\}^{2}
, the desired result is obtained.

E8
Proof of Lemma
S16

In this proof, we first show the result for
R=1
and then extend it to the general rank. In each of them, the proof can be summarized into two steps. In Step 1, we bound
\|{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime\prime}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\ast}\|_{2}
. In Step 2, we use the result in Step 1 to show that
{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime\prime}
satisfies Condition
2
.

Rank
R=1

Step 1:
First, we have the following lemma and its proof is given in Section
E12
.

Lemma S20
.

Let
\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}
satisfies
\nabla_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}Q(\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast},{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}^{\prime}|{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})=\bm{0}
for
{\bm{\Theta}}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{B}_{\frac{1}{2}}({\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})
, it holds that

\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}/\|\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{2}={\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\ast}
.

By Lemma
1b
, with probability at least
1-1/\{\log(nd)\}^{2}
, it holds for any
k,m
that

\displaystyle\frac{\gamma_{0}}{2}\left\|\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\right\|_{2}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle\underbrace{\left\langle\nabla_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}Q_{n/T}(\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}}),\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\right\rangle}_{(i)}

(A120)

\displaystyle+\underbrace{Q_{n/T}(\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})-Q_{n/T}(\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})}_{(ii)}.

First, we discuss the upper bound of
(i)
. Since
\nabla_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}Q(\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})=0
, we have

\displaystyle(i)

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\left\langle\nabla_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}Q_{n/T}(\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})-\nabla_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}Q(\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}}),\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\right\rangle}_{\text{Statistical Error (SE)}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\left\langle\nabla_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}Q(\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})-\nabla_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}Q(\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast}),\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\right\rangle}_{\text{Optimization Error (OE)}}.

For SE, by Lemma
3b
and letting
\epsilon_{0}=c_{1}\omega_{\max}\sqrt{\log d/n}
, it holds that

\displaystyle\left|\text{SE}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\|\nabla_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}Q_{n/T}(\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})-\nabla_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}Q(\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})\|_{\mathcal{P}_{1}^{\ast}}\mathcal{P}_{1}(\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast})

(A121)

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon_{0}\mathcal{P}_{1}\left(\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\right)

with probability at least
1-{K(2K+1)}/\{\log(nd)\}^{2}
. For OE, by Lemma
2b
, it holds that

\displaystyle\left|\text{OE}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\|\nabla_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}Q(\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})-\nabla_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}Q(\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})\|_{2}\|\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{2}

(A122)

\displaystyle\leq\tau_{0}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})\|\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{2}.

Plugging (
A121
) and (
A122
) into term
(i)
, it arrives that

(i)\leq\epsilon_{0}\mathcal{P}_{1}(\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast})+\tau_{0}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})\left\|\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\right\|_{2}.

(A123)

with probability at least
1-(2K^{2}+K+1)/\{\log(nd)\}^{2}
.

Next, we consider
(ii)
. Since
\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}=\arg\max_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}Q_{n/T}({\bm{\beta}}_{k,m},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})-\lambda_{0}\mathcal{P}_{1}({\bm{\beta}}_{k,m})
, some straightforward algebra gives

Q_{n/T}(\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})-Q_{n/T}(\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})\leq\lambda_{0}\left(\mathcal{P}_{1}(\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast})-\mathcal{P}_{1}(\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m})\right).

(A124)

Let
\mathcal{M}_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}
be the support space of
{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\ast}
and
\mathcal{M}_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}^{\perp}
be the corresponding orthogonal space.
The right-hand side of (
A124
) can be bounded as

\begin{split}&\mathcal{P}_{1}(\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast})-\mathcal{P}_{1}(\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m})\\
=&\mathcal{P}_{1}(\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast})-\mathcal{P}_{1}(\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}+\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast})\\
=&\mathcal{P}_{1}(\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast})-\mathcal{P}_{1}\left((\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast})_{\mathcal{M}_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}}+(\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast})_{\mathcal{M}_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}^{\perp}}+\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\right)\\
=&\mathcal{P}_{1}(\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast})-\mathcal{P}_{1}\left((\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast})_{\mathcal{M}_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}}+\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\right)-\mathcal{P}_{1}\left((\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast})_{\mathcal{M}_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}^{\perp}}\right)\\
\leq&\mathcal{P}_{1}\left((\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast})_{\mathcal{M}_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}}\right)-\mathcal{P}_{1}\left((\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast})_{\mathcal{M}_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}^{\perp}}\right),\end{split}

where the third equality holds due to
\mathcal{P}_{1}\left({\bm{\beta}}\right)=\mathcal{P}_{1}({\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}_{{\bm{\beta}}}})+\mathcal{P}_{1}({\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}_{{\bm{\beta}}}^{\perp}})
and the last inequality follows from the fact that
\mathcal{P}_{1}({\bm{\beta}}_{1}+{\bm{\beta}}_{2})\leq\mathcal{P}_{1}({\bm{\beta}}_{1})+\mathcal{P}_{1}({\bm{\beta}}_{2})
.
Next, it holds that

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}_{1}\left((\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast})_{\mathcal{M}_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}^{\perp}}\right)

(A125)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}_{1}(\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m})-\mathcal{P}(\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast})+\mathcal{P}_{1}\left((\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast})_{\mathcal{M}_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\lambda_{0}}\left(Q_{n/T}(\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})-Q_{n/T}(\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})\right)+\mathcal{P}_{1}\left((\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast})_{\mathcal{M}_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\lambda_{0}}\left\langle\nabla_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}Q_{n/T}(\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}}),\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\right\rangle+\mathcal{P}_{1}\left((\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast})_{\mathcal{M}_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\lambda_{0}}\left\{\epsilon_{0}\mathcal{P}_{1}(\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast})+\tau_{0}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})\left\|\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\right\|_{2}\right\}+\mathcal{P}_{1}\left((\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast})_{\mathcal{M}_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}}\right)

with probability at least
1-(2K^{2}+K+1)/\{\log(nd)\}^{2}
, where the second inequality is from (
A124
), the third inequality is from (
A49
) and the last inequality is a direct result of (
A123
).

Q_{\frac{n}{T}}(\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})-Q_{\frac{n}{T}}(\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})\leq\left\langle\nabla_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}Q_{\frac{n}{T}}(\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}}),\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\right\rangle.

Given that
\lambda_{0}=4\epsilon_{0}+\frac{\tau_{0}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})}{\sqrt{s_{1}}}
, (
A125
) can be written as

3\mathcal{P}_{1}\left((\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast})_{\mathcal{M}_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}^{\perp}}\right)\leq 5\mathcal{P}_{1}\left((\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast})_{\mathcal{M}_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}}\right)+4\sqrt{s_{1}}\left\|\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\right\|_{2}.

(A126)

Then, we have that

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}_{1}(\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast})

(A127)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}_{1}\left((\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast})_{\mathcal{M}_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}}\right)+\mathcal{P}_{1}\left((\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast})_{\mathcal{M}_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}^{\perp}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{8}{3}\mathcal{P}_{1}\left((\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast})_{\mathcal{M}_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}}\right)+\frac{4}{3}\sqrt{s_{1}}\left\|\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\right\|_{2}\leq 4\sqrt{s_{1}}\left\|\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\right\|_{2}.

with probability at least
1-(2K^{2}+K+1)/\{\log(nd)\}^{2}
, where the second inequality is due to (
A126
) and the last inequality is due to

\mathcal{P}_{1}\left((\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast})_{\mathcal{M}_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}}\right)=\left\|(\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast})_{\mathcal{M}_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}}\right\|_{1}\leq\sqrt{s_{1}}\left\|\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\right\|_{2}.

(A128)

Correspondingly, by the choice of
\lambda_{0}
, it holds that

\displaystyle\frac{\gamma_{0}}{2}\left\|\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\right\|_{2}^{2}

(A129)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\epsilon_{0}\mathcal{P}_{1}(\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast})+\tau_{0}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})\left\|\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\right\|_{2}+\lambda_{0}\left\{\mathcal{P}_{1}(\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast})-\mathcal{P}_{1}(\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m})\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\epsilon_{0}\sqrt{s_{1}}\left\|\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\right\|_{2}+\tau_{0}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})\left\|\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle+\lambda_{0}\left\{\mathcal{P}_{1}\left((\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast})_{\mathcal{M}_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}}\right)-\mathcal{P}_{1}\left((\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast})_{\mathcal{M}_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}^{\perp}}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\epsilon_{0}\sqrt{s_{1}}\left\|\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\right\|_{2}+\tau_{0}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})\left\|\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\right\|_{2}+\lambda_{0}\mathcal{P}_{1}\left((\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast})_{\mathcal{M}_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\lambda_{0}\sqrt{s_{1}}\left\|\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\right\|_{2},

with probability at least
1-(2K^{2}+K+1)/\{\log(nd)\}^{2}
, where the first inequality is by (
A123
) and (
A124
).
Dividing both sizes of (
A129
) by
\left\|\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\right\|_{2}
, it follows that

\left\|\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\right\|_{2}\leq\frac{16\sqrt{s_{1}}\epsilon_{0}}{\gamma_{0}}+\frac{4\tau_{0}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})}{\gamma_{0}},

(A130)

with probability at least
1-(2K^{2}+K+1)/\{\log(nd)\}^{2}
. Since
\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}/\|\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{2}={\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\ast}
, we have

\displaystyle\|{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\frac{\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}}{\|\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}\|_{2}}-\frac{\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}}{\|\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{2}}\right\|_{2}

(A131)

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\frac{\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}}{\|\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}\|_{2}}-\frac{\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}}{\|\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}\|_{2}}\right\|_{2}+\left\|\frac{\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}}{\|\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}\|_{2}}-\frac{\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}}{\|\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{2}}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\|\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}\|_{2}}\|\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{2},

where the last inequality uses that

\left\|\frac{\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}}{\|\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}\|_{2}}-\frac{\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}}{\|\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{2}}\right\|_{2}=\|\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{2}\frac{\left|\|\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{2}-\|\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}\|_{2}\right|}{\|\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}\|_{2}\|\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{2}}\leq\frac{1}{\|\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}\|_{2}}\|\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{2}.

Recall
\alpha=D({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})\leq\min\left\{\frac{1}{2},\left(\frac{C_{0}\omega_{\min}}{(R-1)\omega_{\max}}\right)^{\frac{1}{M-1}}\right\}
. Next, we have

\displaystyle\|\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{2}=\frac{\omega_{k}^{\ast}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{\ast})^{\top}\left(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\right)\bar{\omega}_{k}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m^{\prime}})}{\bar{\omega}_{k}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m^{\prime}})^{\top}\left(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\right)\bar{\omega}_{k}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{\omega_{k}^{\ast}}{\bar{\omega}_{k}}\left(\frac{\phi_{1}}{3\phi_{2}}\right)^{M-1}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{\ast})^{\top}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m^{\prime}})

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle(1+\alpha)^{-1}\left(\frac{\phi_{1}}{3\phi_{2}}\right)^{M-1}\prod_{m^{\prime}\neq m}(1-\|\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m^{\prime}}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{\ast}\|_{2})\geq\left(\frac{\phi_{1}}{3\phi_{2}}\right)^{M-1}\frac{(1-\alpha)^{M-1}}{1+\alpha},

where the first inequality uses that
\frac{\phi_{1}}{2}\leq\sigma_{\min}(\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m})\leq\sigma_{\min}(\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m})\leq\frac{3\phi_{2}}{2}
and the last inequality uses the fact in Lemma
S11
.

By (
A130
) and
{\bm{\Theta}}\in\mathcal{B}_{\alpha}({\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})
, we have
\|\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{2}\leq\frac{1}{4}\|\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}\|_{2}+\frac{\alpha}{3\sqrt{K(R+1)(M+1)}}
when
n
is sufficiently large and
\gamma\leq\gamma_{0}
. Thus, there exists one positive constant
C
such that

\|\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}\|_{2}\geq\|\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{2}-\|\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}-\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}\|_{2}\geq 2C.

Plugging this into (
A131
), we have

\left\|{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime\prime}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\right\|_{2}\leq\frac{16\sqrt{s_{1}}\epsilon_{0}}{C\gamma_{0}}+\frac{4\tau_{0}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})}{C\gamma_{0}},

(A132)

with probability at least
1-(2K^{2}+K+1)/\{\log(nd)\}^{2}
.

Step 2:
By Lemmas
1b
-
2b
, we know that

\frac{4\tau_{0}}{C\gamma_{0}}=\frac{\gamma}{3Cc_{0}\sqrt{K(R+1)(M+1)}(1-\alpha)^{2}\omega_{\min}^{2}(\phi_{1}/2)^{M}}.

Letting
\gamma\leq Cc_{0}\sqrt{K(R+1)(M+1)}(1-\alpha)^{2}\omega_{\min}^{2}(\phi_{1}/2)^{M}
, it then follows that
\frac{4\tau_{0}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})}{C\gamma_{0}}\leq\frac{\alpha}{3}
. In addition, when
n/T
is sufficiently large, we get that

\frac{16\sqrt{s_{1}}\epsilon_{0}}{C\gamma_{0}}\leq\frac{64c_{1}\omega_{\max}}{Cc_{0}(\phi_{1}/2)^{M}\omega_{\min}}\cdot\frac{1}{\omega_{\min}}\sqrt{\frac{s_{1}\log d}{n/T}}\leq\frac{2\alpha}{3}.

(A133)

Thus, we have
\left\|{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\prime\prime}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\right\|_{2}\leq\alpha
.

Rank
R>1

Step 1:
Using Lemma
5b
, we can bound the difference
\left\|{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime\prime}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\ast}\right\|_{2}
, where
{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime\prime}={\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,r,m}}/{\|\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,r,m}\|_{2}}
and
\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,r,m}=\arg\max_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}}Q_{n/T}({\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})-\lambda_{0}^{(1)}\|{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}\|_{1}
.
Let
\epsilon_{R,0}=c^{\prime}_{1}\omega_{\max}\sqrt{\frac{\log d}{n/T}}
, where
c^{\prime}_{1}
is as defined in Lemma
7b
.
Similar as in Step 1 for
{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}
for
R=1
, we can get

\left\|{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime\prime}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\ast}\right\|_{2}\leq\frac{16\sqrt{s_{1}}\epsilon_{R,0}}{C\gamma_{0}^{\prime}}+\frac{4\tau_{0}^{\prime}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})}{C\gamma_{0}^{\prime}},

with probability at least
1-(2K^{2}+K+1)/\{\log(nd)\}^{2}
.

Step 2:
By Lemmas
5b
-
6b
and
\gamma\leq{C_{1}}/{d_{\max}}
, it is easy to verify that

\frac{4\tau_{0}^{\prime}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})}{C\gamma_{0}^{\prime}}\leq\frac{\alpha}{3}
. In addition, when
n
is sufficiently large,
we have

\frac{16\sqrt{s_{1}}\epsilon_{R,0}}{C\gamma_{0}^{\prime}}\leq\frac{64c_{1}^{\prime}\omega_{\max}}{Cc_{0}(\phi_{1}/2)^{M}\omega_{\min}+(R-1)(\xi+2c_{\alpha}+c_{\alpha}^{2})\omega_{\min}}\cdot\frac{1}{\omega_{\min}}\sqrt{\frac{s_{1}\log d}{n/T}}\leq\frac{2\alpha}{3}.

(A134)

Thus, we have
\left\|{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\prime\prime}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\ast}\right\|_{2}\leq\alpha
.

E9
Proof of Lemma
S17

In this proof, we first show the result for
R=1
and then extend it to the general rank. In each of them, the proof can be summarized into two steps. In Step 1, we bound
|\omega_{k}^{\prime\prime}-\omega_{k}^{\ast}|
. In Step 2, we use the result in Step 1 to show that
\omega_{k}^{\prime\prime}
satisfies Condition
2
.

Rank
R=1
:

Step 1:
It holds from Lemma
1b
to obtain that

\displaystyle\frac{\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}{2}|\omega_{k}^{\prime\prime}-\omega_{k}^{\ast}|^{2}\leq

\displaystyle\underbrace{\left\langle\nabla_{\omega_{k}}Q_{n/T}(\omega_{k}^{\ast},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-\omega_{k}}|{\bm{\Theta}}),\omega_{k}^{\prime\prime}-\omega_{k}^{\ast}\right\rangle}_{(iii)}+\underbrace{Q_{n/T}(\omega_{k}^{\ast},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-\omega_{k}}|{\bm{\Theta}})-Q_{n/T}(\omega_{k}^{\prime\prime},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-\omega_{k}}|{\bm{\Theta}})}_{(iv)},

(A135)

with probability at least
1-1/\{\log(nd)\}^{2}
.
We will bound terms
(iii)
and
(iv)
respectively. Since that
\omega_{k}^{\prime\prime}
is the maximizer, we have

Q_{n/T}(\omega_{k}^{\prime\prime},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-\omega_{k}}|{\bm{\Theta}})\geq Q_{n/T}(\omega_{k}^{\ast},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-\omega_{k}}|{\bm{\Theta}})
,
which implies that

(iv)=Q_{n/T}(\omega_{k}^{\ast},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-\omega_{k}}|{\bm{\Theta}})-Q_{n/T}(\omega_{k}^{\prime\prime},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-\omega_{k}}|{\bm{\Theta}})\leq 0.

(A136)

Let
\epsilon^{\prime\prime}_{0}=c^{\prime\prime}_{1}\omega_{\max}\sqrt{\log\log(nd)/n}
, where
c^{\prime\prime}_{1}
is as defined in Lemma
3b
. Similar to
(i)
, we can get that

\displaystyle(iii)=

\displaystyle\left\langle\nabla_{\omega_{k}}Q_{n/T}(\omega_{k}^{\ast},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-\omega_{k}}|{\bm{\Theta}})-\nabla_{\omega_{k}}Q(\omega_{k}^{\ast},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-\omega_{k}}|{\bm{\Theta}}),\omega_{k}^{\prime\prime}-\omega_{k}^{\ast}\right\rangle

(A137)

\displaystyle+\left\langle\nabla_{\omega_{k}}Q(\omega_{k}^{\ast},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-\omega_{k}}|{\bm{\Theta}})-\nabla_{\omega_{k}}Q(\omega_{k}^{\ast},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-\omega_{k}}|{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast}),\omega_{k}^{\prime\prime}-\omega_{k}^{\ast}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\left\langle\nabla_{\omega_{k}}Q(\omega_{k}^{\ast},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-\omega_{k}}|{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast}),\omega_{k}^{\prime\prime}-\omega_{k}^{\ast}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\epsilon_{0}^{\prime\prime}|\omega_{k}^{\prime\prime}-\omega_{k}^{\ast}|+\tau_{0}^{\prime\prime}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})|\omega_{k}^{\prime\prime}-\omega_{k}^{\ast}|+\left\langle\nabla_{\omega_{k}}Q(\omega_{k}^{\ast},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-\omega_{k}}|{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast}),\omega_{k}^{\prime\prime}-\omega_{k}^{\ast}\right\rangle

with probability at least
1-(K+1)/\{\log(nd)\}^{2}
.
By (
A55
) and (
A156
), we have that

\displaystyle|\nabla_{\omega_{k}}Q(\omega_{k}^{\ast},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-\omega_{k}}|{\bm{\Theta}})|

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left\{\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})\right\}\omega_{k}^{\ast}\left|\left\{\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\ast})-\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m})\right\}^{\top}\left(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\right)\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m})\right|

\displaystyle\leq\omega_{k}^{\ast}(3\phi_{2}/2)^{M-1}\left\{\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\ast})-\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m})\right\}^{\top}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m})

\displaystyle\leq\omega_{k}^{\ast}(3\phi_{2}/2)^{M-1}\sqrt{M}\sum_{m}\|\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{2},

with probability at least
1-(K+1)/\{\log(nd)\}^{2}
, where the first inequality is obtained by
\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime}\|_{2}\leq\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{2}+\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime}-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{2}\leq 3\phi_{2}/2
, the second inequality follows from the fact in Lemma
S11
.

Combining (
A137
) and (
A136
), we can get that, for any
k
,

\frac{|\omega_{k}^{\prime\prime}-\omega_{k}^{\ast}|}{|\omega_{k}^{\ast}|}\leq\frac{2\epsilon_{0}^{\prime\prime}}{\omega_{k}^{\ast}\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}+\frac{2\tau_{0}^{\prime\prime}}{\omega_{k}^{\ast}\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})+2(3\phi_{2}/2)^{M-1}\sqrt{M}\sum_{m}\|\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{2},

with probability at least
1-(2K^{2}+2K+1)/\{\log(nd)\}^{2}
.

Step 2:
Let
\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}
are newly updated from Lemma
S16
, we can obtain that

\displaystyle\frac{|\omega_{k}^{\prime\prime}-\omega_{k}^{\ast}|}{|\omega_{k}^{\ast}|}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\epsilon_{0}^{\prime\prime}}{\omega_{k}^{\ast}\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}+\frac{2\tau_{0}^{\prime\prime}}{\omega_{k}^{\ast}\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})+2(3\phi_{2}/2)^{M-1}\sqrt{M}\sum_{m}\|\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{2}

(A138)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\epsilon_{0}^{\prime\prime}}{\omega_{k}^{\ast}\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}+\frac{2\tau_{0}^{\prime\prime}}{\omega_{k}^{\ast}\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})+\frac{2(3\phi_{2}/2)^{M-1}M^{3/2}}{\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}\left\{\frac{16\sqrt{s_{1}}\epsilon_{0}}{C\gamma_{0}}+\frac{4\tau_{0}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})}{C\gamma_{0}}\right\}

with probability at least
1-(2K^{2}+2K+1)/\{\log(nd)\}^{2}
.
Setting
\gamma
as a sufficiently small constant and then we have

\left\{\frac{2\tau_{0}^{\prime\prime}}{\omega_{k}^{\ast}\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}+\frac{2(3\phi_{2}/2)^{M-1}M^{3/2}}{\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}\frac{4\tau_{0}}{C\gamma_{0}}\right\}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})\leq\frac{1}{3}\alpha.

Also, when
n/T
is sufficiently large, we have

\displaystyle\frac{2\epsilon_{0}^{\prime\prime}}{\omega_{k}^{\ast}\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}+\frac{2(3\phi_{2}/2)^{M-1}M^{3/2}}{\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}\frac{16\sqrt{s_{1}}\epsilon_{0}}{C\gamma_{0}}

(A139)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2^{M+1}c_{1}\omega_{\max}}{c_{0}\phi_{1}^{M}\omega_{\min}}\sqrt{\frac{\log(2/\{\log(nd)\}^{2})}{n/T}}+\frac{2^{M+9}M^{3/2}(3\phi_{2})^{M-1}}{c_{0}^{2}C\phi_{1}^{2M}}\cdot\frac{1}{\omega_{\min}}\sqrt{\frac{s_{1}\log d}{n/T}}\leq\frac{2\alpha}{3}.

Thus, we have
\frac{|\omega_{k}^{\prime\prime}-\omega_{k}^{\ast}|}{|\omega_{k}^{\ast}|}\leq\alpha
.

Rank
R>1
:

Step 1:
For
\omega_{k,r}
, Lemmas
1b
-
3b
still holds for the general rank case. By (
A135
), (
A136
) and (
A137
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}{2}|\omega_{k,r}^{\prime\prime}-\omega_{k,r}^{\ast}|^{2}\leq\epsilon_{0}^{\prime\prime}|\omega_{k,r}^{\prime\prime}-\omega_{k,r}^{\ast}|

\displaystyle+\tau_{0}^{\prime\prime}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})|\omega_{k}^{\prime\prime}-\omega_{k}^{\ast}|

\displaystyle+\left\langle\nabla_{\omega_{k,r}}Q(\omega_{k,r}^{\ast},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-\omega_{k,r}}|{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast}),\omega_{k,r}^{\prime\prime}-\omega_{k,r}^{\ast}\right\rangle

with probability at least
1-(K+1)/\{\log(nd)\}^{2}
. By (
A55
) and (
A156
), we have that

\displaystyle|\nabla_{\omega_{k,r}}Q(\omega_{k,r}^{\ast},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-\omega_{k,r}}|{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})|

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left\{\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})\right\}\omega_{k,r}^{\ast}\left|\left\{\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\ast})-\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m})\right\}^{\top}\left(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\right)\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m})\right|}_{A_{1}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left\{\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})\right\}\left|\sum_{r^{\prime}\neq r}\left\{\omega_{k,r^{\prime}}^{\ast}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r^{\prime},m}^{\ast})-\bar{\omega}_{k,r^{\prime}}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r^{\prime},m})\right\}^{\top}\left(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}\right)\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m})\right|}_{A_{2}}.

Similar as in Step 1 for
\omega_{k}
for
R=1
,
A_{1}\leq\omega_{k,r}^{\ast}(3\phi_{2}/2)^{M-1}M^{1/2}\sum_{m}\|\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\ast}\|_{2}
,
with probability at least
1-1/\{\log(nd)\}^{2}
. Since
\mathbb{E}\left\{\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})\right\}\leq 1
and
\|\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}\|_{2}\leq 3\phi_{2}/2
, it holds that

\displaystyle A_{2}

\displaystyle\leq(R-1)(3\phi_{2}/2)^{M}\max_{r^{\prime}\neq r}\left|\left\{\omega_{k,r^{\prime}}^{\ast}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r^{\prime},m}^{\ast})-\bar{\omega}_{k,r^{\prime}}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r^{\prime},m})\right\}^{\top}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m})\right|

\displaystyle\leq(R-1)(3\phi_{2}/2)^{M}\max_{r^{\prime}\neq r}\underbrace{\left|\left\{\omega_{k,r^{\prime}}^{\ast}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r^{\prime},m}^{\ast})-\bar{\omega}_{k,r^{\prime}}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r^{\prime},m})\right\}^{\top}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\ast})\right|}_{A_{21}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{2(R-1)(3\phi_{2}/2)^{M}(1+\alpha)\omega_{\max}\|\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m})-\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\ast})\|_{2}.}_{A_{22}},

where the second inequality uses the fact that
\|\omega_{k,r^{\prime}}^{\ast}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r^{\prime},m}^{\ast})-\bar{\omega}_{k,r^{\prime}}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r^{\prime},m})\|_{2}\leq 2(1+\alpha)\omega_{\max}
. Similar as in
A_{1}
, we can get that

A_{22}\leq 2(R-1)(3\phi_{2}/2)^{M-1}M^{1/2}(1+\alpha)\omega_{\max}\sum_{m}\|\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\ast}\|_{2}.

By (
A87
), we have that

\displaystyle A_{21}

\displaystyle=\bar{\omega}_{k,r^{\prime}}\left|\prod_{m}\langle{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r^{\prime},m}^{\ast},{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\ast}\rangle-\prod_{m}\langle\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r^{\prime},m},{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\ast}\rangle\right|+|\omega_{k,r^{\prime}}^{\ast}-\bar{\omega}_{k,r^{\prime}}|\prod_{m}|\langle{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r^{\prime},m}^{\ast},{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\ast}\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq\max\{\xi^{M},\xi^{M-1}(1+\alpha),(\alpha+\xi)^{M-1}(1+\alpha)\}\omega_{\max}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast}).

This is true, because
\prod_{m}|\langle{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r^{\prime},m}^{\ast},{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\ast}\rangle|\leq\xi^{M}
and

\left|\prod_{m}\langle{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r^{\prime},m}^{\ast},{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\ast}\rangle-\prod_{m}\langle\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r^{\prime},m},{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\ast}\rangle\right|\leq\max\{\xi^{M-1},(\alpha+\xi)^{M-1}\}\sum_{m}\|\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{2},

which can be verified as follows in the case of
M=3
while general cases follow similarly.
Since
|\langle{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r^{\prime},m}^{\ast},{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\ast}\rangle|\leq\xi
and
|\langle\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r^{\prime},m},{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\ast}\rangle|\leq\xi+\alpha
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\prod_{m}\langle{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r^{\prime},m}^{\ast},{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\ast}\rangle-\prod_{m}\langle\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r^{\prime},m},{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\ast}\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|\langle\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r^{\prime},1}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r^{\prime},1}^{\ast},{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,1}^{\ast}\rangle\langle{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r^{\prime},2}^{\ast},{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,2}^{\ast}\rangle\langle{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r^{\prime},3}^{\ast},{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,3}^{\ast}\rangle|

\displaystyle+|\langle{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r^{\prime},1}^{\ast},{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,1}^{\ast}\rangle\langle\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r^{\prime},2}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r^{\prime},2}^{\ast},{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,2}^{\ast}\rangle\langle{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r^{\prime},3}^{\ast},{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,3}^{\ast}\rangle|

\displaystyle+|\langle{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r^{\prime},1}^{\ast},{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,1}^{\ast}\rangle\langle{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r^{\prime},2}^{\ast},{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,2}^{\ast}\rangle\langle\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r^{\prime},3}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r^{\prime},3}^{\ast},{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,3}^{\ast}\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max\{\xi^{2},\xi(\alpha+\xi),(\alpha+\xi)^{2}\}\sum_{m}\|\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r^{\prime},m}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r^{\prime},m}^{\ast}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\max\{\xi^{M-1},(\alpha+\xi)^{M-1}\}\sum_{m}\|\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{2}.

Correspondingly, term
A_{2}
can be bounded by

A_{2}\leq\tau_{0}^{\prime\prime\prime}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})+2(R-1)(3\phi_{2}/2)^{M-1}M^{3/2}(1+\alpha)\omega_{\max}\sum_{m}\|\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\ast}\|_{2}.

where
\tau_{0}^{\prime\prime\prime}=\omega_{\max}(R-1)(3\phi_{2}/2)^{M}\max\{\xi^{M},\xi^{M-1}(1+\alpha),(\alpha+\xi)^{M-1}(1+\alpha)\}
.
Now we can conclude that

\displaystyle|\nabla_{\omega_{k,r}}Q(\omega_{k,r}^{\ast},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-\omega_{k,r}}|{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})|\leq\frac{R(3\phi_{2})^{M-1}}{2^{M-2}}\sqrt{M}(1+\alpha)\omega_{\max}\sum_{m}\|\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\ast}\|_{2}+\tau_{0}^{\prime\prime\prime}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast}).

Combining (
A137
) and (
A136
), we can get that, for any
k
,

\displaystyle\frac{|\omega_{k,r}^{\prime\prime}-\omega_{k,r}^{\ast}|}{|\omega_{k,r}^{\ast}|}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2\epsilon_{0}^{\prime\prime}}{\omega_{k,r}^{\ast}\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}+\frac{2(\tau_{0}^{\prime\prime}+\tau_{0}^{\prime\prime\prime})}{\omega_{k,r}^{\ast}\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})

\displaystyle+\frac{2R(3\phi_{2}/2)^{M-1}M^{1/2}(1+\alpha)}{\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}\sum_{m}\|\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\ast}\|_{2}

with probability at least
1-(2K^{2}+2K+1)/\{\log(nd)\}^{2}
.

Step 2:
Similarly, let
\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}
are newly updated from Lemma
S16
, we can obtain that

\displaystyle\frac{|\omega_{k,r}^{\prime\prime}-\omega_{k,r}^{\ast}|}{|\omega_{k,r}^{\ast}|}\leq\frac{2\epsilon_{0}^{\prime\prime}}{\omega_{k,r}^{\ast}\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}+\frac{2(\tau_{0}^{\prime\prime}+\tau_{0}^{\prime\prime\prime})}{\omega_{k,r}^{\ast}\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}\alpha+\frac{2R(3\phi_{2}/2)^{M-1}M^{3/2}(1+\alpha)}{\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}\sum_{m}\|\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}^{\ast}\|_{2}

(A140)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2\epsilon_{0}^{\prime\prime}}{\omega_{k,r}^{\ast}\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}+\frac{2(\tau_{0}^{\prime\prime}+\tau_{0}^{\prime\prime\prime})}{\omega_{k,r}^{\ast}\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}\alpha+\frac{2R(3\phi_{2}/2)^{M-1}M^{3/2}(1+\alpha)}{\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}\left\{\frac{16\sqrt{s_{1}}\epsilon_{R,0}}{C\gamma_{0}^{\prime}}+\frac{4\tau_{0}^{\prime}\alpha}{C\gamma_{0}^{\prime}}\right\},

with probability at least
1-(2K^{2}+2K+1)/\{\log(nd)\}^{2}
.
Noting
\gamma\leq{C_{1}}/{d_{\max}}
and letting
C_{0}=\min\left\{\frac{c_{0}\phi_{1}^{m}}{9\phi_{2}^{M}},\sqrt{\frac{R-1}{9R}}\right\}
and
\alpha\leq\left(\frac{C_{0}\omega_{\min}}{(R-1)\omega_{\max}}\right)^{\frac{1}{M-1}}
, we have

\left\{\frac{2\tau_{0}^{\prime\prime}}{\omega_{k,r}^{\ast}\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}+\frac{2R(3\phi_{2}/2)^{M-1}M^{3/2}}{\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}\frac{4\tau^{\prime}_{0}}{C\gamma^{\prime}_{0}}+\frac{2\tau_{0}^{\prime\prime\prime}}{\omega_{k,r}^{\ast}\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}\right\}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})\leq(\frac{1}{3}+\frac{1}{6})\alpha=\frac{1}{2}\alpha.

When
n
is large enough, we can get that

\displaystyle\frac{2\epsilon_{0}^{\prime\prime}}{\omega_{k,r}^{\ast}\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}+\frac{2R(3\phi_{2}/2)^{M-1}M^{3/2}}{\gamma_{0}^{\prime\prime}}\frac{16\sqrt{s_{1}}\epsilon_{R,0}}{C\gamma_{0}^{\prime}}\leq\frac{1}{2}\alpha.

(A141)

Correspondingly, we have that
{|\omega_{k,r}^{\prime\prime}-\omega_{k,r}^{\ast}|}/{|\omega_{k,r}^{\ast}|}\leq\alpha
.

E10
Proof of Lemma
S18

This proof can be summarized into two steps. In Step 1, we bound
\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime\prime}-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{\text{F}}
. In Step 2, we use the result in Step 1 to show that
{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime\prime}
satisfies Condition
2
.

Step 1 for
{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}
:

By Lemma
1b
, with probability at least
1-\{\log(nd)\}^{2}
, it holds for any
k
and
m
that

\displaystyle\frac{\gamma_{m}}{2}\left\|\tilde{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m}-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\right\|_{2}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle\underbrace{\left\langle\nabla_{{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}Q_{n/T}({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}}),\tilde{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m}-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\right\rangle}_{(v)}

(A142)

\displaystyle+\underbrace{Q_{n/T}({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})-Q_{n/T}(\tilde{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})}_{(vi)}.

First, we consider term
(v)
. Letting
\epsilon_{m}=c_{2}(d/d_{m})\sqrt{\log d/n}
, it holds that

\displaystyle(v)=

\displaystyle\left\langle\nabla_{{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}Q_{n/T}({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})-\nabla_{{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}Q({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}}),\tilde{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m}-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\left\langle\nabla_{{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}Q({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}}^{\prime})-\nabla_{{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}Q({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast}),\tilde{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m}-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\left\langle\nabla_{{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}Q({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast}),\tilde{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m}-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon_{m}

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}_{2}(\tilde{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m}-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast})+\tau_{1}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})\left\|\tilde{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m}-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\right\|_{\text{F}}

\displaystyle+\left\langle\nabla_{{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}Q({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast}),\tilde{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m}-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\right\rangle,

with probability at least
1-(8K^{2}+2K+1)/\{\log(nd)\}^{2}
, where the last inequality holds due to Lemmas
2b
-
3b
.
Our analysis is based on the next lemma.

Lemma S21
.

Let
Q({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast},{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}^{\prime}|{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})
be the population level of (
7
), we have

\nabla_{{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}Q({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast},{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}^{\prime}|{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})-\nabla_{{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}Q({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast},{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}^{\ast}|{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})=A_{1}/2+A_{2}+A_{3},

where

\displaystyle A_{1}=\mathbb{E}\left[\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}^{\prime}\right)_{(m)}\left(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{\prime}-\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{\ast}\right)\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}^{\prime}\right)_{(m)}^{\top}\right],

\displaystyle A_{2}=\mathbb{E}\left[\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\right)_{(m)}\Big(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{\ast}\Big)\left(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}^{\prime}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}^{\ast}\right)_{(m)}^{\top}\right],

\displaystyle A_{3}=\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})\left\{\left(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}^{\ast}\right)_{(m)}\Big(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{\ast}\Big)\left(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}^{\ast}\right)_{(m)}^{\top}-(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}^{\prime})_{(m)}\Big(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{\ast}\Big)(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}^{\prime})_{(m)}^{\top}\right\}\right].

Results in this lemma are directly obtained using the following result and is thus omitted. Here
Q({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast},{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}^{\prime}|{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})
is written as

\mathbb{E}\left[\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})\left\{\frac{d}{2d_{m}}({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast})^{-1}-\frac{1}{2}\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}^{\prime}\right)_{(m)}\left(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{\prime}\right)\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}^{\prime}\right)_{(m)}^{\top}\right\}\right].

We discuss these three terms
A_{1}
,
A_{2}
and
A_{3}
, respectively. First, we claim that

A_{1}/\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})=o(d).

(A143)

This claim is shown in Section
E10.1
.
Next, we consider terms
A_{2}
and
A_{3}
. By (
A156
),
A_{2}
can be written as

\displaystyle A_{2}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\right)_{(m)}\right]\Big(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{\ast}\Big)\left(\bar{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{k}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}^{\ast}\right)_{(m)}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})\right]\left(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}^{\ast}\right)_{(m)}\Big(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{\ast}\Big)\left(\bar{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{k}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}^{\ast}\right)_{(m)}^{\top}.

Combining
A_{2}
and
A_{3}
, we have that

A_{2}+A_{3}=-\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})\right]\left(\bar{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{k}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}^{\ast}\right)_{(m)}\Big(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{\ast}\Big)\left(\bar{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{k}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}^{\ast}\right)_{(m)}^{\top}.

Recall that
\bar{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{k}=\bar{\omega}_{k}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,1}\circ\cdots\circ\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,M}
and
\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}^{\ast}=\omega_{k}^{\ast}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,1}^{\ast}\circ\cdots\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{k,M}^{\ast}
. Letting
\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}^{\prime\prime}=\omega_{k}^{\ast}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,1}\circ\cdots\circ\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,M}
, we have

\displaystyle\|A_{2}+A_{3}\|_{\text{F}}\leq\frac{1}{2}\left\|\left(\bar{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{k}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}^{\prime\prime}+\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}^{\prime\prime}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}^{\ast}\right)_{(m)}\Big(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{\ast}\Big)\left(\bar{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{k}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}^{\prime\prime}+\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}^{\prime\prime}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}^{\ast}\right)_{(m)}^{\top}\right\|_{\text{F}}

(A144)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(\omega_{k}^{\ast})^{2}\left\|\Big(\prod_{m^{\prime}}^{\circ}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m^{\prime}}-\prod_{m^{\prime}}^{\circ}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{\ast}\Big)_{(m)}\Big(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{\ast}\Big)\Big(\prod_{m^{\prime}}^{\circ}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m^{\prime}}-\prod_{m^{\prime}}^{\circ}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{\ast}\Big)_{(m)}^{\top}\right\|_{\text{F}}

\displaystyle+(\bar{\omega}_{k}-\omega_{k}^{\ast})^{2}\left\|\Big(\prod_{m^{\prime}}^{\circ}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\Big)_{(m)}\Big(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{\ast}\Big)\Big(\prod_{m^{\prime}}^{\circ}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\Big)_{(m)}^{\top}\right\|_{\text{F}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(\omega_{k}^{\ast})^{2}\phi_{2}^{M-1}\left\|\prod_{m^{\prime}}^{\circ}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m^{\prime}}-\prod_{m^{\prime}}^{\circ}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{\ast}\right\|_{\text{F}}^{2}+(\bar{\omega}_{k}-\omega_{k}^{\ast})^{2}\phi_{2}^{M-1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(\omega_{k}^{\ast})^{2}\phi_{2}^{M-1}(\sum_{m^{\prime}}\left\|\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m^{\prime}}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{\ast}\right\|_{2})^{2}+(\bar{\omega}_{k}-\omega_{k}^{\ast})^{2}\phi_{2}^{M-1},

where the first inequality uses the fact that that
2\bm{a}_{1}^{\top}{\bf B}\bm{a}_{1}+2\bm{a}_{2}^{\top}{\bf B}\bm{a}_{2}-(\bm{a}_{1}+\bm{a}_{2})^{\top}{\bf B}(\bm{a}_{1}+\bm{a}_{2})=(\bm{a}_{1}-\bm{a}_{2})^{\top}{\bf B}(\bm{a}_{1}-\bm{a}_{2})\geq 0
for non-negative definite matrix
{\bf B}
, the second inequality is due to the fact that
\|{\bm{A}}{\bf B}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\leq\|{\bm{A}}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\|{\bf B}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}
for any matrix
{\bm{A}},{\bf B}\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times n}
and the last inequality uses the fact in Lemma
S11
. We claim that
\omega_{k}^{\ast}\leq\prod_{m}{s_{k,m}^{1/2}}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}^{\ast}\|_{\max}=o(d^{1/2})
, where
s_{k,m}=\|{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}\|_{0}
. This can be obtained by the definition of F norm in tensor and Condition
1
. Since
\|{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{2}=1
for any
m
, we get that
\omega_{k}^{\ast}=\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}^{\ast}\|_{F}
.
Next, there are at most
\prod_{m}s_{k,m}
nonzero entries in
\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}^{\ast}
. By the definition of the Frobenius norm in tensors, we have
\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}^{\ast}\|_{F}^{2}\leq{\prod_{m}s_{k,m}}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}^{\ast}\|_{\max}^{2}
and we get that
\omega_{k}^{\ast}\leq{\prod_{m}s_{k,m}^{1/2}}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}^{\ast}\|_{\max}
.
By Condition 1, we know that
\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}^{\ast}\|_{\max}=O(1)
. With the fact that
\prod_{m}s_{k,m}=o(d)
, the last equality holds. Given that
\left\|\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m^{\prime}}-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{\ast}\right\|_{2}\leq\alpha
,
\frac{\omega_{k}^{\prime}-\omega_{k}^{\ast}}{\omega_{k}^{\ast}}\leq\alpha
and
\omega_{k}^{\ast}\leq{\prod_{m}s_{k,m}^{1/2}}\|\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}^{\ast}\|_{\max}=o(d^{1/2})
, we have that
\|A_{2}+A_{3}\|_{\text{F}}/\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})=o(d)
. Together with
A_{1}
, we have that

\|\nabla_{{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}Q({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})-\nabla_{{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}Q({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast},{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}^{\ast}|{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})\|_{\text{F}}/\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})=o(d).

As
\tau_{1}=O(d)
, it then holds that

\|\nabla_{{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}Q({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})-\nabla_{{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}Q({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast},{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}^{\ast}|{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})\|_{\text{F}}\leq\frac{\tau_{1}}{2}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast}).

Next, consider term
(vi)
. Recall that
(vi)=Q_{\frac{n}{T}}({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})-Q_{\frac{n}{T}}(\tilde{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})
and similar as term
(ii)
in Step 1 for
{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}
, it can be bounded by

Q_{n/T}({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})-Q_{n/T}(\tilde{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m},\bar{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}}|{\bm{\Theta}})\leq\lambda_{m}^{(1)}\left(\mathcal{P}_{2}({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast})-\mathcal{P}_{2}(\tilde{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m})\right).

(A145)

Given
\lambda_{m}^{(1)}=4\epsilon_{m}+\frac{3\tau_{1}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})}{2\sqrt{s_{2}+d_{m}}}
, similar arguments as (
A130
) give

\left\|\tilde{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m}-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\right\|_{\text{F}}\leq\frac{16\sqrt{s_{2}+d_{m}}\epsilon_{m}}{\gamma_{m}}+\frac{6\tau_{1}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})}{\gamma_{m}},

(A146)

with probability at least
1-(8K^{2}+2K+1)/\{\log(nd)\}^{2}
, and
\gamma_{m}=c_{0}(2\phi_{2})^{-2}d/d_{m}
. Since
{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime\prime}=\sqrt{d_{m}}\tilde{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m}/\|\tilde{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m}\|_{2}
and
\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{\text{F}}=\sqrt{d_{m}}
, we get that

\displaystyle\frac{\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime\prime}-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{\text{F}}}{\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{\text{F}}}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\frac{\tilde{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m}}{\|\tilde{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m}\|_{\text{F}}}-\frac{{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}}{\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{\text{F}}}\right\|_{\text{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\frac{\tilde{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m}}{\|\tilde{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m}\|_{2}}-\frac{{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}}{\|\tilde{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m}\|_{\text{F}}}\right\|_{\text{F}}+\left\|\frac{{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}}{\|\tilde{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m}\|_{\text{F}}}-\frac{{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}}{\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{\text{F}}}\right\|_{\text{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\|\tilde{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m}\|_{\text{F}}}\|\tilde{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m}-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{\text{F}}.

The last inequality uses that

\left\|\frac{{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}}{\|\tilde{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m}\|_{\text{F}}}-\frac{{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}}{\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{\text{F}}}\right\|_{\text{F}}=\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{\text{F}}\left|\frac{\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{\text{F}}-\|\tilde{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m}\|_{\text{F}}}{\|\tilde{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m}\|_{\text{F}}\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{\text{F}}}\right|\leq\frac{1}{\|\tilde{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m}\|_{\text{F}}}\|\tilde{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m}-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{\text{F}}.

By (
A146
) and
{\bm{\Theta}}
lies in the specific ball, we have
\|\tilde{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m}-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{\text{F}}\leq\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{\text{F}}/4+\frac{\alpha}{2\sqrt{K(R+1)(M+1)}}
when
n
is sufficiently large and
\gamma\leq\gamma_{m}
. Note that
\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{\text{F}}=\sqrt{d_{m}}
, then there exists a positive constant
C^{\prime}
such that
\|\tilde{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m}\|_{\text{F}}\geq 2C^{\prime}\sqrt{d_{m}}
. Now we can claim that

\frac{\left\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime\prime}-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\right\|_{\text{F}}}{\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{\text{F}}}\leq\frac{16\sqrt{s_{2}+d_{m}}\epsilon_{m}}{C^{\prime}\sqrt{d_{m}}\gamma_{m}}+\frac{6\tau_{1}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})}{C^{\prime}\sqrt{d_{m}}\gamma_{m}},

(A147)

with probability at least
1-(8K^{2}+2K+1)/\{\log(nd)\}^{2}
.

Step 2:

Since
\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime\prime}-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{2}\leq\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime\prime}-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{\text{F}}
, we have

\frac{\left\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime\prime}-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\right\|_{2}}{\sigma_{\min}({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast})}\leq\frac{16\sqrt{s_{2}+d_{m}}\epsilon_{m}}{C^{\prime}\phi_{1}\gamma_{m}}+\frac{6\tau_{1}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})}{C^{\prime}\phi_{1}\gamma_{m}}.

Let
\gamma\leq\frac{C^{\prime}\phi_{1}\sqrt{K(R+1)(M+1)}}{54\phi_{2}^{2}d_{m}}
and then we have
\frac{6\tau_{1}\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})}{C^{\prime}\phi_{1}\gamma_{m}}\leq\frac{1}{3}\alpha
.
Also, when
n/T
is sufficiently large, it holds that

\frac{16\sqrt{s_{2}+d_{m}}\epsilon_{m}}{C^{\prime}\phi_{1}\gamma_{m}}\leq\frac{16c_{2}\sqrt{s_{2}+d_{m}}}{C^{\prime}c_{0}\phi_{1}(6\phi_{2})^{-2}\alpha}\sqrt{\frac{\log d}{n/T}}\leq\frac{2\alpha}{3}.

(A148)

Thus, we have
\frac{\left\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime\prime}-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\right\|_{2}}{\sigma_{\min}({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast})}\leq\alpha
.
It then follows that
\frac{\left\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\prime\prime}-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\right\|_{\text{F}}}{\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{\text{F}}}\leq\alpha
, as
\|{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{\text{F}}=\sqrt{d_{m}}
and
\sigma_{\min}({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast})
is bounded below by a positive constant
\phi_{1}
.

E10.1
Proof of claim (
A143
)

By Lemma
S21
,
A_{1}
can be written as

A_{1}=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}\left\{\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}-\bar{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{k}\right)_{(m)}\left(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}-\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{\ast}\right)\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}-\bar{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{k}\right)_{(m)}^{\top}\Big|Z_{i}\right\}\right].

If
Z_{i}=k^{\prime}
, then
\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\sim\mathcal{N}_{T}(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k^{\prime}}^{\ast};\underline{{\bm{\Sigma}}}_{k^{\prime}}^{\ast})
.
We may write

\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}=\sum_{k^{\prime}=1}^{K}I(Z_{i}=k^{\prime})(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k^{\prime}}^{\ast}+\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{k^{\prime}}),

where
\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{k^{\prime}}\sim\mathcal{N}_{T}({\bf 0};\underline{{\bm{\Sigma}}}_{k}^{\ast})
.
Correspondingly,
A_{1}
can be expressed as

\displaystyle A_{1}=

\displaystyle\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left\{\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})^{2}\left(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{k}+\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}^{\ast}-\bar{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{k}\right)_{(m)}\left(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}-\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{\ast}\right)\left(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{k}+\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}^{\ast}-\bar{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{k}\right)_{(m)}^{\top}\right\}}_{A_{11}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\sum_{l\neq k}\mathbb{E}\left\{\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})\tau_{il}({\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}^{\ast}\right)_{(m)}\left(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}-\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{\ast}\right)\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}^{\ast}\right)_{(m)}^{\top}\right\}.}_{A_{12}}

We first bound
A_{11}
. This term can be further written as
A_{11}=A_{111}+A_{112}
, where

\displaystyle A_{111}=\mathbb{E}\left\{\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})^{2}\left(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{k}\right)_{(m)}\left(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}-\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{\ast}\right)\left(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{k}\right)_{(m)}^{\top}\right\},

\displaystyle A_{112}=\mathbb{E}\left\{\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})^{2}\left(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}^{\ast}-\bar{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{k}\right)_{(m)}\left(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}-\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{\ast}\right)\left(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}^{\ast}-\bar{\bm{\mathcal{U}}}_{k}\right)_{(m)}^{\top}\right\}.

Let
{\bm{Y}}=({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast})^{1/2}\left(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{k}\right)_{(m)}\left(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}({\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{\ast})^{1/2}\right)
, where
{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}=({\bm{\Sigma}}_{k,m}^{\ast})^{-1}
. Now
A_{111}
can be bounded by

\|A_{111}\|_{\text{F}}\leq\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[({\bm{\Sigma}}_{k,m}^{\ast})^{1/2}{\bm{Y}}{\bm{D}}{\bm{Y}}^{\top}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{k,m}^{\ast})^{1/2}\right]\right\|_{\text{F}}=\|\text{tr}({\bm{D}}){\bm{\Sigma}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{\text{F}}\leq\sqrt{d_{m}}\phi_{1}^{-1}|\text{tr}({\bm{D}})|,

where
{\bm{D}}=\left(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{\ast})^{1/2}\right)\left(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}-\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{\ast}\right)\left(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{\ast})^{1/2}\right)
, and the second equality is by Lemma
S10
and the last inequality follows from the fact that
\|\bm{B}\|_{\text{F}}\leq\sqrt{m}\|\bm{B}\|_{2}
for any matrix
\bm{B}\in\mathbb{R}^{m\times m}
and
\sigma_{\max}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{k,m}^{\ast})\leq\phi_{1}^{-1}
. Also, we have that

\Big\|\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{\ast}\Big\|_{F}\leq\sqrt{\frac{d}{d_{m}}}\Big\|\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{\ast}\Big\|_{2}\leq\phi_{1}^{1-M}\sqrt{\frac{d}{d_{m}}}.

By the fact that
\text{tr}(\bm{A}\bm{B})=\sum_{i,j}\bm{A}_{i,j}\bm{B}_{j,i}
, we have

\displaystyle|\text{tr}({\bm{D}})|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\sum_{i,j}\left(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{\ast}\right)_{i,j}\left(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}-\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{\ast}\right)_{j,i}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\Big\|\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{\ast}\Big\|_{\text{F}}\Big\|\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}-\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{\ast}\Big\|_{\text{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{d}{d_{m}}\sum_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\frac{\phi_{1}^{1-M}}{\sqrt{d_{m^{\prime}}}}\|\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{\ast}\|_{\text{F}},

where the last inequality follows the fact in Lemma
S12
. Now we can get
\|A_{111}\|_{\text{F}}\leq\frac{d}{\sqrt{d_{m}}}\sum\limits_{m^{\prime}\neq m}\frac{\phi_{1}^{-M}}{\sqrt{d_{m^{\prime}}}}\|\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{\ast}\|_{\text{F}}
. Since
\phi_{1}
is a constant and
\|\bar{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}-{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{\ast}\|_{\text{F}}/\sqrt{d_{m^{\prime}}}\leq\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})
for all
m^{\prime}
, we can get that
\|A_{111}\|_{\text{F}}/\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})=o(d)
. Similar as (
A144
), we can get that
\|A_{112}\|_{\text{F}}/\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})=o(d)
. Then it holds that
\|A_{11}\|_{\text{F}}/\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})\leq(\|A_{111}\|_{\text{F}}+\|A_{112}\|_{\text{F}})/\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})=o(d)
. Using similar arguments as in (
A58
) and (
A144
), we can also establish that
\|A_{12}\|_{\text{F}}/\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})=o(d)
. Correspondingly, we have
A_{1}/\textrm{D}({\bm{\Theta}},{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})=o(d)
.

E11
Proof of Lemma
S19

In this proof, we have two parts. The first part is the proof of (
A97
) and the second part is the proof of (
A98
).

Part I:
In this part, we consider the balanced case. This means
\pi=\frac{1}{2}
and our aim is to bound
\left|\Gamma_{u}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})\right|
in this balanced case. For any
{\bm{\beta}}_{m}
with
\|{\bm{\beta}}_{m}\|_{2}=1
, let
\bm{R}_{m}
be orthonormal matrices such that
\bm{R}_{m}{\bm{\beta}}_{m}=\|{\bm{\beta}}_{m}\|_{2}e_{1}(d_{m})=e_{1}(d_{m})
. Here
e_{1}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{m}}
is a vector with the first element as
1
and other elements as 0. By Lemma
S13
, we have that
\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{\top}\mathrm{vec}({\bm{\beta}}_{1}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{2}\circ{\bm{\beta}}_{3})=\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}
, where
\bm{\mathcal{V}}=\bm{\mathcal{X}}\times_{1}\bm{R}_{1}\times_{2}\bm{R}_{2}\times_{3}\bm{R}_{3}
. Putting this result into
\left|\Gamma_{u}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})\right|
, it can be rewritten as

\displaystyle\left|\Gamma_{u}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})\right|=

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{1}\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})^{\top}\mathrm{vec}(\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{1}\circ\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{2}\circ\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{3})\nabla\omega({\bm{\Theta}}_{u})\mathrm{d}u\right]\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{1}\frac{2(\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{V}})^{\top}\bm{a})\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}}{\sigma^{2}\{\exp(-g(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}))+\exp(g(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}))\}^{2}}\mathrm{d}u\right]\right|

where
\bm{a}=\mathrm{vec}\left((\bm{R}_{1}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{1})\circ(\bm{R}_{2}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{2})\circ(\bm{R}_{3}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{3})\right)
and
g(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1})=\omega_{u}\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}/\sigma^{2}
. Since
\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})\sim N({\bf 0},\sigma^{2}{\bf I}_{d})
with
d=d_{1}\cdot d_{2}\cdot d_{3}
, we have

\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{V}})=\mathrm{vec}\left(\bm{\mathcal{V}}\times_{1}\bm{R}_{1}\times_{2}\bm{R}_{2}\times_{3}\bm{R}_{3}\right)\sim N({\bf 0},\sigma^{2}{\bf I}_{d}).

Since
\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{1}\frac{2\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3}}\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}}{\sigma^{2}\{\exp(-g(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}))+\exp(g(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}))\}^{2}}\mathrm{d}u\right]=0
for
(j_{1},j_{2},j_{3})\neq(1,1,1)
,
|\Gamma_{u}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})|
can be further simplified as

\displaystyle|\Gamma_{u}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})|=\left|\int_{0}^{1}\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{2a_{1}\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}^{2}}{\sigma^{2}\{\exp(-g(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}))+\exp(g(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}))\}^{2}}\right]\mathrm{d}u\right|.

(A149)

where
a_{1}
is the first element of
\bm{a}
. Note that
\|\bm{R}_{m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{m}\|_{2}^{2}=\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{m}^{\top}\bm{R}_{m}\bm{R}_{m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{m}=1
, we know that
|(\bm{R}_{m}\bar{\bm{\beta}}_{m})_{1}|\leq 1
and
|a_{1}|\leq 1
.
Following the fact that
\exp(y)+\exp(-y)\geq 2+y^{2}
, we have that

|\Gamma_{u}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})|\leq\left|\int_{0}^{1}\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{2\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}^{2}}{\sigma^{2}\{2+\omega_{u}^{2}\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}^{2}/\sigma^{4}\}^{2}}\right]\mathrm{d}u\right|.

Letting
{\bf I}_{A}
denote the indicator random variable for event
A
, i.e., it takes value 1 when the event
A
occurs and 0 otherwise. Then we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{2\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}^{2}}{\sigma^{2}\{2+\omega_{u}^{2}\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}^{2}/\sigma^{4}\}^{2}}\right]

(A150)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{2\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}^{2}}{\sigma^{2}\{2+\omega_{u}^{2}\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}^{2}/\sigma^{4}\}^{2}}I_{\{|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}|/\sigma\leq 1\}}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{2\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}^{2}}{\sigma^{2}\{2+\omega_{u}^{2}\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}^{2}/\sigma^{4}\}^{2}}I_{\{|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}|/\sigma>1\}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\sigma^{2}}\mathbb{E}[\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}^{2}I_{\{|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}|/\sigma\leq 1\}}]+\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{2\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}^{2}}{\sigma^{2}\{2+\omega_{u}^{2}/\sigma^{2}\}^{2}}I_{\{|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}|/\sigma>1\}}\right].

The last inequality is true, because

\frac{\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}^{2}}{\sigma^{2}\{2+\omega_{u}V_{1,1}^{2}/\sigma^{4}\}^{2}}\leq\begin{cases}\frac{\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}^{2}}{4\sigma^{2}}&\text{if }|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}|/\sigma\leq 1\\
\frac{\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}^{2}}{\sigma^{2}\{2+\omega_{u}^{2}/\sigma^{2}\}^{2}}&\text{if }|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}|/\sigma>1\end{cases}

(A151)

Define
p_{1}:=\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}^{2}}{\sigma^{2}}I_{\{|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}|/\sigma\leq 1\}}\right]
. We can directly verify that
\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}^{2}}{\sigma^{2}}I_{\{|\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}|/\sigma>1\}}\right]=1-p_{1}
and consequently obtain that

\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{2\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}^{2}}{\sigma^{2}\{2+\omega_{u}^{2}\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}^{2}/\sigma^{4}\}^{2}}\right]\right|\leq|\bm{a}_{1}|\left(\frac{p_{1}}{2}+\frac{1-p_{1}}{2}\frac{1}{\{1+\omega_{u}^{2}/(2\sigma^{2})\}^{2}}\right).

(A152)

With the fact that
\omega_{u}=u\omega
, we get that

\displaystyle|\Gamma_{u}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})|

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{1}\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{2\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}^{2}}{\sigma^{2}\{2+\omega_{u}^{2}2\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}^{2}/\sigma^{4}\}^{2}}\right]\right|\mathrm{d}u

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{1}\left(\frac{p_{1}}{2}+\frac{1-p_{1}}{2}\frac{1}{\{1+\omega_{u}^{2}/(2\sigma^{2})\}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}u

\displaystyle=\frac{p_{1}}{2}+\frac{1-p_{1}}{2}\int_{0}^{1}\frac{1}{\{1+u^{2}\omega^{2}/(2\sigma^{2})\}^{2}}\mathrm{d}u

\displaystyle=\frac{p_{1}}{2}+\frac{1-p_{1}}{4}\left(\frac{1}{1+\omega^{2}/(2\sigma^{2})}+\frac{\text{tan}^{-1}(\omega/(\sqrt{2}\sigma))}{\omega/(\sqrt{2}\sigma)}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{p_{1}}{2}+\frac{1-p_{1}}{4}\left(\frac{1}{1+\omega^{2}/(2\sigma^{2})}+1\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1+p_{1}}{4}+\frac{(1-p_{1})/4}{1+\omega^{2}/(2\sigma^{2})}.

The last inequality is true, because
\text{tan}(y)\leq y
for all
y\geq 0
. Let
p=\frac{1+p_{1}}{2}
and
\gamma_{p}(\omega)=p+\frac{1-p}{1+\omega^{2}/(2\sigma^{2})}
, we have

\left|\Gamma_{u}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})\right|\leq\frac{\gamma_{p}(\omega)}{2}.

Part II:
In this part, we consider the unbalanced case. This means
\pi\neq\frac{1}{2}
and our aim is to bound
\left|\Gamma_{u}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})\right|
in this unbalanced case. Let
\pi=\frac{1-\rho}{2}
with
\rho\in(0,1)
, we have

\displaystyle\pi e^{-y}+(1-\pi)e^{y}\in[\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}},1],

\displaystyle\text{if }e^{y}\in\left[1,\frac{1+\rho}{1-\rho}\right];

(A153)

\displaystyle\pi e^{-y}+(1-\pi)e^{y}>1,

\displaystyle\text{otherwise.}

Define the event

\mathcal{E}_{{\bm{\Theta}}_{u}}:=\left\{e^{\omega_{u}\|{\bm{\beta}}_{1}\|_{2}\|{\bm{\beta}}_{2}\|_{2}\|{\bm{\beta}}_{3}\|_{2}\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}/\sigma^{2}}\in\left[1,\frac{1+\rho}{1-\rho}\right]\right\}.

By (
A96
), (
A110
) and (
A153
), we have

\displaystyle|\Gamma_{u}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})|

\displaystyle=\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{2\pi(1-\pi)\|{\bm{\beta}}_{1}\|_{2}\|{\bm{\beta}}_{2}\|_{2}\|{\bm{\beta}}_{3}\|_{2}a_{1}\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}^{2}}{\sigma^{2}\{\pi\exp(-g(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}))+(1-\pi)\exp(g(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}))\}^{2}}\right]\right|

(A154)

\displaystyle=\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{2\pi(1-\pi)\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}^{2}}{\sigma^{2}\{\pi\exp(-g(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}))+(1-\pi)\exp(g(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}))\}^{2}}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\pi(1-\pi)}{1-\rho^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}^{2}}{\sigma^{2}}{\bf I}_{\mathcal{E}_{{\bm{\beta}}_{u}}}\right]+2\pi(1-\pi)\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}^{2}}{\sigma^{2}}{\bf I}_{\mathcal{E}_{{\bm{\beta}}_{u}}^{c}}\right]

\displaystyle=2\pi(1-\pi)\cdot\frac{1-\rho^{2}+\rho^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}^{2}}{\sigma^{2}}{\bf I}_{\mathcal{E}_{{\bm{\beta}}_{u}}}\right]}{1-\rho^{2}}.

where
\mathcal{E}_{{\bm{\Theta}}_{u}}^{c}
is the complement of
\mathcal{E}_{{\bm{\Theta}}_{u}}
. The first equality is the direct result of
\|{\bm{\beta}}_{m}\|_{2}=1
and
|a_{1}|\leq 1
. The second inequality follows the fact in (
A153
). The last equality use that
\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}^{2}}{\sigma^{2}}{\bf I}_{\mathcal{E}_{{\bm{\Theta}}_{u}}}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}^{2}}{\sigma^{2}}{\bf I}_{\mathcal{E}_{{\bm{\Theta}}_{u}}^{c}}\right]=1
. Note that whenever
\omega_{u}\neq 0
, we have that
\mathcal{E}_{{\bm{\Theta}}_{u}}\subset\{\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}/\sigma\geq 0\}
and then we obtain that

\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}^{2}}{\sigma^{2}}{\bf I}_{\mathcal{E}_{{\bm{\Theta}}_{u}}}\right]\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}^{2}}{\sigma^{2}}{\bf I}_{\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{1,1,1}/\sigma\geq 0}\right]=\frac{1}{2}.

(A155)

Putting (
A154
) and (
A155
) together, we conclude that

|\Gamma_{u}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})|\leq 2\pi(1-\pi)\frac{1-\rho^{2}/2}{1-\rho^{2}}.

E12
Proof of Lemma
S20

We first show that
\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}=c{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\ast}
. Define
\bm{\Psi}=(\pi_{1},\ldots,\pi_{k},\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{1},\ldots,\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{K},\underline{{\bm{\Sigma}}}_{1},\ldots,\underline{{\bm{\Sigma}}}_{K})
. This is a definition without considering the low rank structure for
\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}
.
With a slight abuse of notation, we may write
Q({\bm{\Theta}}|{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})
as
Q^{\prime}(\bm{\Psi}|\bm{\Psi}^{\ast})
. The first derivation of
Q^{\prime}(\bm{\Psi}|\bm{\Psi}^{\ast})
about
\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}
is

\nabla_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}}Q^{\prime}(\bm{\Psi}|\bm{\Psi}^{\ast})=\mathbb{E}\left[\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})\{\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i})-\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k})^{\top}\}\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m}{{\bm{\Omega}}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{\ast}\right].

Since
\nabla_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}}Q^{\prime}(\bm{\Psi}^{\ast}|\bm{\Psi}^{\ast})=\bm{0}
, we can get that

2\mathbb{E}\left[\tau_{ik}(\bm{\Theta}^{\ast})\left\{\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i})-\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}^{\ast})\right\}\prod_{m}^{\otimes}{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\right]=\bm{0}.

(A156)

Since
{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}^{\ast}
’s are positive definite, this implies that
\mathbb{E}\left\{\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\right\}=\mathbb{E}\left\{\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})\right\}\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}^{\ast}
.

Plugging this into
\nabla_{{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}Q(\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast},{\bm{\Theta}}_{-{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}}^{\prime}|{\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})=\bm{0}
, we have

\displaystyle\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\mathbb{E}\{\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})\}\left\{\omega_{k}^{\prime}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{\prime})^{\top}\left(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{\prime}\right)\omega_{k}^{\prime}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{\prime})\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\{\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i})_{(m)}\}\left\{\left(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{\prime}\right)\omega_{k}^{\prime}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{\prime})\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\{\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})\}(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}^{\ast})_{(m)}\left\{\left(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{\prime}\right)\omega_{k}^{\prime}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{\prime})\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\displaystyle\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\mathbb{E}\{\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}^{\ast})\}\left\{\omega_{k}^{\ast}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{\ast})\left(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{\prime}\right)\omega_{k}^{\prime}\mathrm{vec}(\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{\prime})\right\}.

The last equality uses that
\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}^{\ast}=\omega_{k}^{\ast}\prod\limits^{\circ}_{m}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\ast}
. It can be seen from the above equality that
\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}=c{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\ast}
. Combined with
\|{\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{2}=1
, we can get that
\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}/\|\tilde{{\bm{\beta}}}_{k,m}^{\ast}\|_{2}={\bm{\beta}}_{k,m}^{\ast}
.

E13
Proof of Proposition 2

Updating
{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}
in the M-step leads to solving the following problem

\arg\min_{\beta_{k,r,m}}\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}})\left\|(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k})\times\underline{{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}}^{-1/2}\right\|_{F}^{2}+\lambda_{1}\|{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}\|_{1}.

(A157)

Following
Kolda and Bader 2009
, we define
{\bf V}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i},m}=\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\right)_{(m)}\left(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{1/2}\right)^{\top}
and
{\bf V}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k},m}=\left(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k}\right)_{(m)}\left(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{1/2}\right)^{\top}
. Next, we have that

\displaystyle\left\|(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}-\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k})\times\underline{{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}}^{-1/2}\right\|_{F}^{2}

(A158)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\text{tr}\left\{\left({\bf V}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i},m}^{\top}-{\bf V}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k},m}^{\top}\right){\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}({\bf V}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i},m}-{\bf V}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k},m})\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underbrace{\text{tr}({\bf V}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i},m}^{\top}{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}{\bf V}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i},m})}_{I_{1}}-2\underbrace{\text{tr}({\bf V}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i},m}^{\top}{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}{\bf V}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k},m})}_{I_{2}}+\underbrace{\text{tr}({\bf V}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k},m}^{\top}{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}{\bf V}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k},m})}_{I_{3}}.

In what follows, we will take derivatives of
I_{1}
,
I_{2}
and
I_{3}
with respect to
{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}
, respectively. First, it is easy to see that the derivative of
I_{1}
with respect to
{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}
is zero.
Next, the term
I_{2}
can be calculated as follows

\displaystyle\text{tr}({\bf V}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i},m}^{\top}{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}{\bf V}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k},m})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{R}\omega_{k,r^{\prime}}\text{tr}\left\{{\bf V}_{\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i},m}^{\top}{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r^{\prime},m}\mathrm{vec}(\prod_{{\bm{m}}^{\prime}\neq m}^{\circ}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m^{\prime}})^{\top}\left(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}^{1/2}\right)^{\top}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{R}\omega_{k,r^{\prime}}\mathrm{vec}(\prod_{{\bm{m}}^{\prime}\neq m}^{\circ}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m^{\prime}})^{\top}\left(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\right)^{\top}\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\right)_{(m)}^{\top}{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r^{\prime},m},

where the first equality is due to Lemma
S9
and the second equality is due to the fact that
\text{tr}({\bf A}\bm{B})=\text{tr}(\bm{B}{\bf A})
. Correspondingly, the first derivative of
I_{2}
with respect to
{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}
is

{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}\left(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\right)_{(m)}\left(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\right)\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{R}\xi_{k,m,r^{\prime}r}\omega_{k,r^{\prime}}{\mathrm{vec}(\prod_{{\bm{m}}^{\prime}\neq m}^{\circ}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m^{\prime}})}

(A159)

Similarly,
I_{3}
can be calculated as

\displaystyle\text{tr}({\bf V}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k},m}^{\top}{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}{\bf V}_{\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{k},m})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\text{tr}\left\{\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}(\sum_{r_{1}=1}^{R}\omega_{k,r_{1}}\mathrm{vec}(\prod_{{\bm{m}}^{\prime}\neq m}^{\circ}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r_{1},m^{\prime}}){\bm{\beta}}_{k,r_{1},m}^{\top}){\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}(\sum_{r_{2}=1}^{R}\omega_{k,r_{2}}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r_{2},m}\mathrm{vec}(\prod_{{\bm{m}}^{\prime}\neq m}^{\circ}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r_{2},m^{\prime}})^{\top})\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{r_{1},r_{2}}\omega_{k,r_{1}}\omega_{k,r_{2}}\left\{\mathrm{vec}(\prod_{{\bm{m}}^{\prime}\neq m}^{\circ}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r_{1},m^{\prime}})^{\top}\left(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\right)\mathrm{vec}(\prod_{{\bm{m}}^{\prime}\neq m}^{\circ}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r_{2},m^{\prime}})\right\}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r_{1},m}^{\top}{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r_{2},m}.

Correspondingly, the first derivative of
I_{3}
with respect to
{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}
can be written as

2\,{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m}(\sum_{r_{1}=1}^{R}\omega_{k,r_{1}}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r_{1},m}\mathrm{vec}(\prod_{{\bm{m}}^{\prime}\neq m}^{\circ}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r_{1},m^{\prime}})^{\top})\left(\prod\limits^{\otimes}_{m^{\prime}\neq m}{\bm{\Omega}}_{k,m^{\prime}}\right)(\sum_{r_{2}=1}^{R}\xi_{k,rr_{2},m}\omega_{k,r_{2}}{\mathrm{vec}(\prod_{{\bm{m}}^{\prime}\neq m}^{\circ}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r_{2},m^{\prime}})}).

(A160)

Combining (
A159
) and (
A160
) and given
\tau_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}^{(t)})
and
{\bm{\Theta}}^{(t)}
, the subgradient of the objective function in (
A157
) with respect to
{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}
is

-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\tau^{(t+1)}_{ik}({\bm{\Theta}}^{(t)})\tilde{{\bm{g}}}^{(t+1)}_{k,r,m}+\frac{n_{k}^{(t)}}{n}C^{(t+1)}_{k,r,m}{\bm{\Omega}}^{(t)}_{k,m}{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}+\lambda_{0}^{(t+1)}\text{sign}({\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}),

(A161)

where
\tilde{{\bm{g}}}^{(t+1)}_{k,r,m}
,
C^{(t+1)}_{k,r,m}
are as defined in (
10
). Hence, given
{\bm{\Theta}}^{(t)}
, the updating formula for
{\bm{\beta}}_{k,r,m}
is given as in (
10
).

F
Additional Real Data Analysis

The fMRI data have been preprocessed and are summarized as a
116\times 236
spatial-temporal matrix for each subject. In the matrix, the 116 rows correspond to 116 regions from the Anatomical Automatic Labeling (AAL) atlas
(
Tzourio-Mazoyer et al. 2002
)
and the 236 columns correspond to the fMRI measures taken at 236 time points. For each subject, the tensor object is constructed by stacking a sequence of Fisher-transformed correlation matrices of dimension
116\times 116
over
N
sliding windows, each summarizing the connectivity between 116 brain regions in a given window. We vary the number of sliding windows
N
among
\{1,15,30\}
. When
N=1
, each subject only has one correlation matrix calculated based on the entire spatial-temporal matrix. For
N=15
and
30
, we let the length of the window be 20, as suggested in
Sun and Li 2019
, to balance the number of samples in each window and the overlap between adjacent windows.

Figure 4:
Brain connectivity in ASD (columns 1-2) and normal control (NC; columns 3-4) groups at three representative windows ordered by time when
N=30
. Columns 1, 3 and Columns 2, 4 show the connectivity in the left and right hemispheres, respectively. Edge widths in the plot are proportional to edge values.

We further explore the difference of connectivity between ASD and normal control groups. Figure
4
shows the estimated brain connectivity for ASD subjects and normal controls at three representative windows (i.e., 3, 14, 27) when
N=30
. For each brain network, we report the identified edges with absolute values greater than 0.3.
It is seen that the ASD subjects and the normal controls show notable differences in their brain connectivity.
The brain networks from the ASD group are less connected and exhibit less changes across different windows, which agree with the existing finding that ASD subjects are usually found less active in brain connectivity
(
Rudie et al. 2013
)
. It is seen that the occipital lobe is a relatively active area for both ASD subjects and normal controls; this agrees with existing findings that the occipital is important in posture and vision perception, and it tends to be more active during fMRI data collections
(
Ouchi et al. 1999
)
.
Notably, the frontal area of the normal controls is active, both within itself and in its connection to other areas, and this activity first decreases and then increases for normal controls.
Such a change in activity in the frontal area is not observed for the ASD subjects, as the frontal area appears inactive in all three windows.
This agrees with existing findings that the frontal area, which may underlie impaired social and communication behaviors, shows reduced connectivity in ASD subjects
(
Monk et al. 2009
)
. Moreover, we observe that the connectivity for normal controls in both hemispheres first increases and then decreases, by comparing the number of edges in the three windows, while the connectivity for ASD subjects remains relatively unchanged over time.
These findings suggest some interesting resting-state connectivity patterns that warrant more in-depth investigation and validation.

G
Additional Numerical Results

F1
Toy example on tensor algebra

In this subsection, we give one simple example to illustrate some tensor notations and algebra. Let
\bm{\mathcal{X}}\in\mathbb{R}^{3\times 4\times 2}
, the first and second mode-
(1,2)
slices of
\bm{\mathcal{X}}
are

\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{::1}=\begin{pmatrix}1&4&7&10\\
2&5&8&11\\
3&6&9&12\end{pmatrix}\text{ and }\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{::2}\begin{pmatrix}13&16&19&22\\
14&17&20&23\\
15&18&21&24\end{pmatrix}.

Each column in
\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{::1}
and
\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{::2}
is a mode-1 fibers of
\bm{\mathcal{X}}
. The mode-
1
, mode-
2
and mode-
3
unfoldings of
\bm{\mathcal{X}}
are, respectively,

\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{(1)}=\begin{pmatrix}1&4&7&10&13&16&19&22\\
2&5&8&11&14&17&20&23\\
3&6&9&12&15&18&21&24\end{pmatrix},

\displaystyle\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{(2)}=\begin{pmatrix}1&2&3&13&14&15\\
4&5&6&16&17&18\\
7&8&9&19&20&21\\
10&11&12&22&23&24\end{pmatrix},

\displaystyle\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{(3)}=\begin{pmatrix}1&2&3&\cdots&10&11&12\\
13&14&15&\cdots&22&23&24\end{pmatrix}.

Finally, the vectorization of
\bm{\mathcal{X}}
is

\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})=(1,2,3,4,5,6,\cdots,22,23,24)^{\top}.

F2
Time complexity evaluation

In this section, we evaluate the computational complexity of our algorithm. Specifically, under the same setting as in Table 2 with
\mu=0.85
and
\nu=0.3
with tensor dimensions
10\times 10\times 10
, we evaluate the computing time as the sample size varies from 100 to 500. The simulations were run on an Intel Xeon Gold 6240 @ 2.60GHz with one node and 16GB memory. The results from 50 data replicates are shown in Figure
5
below. It is seen that the relationship between the computation time and sample size is approximately linear. Moreover, the variance in computation time decreases with a larger sample size, as a larger sample size provides a more stable initialization, leading to reduced variability in the computation time. Next, we let
d_{3}
, the dimension in the last mode, increases from 10 to 50, fixing the sample size at 400. The results from 50 data replicates are shown in Figure
5
below. It is seen that the computation time increases almost linearly with
d_{3}
.
The main computing cost in running our current algorithm is in estimating the sparse separable precision matrices using the GLasso algorithm
(
Friedman et al. 2008
)
. When the sparse precision matrix estimation isn’t necessary, the computational time can be significantly reduced.

Figure 5:
Boxplots of computing time with a varying sample size
n
and tensor dimension
d_{3}
. The red solid line connects the median values from the settings in each plot.

F3
Sensitivity to rank specification

In this subsection, we assess the impact of different ranks on the estimation outcomes. In the simulation setting in (
32
), we consider the parameters
\mu=0.85
and
\nu=0.3
. The true rank in this setting is 4. In the estimation, we specified the rank from 1 to 10. Figure
6
illustrates the cluster mean error (CME) and clustering error (CE) corresponding to different rank specifications.

Figure 6:
The cluster mean error (CME) and clustering error (CE) in varying ranks.

From the results in Figure
6
, several useful observations can be made. When the rank is under-specified, a clear bias is observed in the CME, indicating the difficulty of accurately estimating the means. The minimal CME is achieved when the true rank is specified. On the other hand, when the rank is over-specified, the error slightly increases due to the introduction of noise from additional ranks. Regarding the CE, specifying the rank as 1 or 2 leads to poor performance, while CE decreases as the specified rank approaches the true rank. However, when the rank is over-specified, the CE shows a slight increasing trend, again due to the introduction of noise from the additional ranks.

In our empirical study, we propose using the extended Bayesian information criterion (eBIC)
(
Chen and Chen 2008
)
for rank selection. The eBIC incorporates the likelihood of the tensor mixture model, the number of parameters, and a penalty term related to the rank. In this simulation setting, the eBIC criterion successfully selected the rank across 50 replications, providing evidence for its effectiveness in rank selection.
